<script>









// Helper Global RPC untuk Vercel / Supabase API
async function rpc(methodName, params = []) {
  try {
    const response = await fetch('/api/rpc', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ method: methodName, params: Array.isArray(params) ? params : [params] })
    });
    const text = await response.text();
    let data;
    try { data = JSON.parse(text); } catch(e) {
      throw new Error('Respon server tidak valid (' + response.status + ')');
    }
    if (!response.ok || data.success === false) {
      throw new Error((data && (data.message || data.error)) || 'Terjadi kesalahan pada server (' + response.status + ')');
    }
    return data;
  } catch(err) {
    console.error('[rpc] Fetch error:', err);
    throw err;
  }
}
window.rpc = rpc;

/**
 * =================================================================
 * JAVASCRIPTCLIENT.HTML
 * SELURUH logika di sini murni UI: state tampilan, validasi form
 * ringan, dan pengiriman/penerimaan data via google.script.run.
 * TIDAK ADA logika bisnis (autentikasi, kalkulasi AK, tag parsing)
 * di sisi client — semuanya di backend .gs. Ada DUA sumber daftar
 * pilihan (dropdown), sengaja dipisah:
 *   - FORM_OPTIONS       -> dari getEligibilityFormOptions() / Config.gs
 *                           (mesin lama, dipakai tab "Check Eligible")
 *   - DOCGEN_FORM_OPTIONS -> dari getDocGenFormOptions() / DocGen.gs
 *                           (khusus saat generate dokumen Opsi A & B,
 *                           mis. Predikat SKP versi docx hanya 2 kategori)
 * =================================================================
 */

// Helper function to read input values safely
const val = id => {
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  return el ? el.value.trim() : '';
};

// Helper function to format date into Indonesian format
function formatTanggalIndonesia(value) {
  if (!value) return '-';
  const d = new Date(value);
  if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(value);
  const months = ['Januari','Februari','Maret','April','Mei','Juni','Juli','Agustus','September','Oktober','November','Desember'];
  return `${d.getDate()} ${months[d.getMonth()]} ${d.getFullYear()}`;
}

let SESSION = { token: null, user: null };
let FORM_OPTIONS = { golongan: [], jabatan: [], predikat: [], bulan: [], ijazahBaru: [] };
let DOCGEN_FORM_OPTIONS = { predikat: [], ijazahBaru: [] };
let ceSelectedEmployee = null; // { nip, nama } — hasil pilihan pada tab Check Eligible
let ceOriginalGolongan = ''; // baseline golongan dari data utama, dipakai deteksi "berubah" utk TMT Golongan (baru)
let ceOriginalJabatan = '';  // baseline jabatan dari data utama, dipakai deteksi "berubah" utk TMT Jabatan (baru)

/* ------------------------- INIT ------------------------- */
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  initTheme();
  bindLoginForm();
  bindRegisterForm();
  bindAuthSwitch();
  bindThemeToggle();
  bindSidebarNav();
  bindTabs();
  bindTemplateManagerForm();
  restoreSessionIfAny();

  const defaultFormOptions = {
    golongan: ['I/a','I/b','I/c','I/d','II/a','II/b','II/c','II/d','III/a','III/b','III/c','III/d','IV/a','IV/b','IV/c','IV/d','IV/e','Set. I/a','Set. I/b','Set. I/c','Set. I/d','Set. II/a','Set. II/b','Set. II/c','Set. II/d','Set. III/a','Set. III/b','Set. III/c','Set. III/d','Set. IV/a','Set. IV/b','Set. IV/c','Set. IV/d','Set. IV/e'],
    jabatan: ['Asisten Ahli','Lektor','Lektor Kepala','Guru Besar'],
    predikat: ['Sangat Baik','Baik','Butuh Perbaikan','Kurang','Sangat Kurang'],
    bulan: [{value:1,label:'Januari'},{value:2,label:'Februari'},{value:3,label:'Maret'},{value:4,label:'April'},{value:5,label:'Mei'},{value:6,label:'Juni'},{value:7,label:'Juli'},{value:8,label:'Agustus'},{value:9,label:'September'},{value:10,label:'Oktober'},{value:11,label:'November'},{value:12,label:'Desember'}],
    ijazahBaru: ['Ada','Tidak']
  };

  const defaultDocGenOptions = {
    predikat: ['Sangat Baik','Baik'],
    ijazahBaru: ['Ada','Tidak']
  };

  FORM_OPTIONS = defaultFormOptions;
  DOCGEN_FORM_OPTIONS = defaultDocGenOptions;

  bindKenaikanPangkatForms();
  bindCheckEligibleForm();
  bindPensiunForm();
  bindSkPensiunNonAsnForm();
  bindKontrakForm();

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(opts => {
      if (opts && typeof opts === 'object' && opts.golongan) {
        FORM_OPTIONS = opts;
        bindCheckEligibleForm();
      }
    })
    .getEligibilityFormOptions();

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(docGenOpts => {
      if (docGenOpts && typeof docGenOpts === 'object' && docGenOpts.predikat) {
        DOCGEN_FORM_OPTIONS = docGenOpts;
        bindKenaikanPangkatForms();
      }
    })
    .getDocGenFormOptions();
});

/* ------------------------- THEME ------------------------- */
function initTheme() {
  const saved = localStorage.getItem('sikp_theme') || 'light';
  document.body.classList.remove('theme-light', 'theme-dark');
  document.body.classList.add('theme-' + saved);
  updateThemeIcon(saved);
}
function bindThemeToggle() {
  document.getElementById('themeToggleBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const isDark = document.body.classList.contains('theme-dark');
    const next = isDark ? 'light' : 'dark';
    document.body.classList.remove('theme-light', 'theme-dark');
    document.body.classList.add('theme-' + next);
    localStorage.setItem('sikp_theme', next);
    updateThemeIcon(next);
  });
}
function updateThemeIcon(mode) {
  const btn = document.getElementById('themeToggleBtn');
  btn.textContent = mode === 'dark' ? '🌙' : '☀️';
  document.getElementById('themeLabel').textContent = mode === 'dark' ? 'Dark' : 'Light';
}

/* ------------------------- SESSION / LOGIN ------------------------- */
function restoreSessionIfAny() {
  const token = sessionStorage.getItem('sikp_token');
  if (!token) return;
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (res.valid) {
        SESSION.token = token;
        SESSION.user = res.user;
        showApp();
      } else {
        sessionStorage.removeItem('sikp_token');
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(() => sessionStorage.removeItem('sikp_token'))
    .validateSession(token);
}

function bindLoginForm() {
  const form = document.getElementById('loginForm');
  form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const nip = document.getElementById('loginNip').value.trim();
    const password = document.getElementById('loginPassword').value.trim();
    const errBox = document.getElementById('loginError');
    const btn = document.getElementById('loginSubmitBtn');

    errBox.style.display = 'none';
    setBtnLoading(btn, true, 'Masuk...');

    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(res => {
        setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Masuk');
        if (res.success) {
          SESSION.token = res.token;
          SESSION.user = res.user;
          sessionStorage.setItem('sikp_token', res.token);
          showApp();
        } else {
          errBox.textContent = res.message;
          errBox.style.display = 'block';
        }
      })
      .withFailureHandler(err => {
        setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Masuk');
        errBox.textContent = 'Terjadi kesalahan: ' + (err.message || err);
        errBox.style.display = 'block';
      })
      .login(nip, password);
  });
}

/** Toggle antara panel "Masuk" dan "Daftar Akun" pada layar login. */
function bindAuthSwitch() {
  const buttons = document.querySelectorAll('[data-auth-target]');
  const subtitle = document.getElementById('authSubtitle');
  buttons.forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      buttons.forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
      btn.classList.add('active');

      document.querySelectorAll('[data-auth-group-pane="auth"]').forEach(pane => {
        pane.classList.toggle('active', pane.id === btn.dataset.authTarget);
      });

      document.getElementById('loginError').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('registerError').style.display = 'none';

      subtitle.textContent = btn.dataset.authTarget === 'pane-register'
        ? 'Daftar menggunakan Nama, NIP, dan Unit Kerja Anda'
        : 'Silakan masuk menggunakan NIP dan password Anda';
    });
  });
}

function bindRegisterForm() {
  const form = document.getElementById('registerForm');
  form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const nama = document.getElementById('regNama').value.trim();
    const nip = document.getElementById('regNip').value.trim();
    const unitKerja = document.getElementById('regUnitKerja').value.trim();
    const errBox = document.getElementById('registerError');
    const btn = document.getElementById('registerSubmitBtn');

    errBox.style.display = 'none';
    setBtnLoading(btn, true, 'Mendaftar...');

    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(res => {
        setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Daftar & Masuk');
        if (res.success) {
          SESSION.token = res.token;
          SESSION.user = res.user;
          sessionStorage.setItem('sikp_token', res.token);
          showToast('success', res.message);
          showApp();
        } else {
          errBox.textContent = res.message;
          errBox.style.display = 'block';
        }
      })
      .withFailureHandler(err => {
        setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Daftar & Masuk');
        errBox.textContent = 'Terjadi kesalahan: ' + (err.message || err);
        errBox.style.display = 'block';
      })
      .register(nama, nip, unitKerja);
  });
}

function showApp() {
  document.getElementById('loginScreen').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('appShell').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('sidebarUserName').textContent = SESSION.user.nama || SESSION.user.nip;
  const roleLabel = SESSION.user.role ? (SESSION.user.role.toUpperCase().replace(/_/g, ' ')) : '';
  const unitLabel = SESSION.user.unitEsIi ? ` · ${SESSION.user.unitEsIi}` : '';
  document.getElementById('sidebarUserRole').textContent = (SESSION.user.jabatan || SESSION.user.status_kepegawaian || roleLabel) + unitLabel;
  terapkanBatasanPeran_();
  if (SESSION.user.role !== 'normal') {
    loadTemplateList();
    loadServiceTemplates('Kenaikan Pangkat', 'AK Konversi Tahunan', 'optA_templateSelect');
    loadServiceTemplates('Kenaikan Pangkat', 'AK Konversi Kumulatif', 'optB_templateSelect');
    loadServiceTemplates('Pensiun', 'DPCP', 'pensiun_dpcpTemplateSelect');
    loadServiceTemplates('Pensiun', 'SUPER', 'pensiun_superTemplateSelect');
    loadServiceTemplates('Pensiun', 'Buat SK Pensiun Pegawai Undip Non ASN', 'skNonAsn_templateSelect');
    loadPromosiDashboard();
  }
}

/**
 * Terapkan batasan tampilan sesuai peran akun (SESSION.user.role):
 * 'normal'      - HANYA "Profil Saya", semua menu lain disembunyikan.
 * 'user'        - Kenaikan Pangkat (tanpa tab Opsi A/B) & Pensiun, TANPA
 *                 menu Template atau Perubahan Status Akun.
 * 'admin'       - akses penuh Kenaikan Pangkat/Pensiun/Template.
 * 'super_admin' - sama seperti admin + Perubahan Status Akun.
 *
 * PENTING: ini pembatasan TAMPILAN saja (UX) -- pembatasan yang
 * sesungguhnya (keamanan) ada di server lewat requireRole_() pada setiap
 * fungsi backend yang sensitif (generateDocument, addTemplate, dst.),
 * supaya tidak bisa dilewati lewat DevTools browser.
 */
function terapkanBatasanPeran_() {
  const role = SESSION.user.role || 'normal';

  const navProfil = document.querySelector('.nav-item[data-view="profil-saya"]');
  const navKP = document.querySelector('.nav-item[data-view="kenaikan-pangkat"]');
  const navPensiun = document.querySelector('.nav-item[data-view="pensiun"]');
  const navKontrak = document.querySelector('.nav-item[data-view="kontrak"]');
  const navPmk = document.querySelector('.nav-item[data-view="pmk"]');
  const navBuatSk = document.querySelector('.nav-item[data-view="buat-sk"]');
  const navTemplate = document.querySelector('.nav-item[data-view="template"]');
  const navPeranAkun = document.querySelector('.nav-item[data-view="peran-akun"]');
  const navLainnya = document.querySelector('.nav-item[data-view="lainnya"]');

  // Sembunyikan semua dulu (deny-by-default), baru tampilkan sesuai peran.
  [navProfil, navKP, navPensiun, navKontrak, navPmk, navBuatSk, navTemplate, navPeranAkun, navLainnya].forEach(el => { if (el) el.style.display = 'none'; });

  let viewAktif = 'kenaikan-pangkat';
  let judulAktif = 'Kenaikan Pangkat';

  if (role === 'normal') {
    if (navProfil) navProfil.style.display = 'flex';
    viewAktif = 'profil-saya';
    judulAktif = 'Profil Saya';
    
    rpc('getMenuAksesKontrak', [SESSION.token])
      .then(res => {
        const canAccess = res && res.success && (
          (res.eligible && res.diizinkan) || 
          res.isAtasanLangsung || 
          res.isAdmin
        );
        if (navKontrak) {
          navKontrak.style.display = canAccess ? 'flex' : 'none';
        }
      })
      .catch(err => {
        console.warn('getMenuAksesKontrak error:', err);
      });
  } else {
    if (navKP) navKP.style.display = 'flex';
    if (navPensiun) navPensiun.style.display = 'flex';
    if (navKontrak) navKontrak.style.display = 'flex';
    if (['user', 'admin', 'super_admin'].includes(role) && navPmk) navPmk.style.display = 'flex';
    if (['user', 'admin', 'super_admin'].includes(role) && navBuatSk) navBuatSk.style.display = 'flex';
    if (navLainnya) navLainnya.style.display = 'flex';
    if (role === 'admin' || role === 'super_admin') {
      if (navTemplate) navTemplate.style.display = 'flex';
    }
    if (role === 'super_admin' && navPeranAkun) {
      navPeranAkun.style.display = 'flex';
    }
  }

  // Peran 'user' tidak boleh melihat tab Opsi A/B (hanya Check Eligible).
  const tabOptA = document.querySelector('.tab-btn[data-tab-target="tab-optA"]');
  const tabOptB = document.querySelector('.tab-btn[data-tab-target="tab-optB"]');
  const tampilkanOpsiAB = (role === 'admin' || role === 'super_admin');
  if (tabOptA) tabOptA.style.display = tampilkanOpsiAB ? '' : 'none';
  if (tabOptB) tabOptB.style.display = tampilkanOpsiAB ? '' : 'none';

  // Tab "Status Usulan Saya" (user) & "Usulan Masuk" (admin/super_admin).
  const tabStatusUsulan = document.getElementById('tabBtnStatusUsulan');
  const tabUsulanMasuk = document.getElementById('tabBtnUsulanMasuk');
  const tabBtnSiapSk = document.getElementById('tabBtnSiapSk');
  const tabBtnApprovalSk = document.getElementById('tabBtnApprovalSk');
  const tabBtnUploadSk = document.getElementById('tabBtnUploadSk');

  if (tabStatusUsulan) tabStatusUsulan.style.display = (role === 'user') ? '' : 'none';
  if (tabUsulanMasuk) tabUsulanMasuk.style.display = (role === 'admin' || role === 'super_admin') ? '' : 'none';
  if (tabBtnSiapSk) tabBtnSiapSk.style.display = (role === 'admin' || role === 'super_admin') ? '' : 'none';
  if (tabBtnUploadSk) tabBtnUploadSk.style.display = (role === 'admin' || role === 'super_admin') ? '' : 'none';
  if (tabBtnApprovalSk) {
    if ((role === 'admin' || role === 'super_admin') && SESSION.user && SESSION.user.sub_role) {
      tabBtnApprovalSk.style.display = '';
      loadApprovalSkBadge();
    } else {
      tabBtnApprovalSk.style.display = 'none';
    }
  }

  if (role === 'admin' || role === 'super_admin') loadUsulanMasukBadge();

  // Sama untuk modul Pensiun.
  const tabStatusUsulanPensiun = document.getElementById('tabBtnStatusUsulanPensiun');
  const tabUsulanMasukPensiun = document.getElementById('tabBtnUsulanMasukPensiun');
  const tabBtnSkPensiunNonAsn = document.getElementById('tabBtnSkPensiunNonAsn');
  const tabBtnUploadSkPensiun = document.getElementById('tabBtnUploadSkPensiun');
  if (tabStatusUsulanPensiun) tabStatusUsulanPensiun.style.display = (role === 'user') ? '' : 'none';
  if (tabUsulanMasukPensiun) tabUsulanMasukPensiun.style.display = (role === 'admin' || role === 'super_admin') ? '' : 'none';
  if (tabBtnSkPensiunNonAsn) tabBtnSkPensiunNonAsn.style.display = (role === 'admin' || role === 'super_admin') ? '' : 'none';
  if (tabBtnUploadSkPensiun) tabBtnUploadSkPensiun.style.display = (role === 'admin' || role === 'super_admin') ? '' : 'none';
  if (role === 'admin' || role === 'super_admin') loadUsulanMasukPensiunBadge();

  // Tombol akses manual di dashboard Pensiun berubah makna sesuai peran:
  // user -> buka modal Ajukan Usulan; admin/super_admin -> form DPCP penuh.
  const pensiunGoToFormBtn = document.getElementById('pensiunGoToFormBtn');
  if (pensiunGoToFormBtn) {
    pensiunGoToFormBtn.textContent = (role === 'user')
      ? 'Cari Pegawai Manual / Ajukan Usulan'
      : 'Cari Pegawai Manual / Isi Form DPCP';
  }

  // Tampilkan/sembunyikan tombol Kelola Akses & Admin Tabs sesuai peran
  const kontrakKelolaAksesBtn = document.getElementById('kontrakKelolaAksesBtn');
  if (kontrakKelolaAksesBtn) {
    kontrakKelolaAksesBtn.style.display = (role === 'admin' || role === 'super_admin') ? 'inline-flex' : 'none';
  }
  const kontrakAdminTabs = document.getElementById('kontrakAdminTabs');
  if (kontrakAdminTabs) {
    kontrakAdminTabs.style.display = 'flex';
  }

  // Arahkan ke view default sesuai peran.
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item[data-view]').forEach(i => i.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.view-panel').forEach(p => p.style.display = 'none');
  const navAktif = document.querySelector(`.nav-item[data-view="${viewAktif}"]`);
  if (navAktif) navAktif.classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('view-' + viewAktif).style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('topbarTitle').textContent = judulAktif;

  if (viewAktif === 'profil-saya') loadProfilSaya();
  if (viewAktif === 'buat-sk') onEnterBuatSkView();
}

/** Muat & tampilkan data diri terbatas (peran 'normal'). */
function loadProfilSaya() {
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (!res.success) { showToast('error', res.message); return; }
      renderProfilSayaView(res.profil || {});
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat profil: ' + (err.message || err)))
    .getProfilSaya(SESSION.token);
}

function renderProfilSayaView(profil) {
  const labelMap = {
    nama_lengkap: 'Nama Lengkap',
    nip: 'NIP / NIK',
    unit_kerja: 'Unit Kerja',
    jabatan: 'Jabatan Terakhir',
    golongan: 'Pangkat / Golongan',
    tmt_pensiun_bup: 'TMT BUP (Pensiun)',
    tgl_lhr: 'Tanggal Lahir',
    status_kepegawaian: 'Status Kepegawaian',
    status_bekerja: 'Status Bekerja'
  };

  const p = profil || {};
  const nama = p.nama_lengkap || SESSION.nama || (SESSION.user && SESSION.user.nama_lengkap) || '-';
  const nip = p.nip || SESSION.nip || (SESSION.user && SESSION.user.nip) || '-';
  const unit = p.unit_kerja || SESSION.unit_kerja || (SESSION.user && SESSION.user.unit_kerja) || '-';
  const jabatan = p.jabatan || SESSION.jabatan || '-';
  const golongan = p.golongan || SESSION.golongan || '-';
  const pensiun = p.tmt_pensiun_bup || p.tgl_lhr || '-';
  const statPeg = p.status_kepegawaian || 'Aktif';
  const statBek = p.status_bekerja || 'Aktif';

  const sideNama = document.getElementById('prof_side_nama');
  if (sideNama) sideNama.textContent = nama;
  const sideNip = document.getElementById('prof_side_nip');
  if (sideNip) sideNip.textContent = 'NIP: ' + nip;

  const container = document.getElementById('profilSayaContent');
  if (!container) return;

  container.innerHTML = `
    <div class="mecha-border" style="padding:28px; background:var(--surface);">
      <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:flex-start; margin-bottom:24px; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:12px;">
        <div>
          <div class="label-tech" style="color:var(--primary); margin-bottom:4px;">Identitas Pegawai</div>
          <h3 style="margin:0; font-family:'Sora',sans-serif; font-size:22px; font-weight:700; color:var(--text);">Data Utama Personal</h3>
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline" onclick="alert('Untuk mengajukan perubahan data utama profil, silakan hubungi Subbagian Kepegawaian DSDM UNDIP dengan membawa dokumen pendukung.');" style="font-size:12px; font-weight:600; padding:6px 14px;">
          ✏️ Ajukan Perubahan
        </button>
      </div>

      <div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr; gap:24px 36px;">
        <div>
          <label class="label-tech" style="color:var(--text-muted); display:block; margin-bottom:4px;">Nama Lengkap</label>
          <p style="margin:0; font-size:16px; font-weight:700; color:var(--text);">${escapeHtml(nama)}</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="label-tech" style="color:var(--text-muted); display:block; margin-bottom:4px;">NIP / NIK</label>
          <p style="margin:0; font-size:16px; font-weight:700; color:var(--text);">${escapeHtml(nip)} <span class="badge-auto-fill">(auto-fill)</span></p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="label-tech" style="color:var(--text-muted); display:block; margin-bottom:4px;">Unit Kerja</label>
          <p style="margin:0; font-size:15px; font-weight:600; color:var(--text);">${escapeHtml(unit)}</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="label-tech" style="color:var(--text-muted); display:block; margin-bottom:4px;">Jabatan Terakhir</label>
          <p style="margin:0; font-size:15px; font-weight:600; color:var(--text);">${escapeHtml(jabatan)}</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="label-tech" style="color:var(--text-muted); display:block; margin-bottom:4px;">Pangkat / Golongan</label>
          <p style="margin:0; font-size:15px; font-weight:600; color:var(--text);">${escapeHtml(golongan)}</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="label-tech" style="color:var(--text-muted); display:block; margin-bottom:4px;">TMT BUP (Pensiun)</label>
          <p style="margin:0; font-size:15px; font-weight:600; color:var(--text);">${escapeHtml(pensiun)}</p>
        </div>
        ${Object.keys(p).filter(k => !['nama_lengkap','nip','unit_kerja','jabatan','golongan','tmt_pensiun_bup','tgl_lhr','status_kepegawaian','status_bekerja'].includes(k)).map(k => `
          <div>
            <label class="label-tech" style="color:var(--text-muted); display:block; margin-bottom:4px;">${escapeHtml(labelMap[k] || k)}</label>
            <p style="margin:0; font-size:15px; font-weight:600; color:var(--text);">${escapeHtml(p[k] || '-')}</p>
          </div>
        `).join('')}
      </div>

      <!-- Summary Stats Bar -->
      <div style="margin-top:32px; border-top:1px solid var(--border); display:grid; grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr; gap:16px; padding-top:24px;">
        <div style="background:var(--surface-alt); padding:16px; border-radius:8px; text-align:center;">
          <div class="label-tech" style="color:var(--text-muted); margin-bottom:4px;">Status Akun</div>
          <div style="font-family:'Sora',sans-serif; font-size:17px; font-weight:700; color:var(--primary);">${escapeHtml(SESSION.peran || 'Normal User')}</div>
        </div>
        <div style="background:var(--surface-alt); padding:16px; border-radius:8px; text-align:center;">
          <div class="label-tech" style="color:var(--text-muted); margin-bottom:4px;">Status Kepegawaian</div>
          <div style="font-family:'Sora',sans-serif; font-size:17px; font-weight:700; color:var(--primary);">${escapeHtml(statPeg)}</div>
        </div>
        <div style="background:var(--surface-alt); padding:16px; border-radius:8px; text-align:center;">
          <div class="label-tech" style="color:var(--text-muted); margin-bottom:4px;">Status Bekerja</div>
          <div style="font-family:'Sora',sans-serif; font-size:17px; font-weight:700; color:var(--primary);">${escapeHtml(statBek)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

/* ------------------------- MENU PERUBAHAN STATUS AKUN (Super Admin) ------------------------- */
let peranAkunSearchTimer = null;

function loadPeranAkunList(query = '') {
  const container = document.getElementById('peranAkunList');
  if (container && !query) {
    container.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Memuat daftar akun...</div>';
  }

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (!res.success) { showToast('error', res.message); return; }
      renderPeranAkunList(res.daftar || []);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat daftar akun: ' + (err.message || err)))
    .searchUserRolesForAdmin(SESSION.token, query);
}

function renderPeranAkunList(daftar) {
  const el = document.getElementById('peranAkunList');
  if (!daftar.length) { el.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Tidak ada data pegawai yang cocok dengan pencarian.</div>'; return; }

  el.innerHTML = daftar.map(p => {
    const isSeedSuperAdmin = p.nip === '200103310225061024';
    
    let subRoleSelectHtml = '';
    if (p.role === 'admin' || p.role === 'super_admin') {
      subRoleSelectHtml = `
        <select class="field-input sub-role-select" style="min-height:36px; padding:6px 10px; font-size:12.5px; margin-left:8px;" data-nip="${escapeHtml(p.nip)}">
          <option value="" ${!p.sub_role ? 'selected' : ''}>-- Tanpa Sub-Role --</option>
          <option value="staff" ${p.sub_role === 'staff' ? 'selected' : ''}>Staff</option>
          <option value="supervisor" ${p.sub_role === 'supervisor' ? 'selected' : ''}>Supervisor (SPV)</option>
          <option value="manajer" ${p.sub_role === 'manajer' ? 'selected' : ''}>Manajer</option>
          <option value="wakil_direktur" ${p.sub_role === 'wakil_direktur' ? 'selected' : ''}>Wakil Direktur</option>
          <option value="direktur" ${p.sub_role === 'direktur' ? 'selected' : ''}>Direktur</option>
          <option value="wakil_rektor" ${p.sub_role === 'wakil_rektor' ? 'selected' : ''}>Wakil Rektor</option>
          <option value="rektor" ${p.sub_role === 'rektor' ? 'selected' : ''}>Rektor</option>
        </select>
      `;
    }

    const currentStatusKep = p.status_kepegawaian || '';
    const statusKepSelectHtml = `
      <select class="field-input status-kep-select" style="min-height:36px; padding:6px 10px; font-size:12.5px; margin-left:8px;" data-nip="${escapeHtml(p.nip)}">
        <option value="" ${!currentStatusKep ? 'selected' : ''}>-- Status Kepegawaian --</option>
        <option value="Tenaga Profesional" ${currentStatusKep === 'Tenaga Profesional' ? 'selected' : ''}>Tenaga Profesional</option>
        <option value="Kontrak Penuh Waktu" ${currentStatusKep === 'Kontrak Penuh Waktu' ? 'selected' : ''}>Kontrak Penuh Waktu</option>
        <option value="Kontrak Paruh Waktu" ${currentStatusKep === 'Kontrak Paruh Waktu' ? 'selected' : ''}>Kontrak Paruh Waktu</option>
        <option value="Tenaga Kontrak Penghargaan" ${currentStatusKep === 'Tenaga Kontrak Penghargaan' ? 'selected' : ''}>Tenaga Kontrak Penghargaan</option>
        <option value="Pegawai Tetap Undip Non ASN" ${currentStatusKep === 'Pegawai Tetap Undip Non ASN' ? 'selected' : ''}>Pegawai Tetap Undip Non ASN</option>
        <option value="PNS" ${currentStatusKep === 'PNS' ? 'selected' : ''}>PNS</option>
        <option value="PPPK" ${currentStatusKep === 'PPPK' ? 'selected' : ''}>PPPK</option>
      </select>
    `;

    return `
    <div class="ce-dashboard-list-item" style="cursor:default; flex-wrap:wrap;">
      <div style="flex: 1; min-width: 250px;">
        <div class="cdli-name">${escapeHtml(p.nama)}</div>
        <div class="cdli-meta">NIP ${escapeHtml(p.nip)} &middot; ${escapeHtml(p.unitEsIi || '-')}</div>
        <div class="cdli-meta" style="margin-top:4px;">
          Peran: <strong style="color:#2563eb;">${escapeHtml(p.role)}</strong> &middot; 
          Sub-Role: <strong style="color:#d97706;">${escapeHtml(p.sub_role ? (p.sub_role.toUpperCase().replace(/_/g, ' ')) : 'Tidak ada')}</strong> &middot;
          Status Kepegawaian: <strong style="color:#059669;">${escapeHtml(currentStatusKep || 'Belum Diatur')}</strong>
        </div>
      </div>
      ${isSeedSuperAdmin
        ? '<span class="cdli-badge" style="background:#f3f4f6; color:#1f2937;">SEED SUPER ADMIN (Sistem Utama)</span>'
        : `<div style="display:flex; gap:8px; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap; margin-top:8px;">
            <select class="field-input role-select" style="min-height:36px; padding:6px 10px; font-size:12.5px;" data-nip="${escapeHtml(p.nip)}">
              <option value="normal" ${p.role === 'normal' ? 'selected' : ''}>normal</option>
              <option value="user" ${p.role === 'user' ? 'selected' : ''}>user</option>
              <option value="admin" ${p.role === 'admin' ? 'selected' : ''}>admin</option>
              <option value="super_admin" ${p.role === 'super_admin' ? 'selected' : ''}>super_admin</option>
            </select>
            ${subRoleSelectHtml}
            ${statusKepSelectHtml}
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline kp-small-btn" data-action="simpan-peran" data-nip="${escapeHtml(p.nip)}">Simpan Pengaturan Akun</button>
          </div>`
      }
    </div>
    `;
  }).join('');

  el.querySelectorAll('[data-action="simpan-peran"]').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      const nip = btn.getAttribute('data-nip');
      const roleSelect = el.querySelector(`.role-select[data-nip="${nip}"]`);
      const subRoleSelect = el.querySelector(`.sub-role-select[data-nip="${nip}"]`);
      const statusKepSelect = el.querySelector(`.status-kep-select[data-nip="${nip}"]`);
      
      const newRole = roleSelect.value;
      const newSubRole = subRoleSelect ? subRoleSelect.value : null;
      const newStatusKep = statusKepSelect ? statusKepSelect.value : null;

      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.textContent = 'Menyimpan...';

      const searchQ = val('peranAkun_search');

      try {
        const resRole = await rpc('ubahPeranAkun', [SESSION.token, nip, newRole]);
        if (!resRole || !resRole.success) {
          showToast('error', resRole?.message || 'Gagal mengubah peran.');
          btn.disabled = false;
          btn.textContent = 'Simpan Pengaturan Akun';
          return;
        }

        if (newRole === 'admin' || newRole === 'super_admin') {
          await rpc('setUserSubRole', [SESSION.token, nip, newSubRole]);
        }
        if (newStatusKep) {
          await rpc('setUserStatusKepegawaian', [SESSION.token, nip, newStatusKep]);
        }

        showToast('success', 'Pengaturan akun & status kepegawaian berhasil disimpan.');
        loadPeranAkunList(searchQ);
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('error', 'Gagal menyimpan: ' + (err.message || err));
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.textContent = 'Simpan Pengaturan Akun';
      }
    });
  });
}

function bindPeranAkunSearch() {
  const input = document.getElementById('peranAkun_search');
  if (!input) return;
  input.addEventListener('input', e => {
    clearTimeout(peranAkunSearchTimer);
    const q = e.target.value.trim();
    peranAkunSearchTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      loadPeranAkunList(q);
    }, 300);
  });
}

function logoutUser() {
  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
    google.script.run.logout(SESSION.token);
  }
  sessionStorage.removeItem('sikp_token');
  SESSION = { token: null, user: null };
  currentSelectedTmt = '';
  promosiDashboardData = null;
  document.getElementById('appShell').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('loginScreen').style.display = 'flex';
  document.getElementById('loginForm').reset();
}

function setBtnLoading(btn, loading, label) {
  btn.disabled = loading;
  btn.innerHTML = loading ? '<span class="spinner"></span> ' + label : label;
}

/* ------------------------- SIDEBAR NAV ------------------------- */
function bindSidebarNav() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item[data-view]').forEach(item => {
    item.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const sb = document.querySelector('.sidebar');
      if (sb) sb.classList.remove('mobile-open');
      const view = item.getAttribute('data-view');
      if (view === 'lainnya') {
        showToast('info', 'postpone');
        return;
      }
      document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item[data-view]').forEach(i => i.classList.remove('active'));
      item.classList.add('active');
      document.querySelectorAll('.view-panel').forEach(p => p.style.display = 'none');
      document.getElementById('view-' + view).style.display = 'block';
      document.getElementById('topbarTitle').textContent = item.getAttribute('data-title');
      if (view === 'template') { loadGlosariumTag(); loadTemplateLayananOptions(); loadTemplateList(); }
      if (view === 'pensiun') loadPensiunDashboardOnce();
      if (view === 'peran-akun') loadPeranAkunList();
      if (view === 'kontrak') initKontrakViewForUser();
      if (view === 'pmk') loadPmkUsulanList();
      if (view === 'buat-sk') onEnterBuatSkView();
    });
  });
  document.getElementById('logoutBtn').addEventListener('click', logoutUser);
  bindPeranAkunSearch();
}

/* ------------------------- GLOSARIUM TAG (Menu Lainnya) ------------------------- */
let glosariumLoaded = false;
async function loadGlosariumTag() {
  if (glosariumLoaded) return; // cache: sheet header & daftar tag jarang berubah dalam satu sesi
  try {
    const data = await rpc('getGlosariumTag', [SESSION.token]);
    if (data) {
      glosariumLoaded = true;
      renderGlosariumTag(data);
    }
  } catch(err) {
    showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat glosarium tag: ' + (err.message || err));
  }
}

function renderGlosariumTag(data) {
  const accents = ['accent-1', 'accent-2', 'accent-3'];

  const cardHtml = (label, tag, ket, idx, icon) => `
    <div class="glossary-card ${accents[idx % accents.length]}">
      <div class="card-icon">${icon}</div>
      <div class="card-label">${escapeHtml(label)}</div>
      <code>${escapeHtml(tag)}</code>
      ${ket ? `<div class="helper-text">${escapeHtml(ket)}</div>` : ''}
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-copy" onclick="copyGlosariumTag(this, '${tag.replace(/\\/g, '\\\\').replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">Salin</button>
    </div>`;

  document.getElementById('glosariumSpreadsheetGrid').innerHTML =
    data.tagSpreadsheet.map((t, i) => cardHtml(t.label, t.tag, '', i, '📄')).join('');

  document.getElementById('glosariumTurunanGrid').innerHTML =
    data.tagTurunan.map((t, i) => cardHtml(t.label, t.tag, t.ket, i, '⚙️')).join('');

  document.getElementById('glosariumFormulaList').innerHTML =
    data.referensiFormula
      .map(f => `<tr><td>${escapeHtml(f.label)}</td><td><code>${escapeHtml(f.contoh)}</code></td></tr>`)
      .join('');

  document.getElementById('glosariumCatatan').textContent = data.catatan || '';
}

function copyGlosariumTag(btn, tag) {
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(tag)
    .then(() => showToast('success', 'Tag disalin ke clipboard'))
    .catch(() => showToast('error', 'Gagal menyalin tag'));
}

/* ------------------------- BACA RUMUS DARI TEMPLATE ------------------------- */
function toggleSourceInput() {
  const type = document.querySelector('input[name="sourceType"]:checked').value;
  document.getElementById('sourceInputGdrive').style.display = type === 'gdrive' ? 'block' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('sourceInputUpload').style.display = type === 'upload' ? 'block' : 'none';
}

function bacaRumusTemplate() {
  const type = document.querySelector('input[name="sourceType"]:checked').value;

  if (type === 'gdrive') {
    const url = document.getElementById('driveUrlInput').value.trim();
    if (!url) { showToast('error', 'Isi link Google Drive dulu.'); return; }

    showToast('info', 'Membaca rumus dari Google Drive...');
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(renderHasilScanTemplate)
      .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal membaca template: ' + (err.message || err)))
      .scanTemplateFormulas(SESSION.token, { sourceType: 'gdrive', driveUrl: url });
    return;
  }

  const fileInput = document.getElementById('docxFileInput');
  const file = fileInput.files[0];
  if (!file) { showToast('error', 'Pilih file .docx dulu.'); return; }

  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = () => {
    const base64 = reader.result.split(',')[1];
    showToast('info', 'Membaca rumus dari file...');
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(renderHasilScanTemplate)
      .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal membaca template: ' + (err.message || err)))
      .scanTemplateFormulas(SESSION.token, {
        sourceType: 'upload',
        fileName: file.name,
        fileBase64: base64
      });
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}

function renderHasilScanTemplate(hasil) {
  const box = document.getElementById('hasilScanTemplate');
  const chip = (tag, variant) => `<span class="tag-chip ${variant || ''}"><code>${escapeHtml(tag)}</code></span>`;

  let html = `
    <div class="glossary-card">
      <div class="card-label">Tag Nilai / Formula Ditemukan (${hasil.tagNilai.length})</div>
      <div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:8px; margin-top:8px;">
        ${hasil.tagNilai.map(t => chip('{{' + t + '}}')).join('') || '<span class="helper-text">Tidak ada</span>'}
      </div>
    </div>`;

  if (hasil.tagSetVariable.length) {
    html += `
      <div class="glossary-card accent-3" style="margin-top:12px;">
        <div class="card-label">Variabel Turunan / "set" (${hasil.tagSetVariable.length})</div>
        <p class="helper-text" style="margin:4px 0 8px;">
          Dihitung di dalam template sendiri, bukan diisi dari data context — pastikan mesin mendukung sintaks <code>set nama = ekspresi</code>.
        </p>
        <div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:8px;">
          ${hasil.tagSetVariable.map(t => chip(t, 'accent-3')).join('')}
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }

  html += `
    <div class="glossary-card" style="margin-top:12px;">
      <div class="card-label">Tag Loop Valid (${hasil.tagLoopBenar.length})</div>
      <div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:8px; margin-top:8px;">
        ${hasil.tagLoopBenar.map(t => chip(t, 'accent-1')).join('') || '<span class="helper-text">Tidak ada</span>'}
      </div>
    </div>`;

  if (hasil.tagLoopBermasalah.length || hasil.issues.length) {
    html += `
      <div class="glossary-card accent-warning" style="margin-top:12px;">
        <div class="card-label">⚠ Masalah Ditemukan (${hasil.issues.length})</div>
        <div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:8px; margin:8px 0;">
          ${hasil.tagLoopBermasalah.map(t => chip(t, 'accent-warning')).join('')}
        </div>
        <ul style="margin:0; padding-left:18px;">
          ${hasil.issues.map(i => `<li class="helper-text">${escapeHtml(i.detail)}</li>`).join('')}
        </ul>
      </div>`;
  }

  box.innerHTML = html;
}

/* ------------------------- TABS (Check Eligible / Opsi A / Opsi B) ------------------------- */
function bindTabs() {
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-tab-group]').forEach(group => {
    const groupName = group.getAttribute('data-tab-group');
    group.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => activateTab(groupName, btn.getAttribute('data-tab-target')));
    });
  });
}
function activateTab(groupName, targetId) {
  const group = document.querySelector(`[data-tab-group="${groupName}"]`);
  group.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.getAttribute('data-tab-target') === targetId));
  document.querySelectorAll(`.tab-panel[data-tab-group-panel="${groupName}"]`).forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById(targetId).classList.add('active');
  if (targetId === 'tab-statusUsulan') loadStatusUsulanSaya();
  if (targetId === 'tab-usulanMasuk') loadUsulanMasukDashboard();
  if (targetId === 'tab-siapSk') { bindSkKpNonAsnForm(); loadSiapSkList(); }
  if (targetId === 'tab-approvalSk') loadApprovalSkQueue();
  if (targetId === 'tab-uploadSk') loadUploadSkList();
  if (targetId === 'tab-statusUsulanPensiun') loadStatusUsulanPensiunSaya();
  if (targetId === 'tab-usulanMasukPensiun') loadUsulanMasukPensiunDashboard();
  if (targetId === 'tab-uploadSkPensiun') loadUploadSkPensiunList();
}

/* ------------------------- AUTOCOMPLETE PENCARIAN PEGAWAI ------------------------- */
/**
 * Versi berbasis ELEMEN (bukan id) — dipakai entri Opsi A/B yang dibuat
 * dinamis (fitur "+ Tambah Data"), karena elemen-elemen itu tidak punya
 * id tetap. hiddenNipInput menyimpan NIP terpilih; onSelect(nip, nama)
 * dipanggil setelah user memilih salah satu hasil.
 */
function setupAutocompleteEl(inputEl, listEl, hiddenNipInput, onSelect) {
  let debounceTimer = null;

  inputEl.addEventListener('input', () => {
    const q = inputEl.value.trim();
    hiddenNipInput.value = '';
    clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
    if (q.length < 2) { listEl.style.display = 'none'; return; }

    debounceTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
      const showResults = (results) => {
        listEl.innerHTML = '';
        if (!results || !results.length) { listEl.style.display = 'none'; return; }
        results.forEach(r => {
          const item = document.createElement('div');
          item.className = 'autocomplete-item';
          item.innerHTML = `${escapeHtml(r.nama || r.nama_lengkap || '')}<small>NIP: ${escapeHtml(r.nip || '')}${r.unit_es_ii ? ' - ' + escapeHtml(r.unit_es_ii) : ''}</small>`;
          item.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const displayName = r.nama || r.nama_lengkap || '';
            inputEl.value = `${displayName} (${r.nip})`;
            hiddenNipInput.value = r.nip;
            listEl.style.display = 'none';
            if (onSelect) onSelect(r.nip, displayName, r);
          });
          listEl.appendChild(item);
        });
        listEl.style.display = 'block';
      };

      try {
        const res = await rpc('searchEmployees', [SESSION.token, q]);
        const results = Array.isArray(res) ? res : (res?.data || []);
        showResults(results);
      } catch (err) {
        if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
          google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(showResults)
            .withFailureHandler(e => showToast('error', 'Pencarian gagal: ' + (e.message || e)))
            .searchEmployees(SESSION.token, q);
        } else {
          showToast('error', 'Pencarian gagal: ' + (err.message || err));
        }
      }
    }, 300);
  });

  document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    if (!listEl.contains(e.target) && e.target !== inputEl) listEl.style.display = 'none';
  });
}

function setupAutocompleteElCustom(inputEl, listEl, hiddenNipInput, onSelect, searchFnName) {
  let debounceTimer = null;

  inputEl.addEventListener('input', () => {
    const q = inputEl.value.trim();
    hiddenNipInput.value = '';
    clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
    if (q.length < 2) { listEl.style.display = 'none'; return; }

    debounceTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
      const showResults = (results) => {
        listEl.innerHTML = '';
        if (!results || !results.length) { listEl.style.display = 'none'; return; }
        results.forEach(r => {
          const item = document.createElement('div');
          item.className = 'autocomplete-item';
          item.innerHTML = `${escapeHtml(r.nama || r.nama_lengkap || '')}<small>NIP: ${escapeHtml(r.nip || '')}</small>`;
          item.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const displayName = r.nama || r.nama_lengkap || '';
            inputEl.value = `${displayName} (${r.nip})`;
            hiddenNipInput.value = r.nip;
            listEl.style.display = 'none';
            if (onSelect) onSelect(r.nip, displayName, r);
          });
          listEl.appendChild(item);
        });
        listEl.style.display = 'block';
      };

      try {
        const fnName = searchFnName || 'searchEmployees';
        const res = await rpc(fnName, [SESSION.token, q]);
        const results = Array.isArray(res) ? res : (res?.data || []);
        showResults(results);
      } catch (err) {
        if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && searchFnName && google.script.run[searchFnName]) {
          google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(showResults)
            .withFailureHandler(e => showToast('error', 'Pencarian gagal: ' + (e.message || e)))
            [searchFnName](SESSION.token, q);
        } else {
          showToast('error', 'Pencarian gagal: ' + (err.message || err));
        }
      }
    }, 300);
  });

  document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    if (!listEl.contains(e.target) && e.target !== inputEl) listEl.style.display = 'none';
  });
}

/** Wrapper lama berbasis id (dipakai tab "0. Check Eligible" yang masih statis). */
function setupAutocomplete(inputId, listId, hiddenNipId, onSelect) {
  setupAutocompleteEl(
    document.getElementById(inputId),
    document.getElementById(listId),
    document.getElementById(hiddenNipId),
    onSelect
  );
}

/* ------------------------- SELECT HELPER (sumber opsi selalu dari backend) ------------------------- */
/**
 * items: array of string, ATAU array of object bila valueField/labelField diisi
 * (dipakai khusus untuk daftar Bulan yang bisa direpresentasikan sebagai
 * angka 1-12 untuk kalkulasi, atau sebagai nama untuk teks dokumen).
 */
function populateSelect(idOrEl, items, { placeholder = 'Pilih...', valueField = null, labelField = null } = {}) {
  const sel = typeof idOrEl === 'string' ? document.getElementById(idOrEl) : idOrEl;
  const optionsHtml = (items || []).map(item => {
    if (valueField && labelField) return `<option value="${item[valueField]}">${item[labelField]}</option>`;
    return `<option value="${item}">${item}</option>`;
  }).join('');
  sel.innerHTML = `<option value="">${placeholder}</option>` + optionsHtml;
}

/* ==================================================================
 * TAB 0: CHECK ELIGIBLE
 * ================================================================== */
let _isCheckEligibleFormBound = false;

function bindCheckEligibleForm() {
  populateSelect('ce_golongan', FORM_OPTIONS.golongan, { placeholder: 'Pilih Golongan...' });
  populateSelect('ce_jabatan', FORM_OPTIONS.jabatan);
  populateSelect('ce_jabatanIntegrasi', FORM_OPTIONS.jabatan, { placeholder: 'Pilih Golongan...' });
  populateSelect('ce_golonganIntegrasi', FORM_OPTIONS.golongan, { placeholder: 'Pilih Golongan...' });
  populateSelect('ce_ijazahBaru', FORM_OPTIONS.ijazahBaru);

  if (_isCheckEligibleFormBound) return;
  _isCheckEligibleFormBound = true;

  setupAutocomplete('subjectCE_input', 'subjectCE_list', 'subjectCE_nip', onSelectCheckEligibleSubject);

  const ce_gol = document.getElementById('ce_golongan');
  if (ce_gol) ce_gol.onchange = updateCeTmtGolonganVisibility;

  const ce_jab = document.getElementById('ce_jabatan');
  if (ce_jab) ce_jab.onchange = updateCeTmtJabatanVisibility;

  const ce_addPred = document.getElementById('ce_addPredikatRow');
  if (ce_addPred) ce_addPred.onclick = () => addPredikatRow();

  const predTableBody = document.getElementById('ce_predikatTableBody');
  if (predTableBody) predTableBody.innerHTML = '';
  addPredikatRow(); // baris pertama default

  const ce_hit = document.getElementById('ce_hitungBtn');
  if (ce_hit) ce_hit.onclick = runCheckEligibility;

  const ce_lanjA = document.getElementById('ce_lanjutABtn');
  if (ce_lanjA) ce_lanjA.onclick = () => lanjutKeOpsi('A');

  const ce_lanjB = document.getElementById('ce_lanjutBBtn');
  if (ce_lanjB) ce_lanjB.onclick = () => lanjutKeOpsi('B');

  const ce_ajuk = document.getElementById('ce_ajukanUsulanBtn');
  if (ce_ajuk) ce_ajuk.onclick = bukaModalAjukanUsulan;

  const ce_dash = document.getElementById('ceDashboardAjukanUsulanBtn');
  if (ce_dash) ce_dash.onclick = bukaModalAjukanUsulan;
}

/** Sama seperti updateTmtJabatanVisibility (kartu Opsi A/B), versi berbasis ID untuk tab Check Eligible. */
function updateCeTmtGolonganVisibility() {
  const sel = document.getElementById('ce_golongan');
  const wrap = document.getElementById('ce_tmtGolonganBaruWrap');
  const input = document.getElementById('ce_tmtGolonganBaru');
  const berubah = sel.value !== '' && sel.value !== ceOriginalGolongan;
  wrap.style.display = berubah ? 'block' : 'none';
  if (!berubah) input.value = '';
}
function updateCeTmtJabatanVisibility() {
  const sel = document.getElementById('ce_jabatan');
  const wrap = document.getElementById('ce_tmtJabatanBaruWrap');
  const input = document.getElementById('ce_tmtJabatanBaru');
  const berubah = sel.value !== '' && sel.value !== ceOriginalJabatan;
  wrap.style.display = berubah ? 'block' : 'none';
  if (!berubah) input.value = '';
}

function onSelectCheckEligibleSubject(nip, nama) {
  ceSelectedEmployee = { nip, nama };
  document.getElementById('ce_resultCard').style.display = 'none';

  // Tombol "Ajukan Usulan" hanya akan tampil di bawah hasil kalkulasi jika statusnya Eligible
  const ajukanBtn = document.getElementById('ce_ajukanUsulanBtn');
  if (ajukanBtn) ajukanBtn.style.display = 'none';

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(emp => {
      const golonganSel = document.getElementById('ce_golongan');
      const golonganMatch = cariOpsiCocok_(FORM_OPTIONS.golongan, emp.golongan);
      golonganSel.value = golonganMatch;
      ceOriginalGolongan = golonganMatch;
      updateCeTmtGolonganVisibility();
      document.getElementById('ce_golonganAutoFillMsg').textContent =
        pesanStatusAutoFill_('Golongan', emp.golongan, golonganMatch, 'Otomatis dari data pegawai, dapat diubah bila perlu.');

      document.getElementById('ce_tmtGolonganSaatIni').value = pensiunToDateInputValue(emp.tmt_gol);
      document.getElementById('ce_tmtJabatanSaatIni').value = pensiunToDateInputValue(emp.tmt_jab);

      const jabatanSel = document.getElementById('ce_jabatan');
      const jabatanMatch = cariOpsiCocok_(FORM_OPTIONS.jabatan, emp.jabatan);
      jabatanSel.value = jabatanMatch;
      ceOriginalJabatan = jabatanMatch;
      updateCeTmtJabatanVisibility();
      document.getElementById('ce_jabatanAutoFillMsg').textContent =
        pesanStatusAutoFill_('Jabatan', emp.jabatan, jabatanMatch, 'Otomatis dari data pegawai, dapat diubah bila perlu.');

      const jabatanIntegrasiSel = document.getElementById('ce_jabatanIntegrasi');
      if (jabatanIntegrasiSel) {
        jabatanIntegrasiSel.value = cariOpsiCocok_(FORM_OPTIONS.jabatan, emp.jabatan);
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat data pegawai: ' + (err.message || err)))
    .getEmployeeFullData(SESSION.token, nip);
}

function addPredikatRow() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('ce_predikatTableBody');
  const tr = document.createElement('tr');
  tr.innerHTML = `
    <td><input type="text" class="ce-tahun" placeholder="cth. 2024" /></td>
    <td><select class="ce-predikat"></select></td>
    <td><select class="ce-bulanMulai"></select></td>
    <td><select class="ce-bulanAkhir"></select></td>
    <td><button type="button" class="row-remove-btn" title="Hapus baris">✕</button></td>
  `;
  tbody.appendChild(tr);

  fillSelectOptions(tr.querySelector('.ce-predikat'), FORM_OPTIONS.predikat);
  fillBulanSelectNumeric(tr.querySelector('.ce-bulanMulai'));
  fillBulanSelectNumeric(tr.querySelector('.ce-bulanAkhir'));

  tr.querySelector('.row-remove-btn').addEventListener('click', () => tr.remove());
}
function fillSelectOptions(selectEl, items) {
  selectEl.innerHTML = '<option value="">Pilih</option>' + items.map(o => `<option>${o}</option>`).join('');
}
function fillBulanSelectNumeric(selectEl) {
  selectEl.innerHTML = '<option value="">Pilih</option>' +
    FORM_OPTIONS.bulan.map(b => `<option value="${b.value}">${b.label}</option>`).join('');
}

function collectPredikatRows() {
  const rows = [];
  document.querySelectorAll('#ce_predikatTableBody tr').forEach(tr => {
    rows.push({
      tahun: tr.querySelector('.ce-tahun').value,
      predikat: tr.querySelector('.ce-predikat').value,
      bulanMulai: tr.querySelector('.ce-bulanMulai').value,
      bulanAkhir: tr.querySelector('.ce-bulanAkhir').value
    });
  });
  return rows;
}

function runCheckEligibility() {
  const nip = document.getElementById('subjectCE_nip').value;
  if (!nip) { showToast('error', 'Pilih pegawai pada Search Nama terlebih dahulu.'); return; }

  const golongan = document.getElementById('ce_golongan').value;
  const jabatan = document.getElementById('ce_jabatan').value;
  const jabatanIntegrasi = document.getElementById('ce_jabatanIntegrasi') ? document.getElementById('ce_jabatanIntegrasi').value : '';
  const golonganIntegrasi = document.getElementById('ce_golonganIntegrasi').value;
  const akIntegrasi = document.getElementById('ce_akIntegrasi').value;
  const ijazahBaru = document.getElementById('ce_ijazahBaru').value;

  if (!golongan) { showToast('error', 'Golongan (saat ini) wajib dipilih.'); return; }
  if (!jabatan) { showToast('error', 'Jabatan wajib dipilih.'); return; }
  if (!jabatanIntegrasi) { showToast('error', 'Jabatan Saat Integrasi wajib dipilih.'); return; }
  if (!golonganIntegrasi) { showToast('error', 'Golongan Saat Integrasi wajib dipilih.'); return; }
  if (!ijazahBaru) { showToast('error', 'Ada Ijazah Baru Setelah 2023 wajib dipilih.'); return; }

  const golonganBerubah = golongan !== ceOriginalGolongan;
  if (golonganBerubah && !document.getElementById('ce_tmtGolonganBaru').value) {
    showToast('error', 'Golongan diubah dari data utama — isi dulu "TMT Golongan (baru)" secara manual.');
    return;
  }
  const jabatanBerubah = jabatan !== ceOriginalJabatan;
  if (jabatanBerubah && !document.getElementById('ce_tmtJabatanBaru').value) {
    showToast('error', 'Jabatan diubah dari data utama — isi dulu "TMT Jabatan (baru)" secara manual.');
    return;
  }

  const payload = {
    targetNip: nip,
    golongan: golongan,
    jabatan: jabatan,
    tmt_gol_saat_ini: document.getElementById('ce_tmtGolonganSaatIni').value,
    tmt_jab_saat_ini: document.getElementById('ce_tmtJabatanSaatIni').value,
    akIntegrasi: akIntegrasi,
    jabatanIntegrasi: jabatanIntegrasi,
    golonganIntegrasi: golonganIntegrasi,
    adaIjazahBaru2023: ijazahBaru,
    daftarPredikatSkp: collectPredikatRows()
  };
  if (golonganBerubah) payload.tmt_gol = document.getElementById('ce_tmtGolonganBaru').value;
  if (jabatanBerubah) payload.tmt_jab = document.getElementById('ce_tmtJabatanBaru').value;

  const btn = document.getElementById('ce_hitungBtn');
  setBtnLoading(btn, true, 'Menghitung...');

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Hitung / Cek Eligibility');
      if (!res.success) { showToast('error', res.message); return; }
      ceSelectedEmployee = { nip: res.nip, nama: res.nama };
      renderEligibilityResult(res);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => {
      setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Hitung / Cek Eligibility');
      showToast('error', 'Gagal menghitung: ' + (err.message || err));
    })
    .checkEligibility(SESSION.token, payload);
}

function formatTanggalIndoClient(dateStr) {
  if (!dateStr) return '-';
  const parts = dateStr.split('-');
  if (parts.length === 3) {
    const y = parts[0];
    const m = parseInt(parts[1]) - 1;
    const d = parseInt(parts[2]);
    const listBulan = ['Januari','Februari','Maret','April','Mei','Juni','Juli','Agustus','September','Oktober','November','Desember'];
    if (m >= 0 && m < 12) return `${d} ${listBulan[m]} ${y}`;
  }
  return dateStr;
}

function renderEligibilityResult(res) {
  const card = document.getElementById('ce_resultCard');
  const body = document.getElementById('ce_resultBody');
  const isEligible = res.eligibility.status === 'Eligible';
  const badgeColor = isEligible ? 'var(--success)' : 'var(--danger)';

  const hangusText = res.rincian.isIntegrasiHangus ? ' <span style="color:var(--danger); font-size:12px; font-weight:600;">(Kedaluwarsa / Hangus - Batas Jabatan)</span>' : '';
  const jabKalkulasiLabel = (res.rincian.jabatanKalkulasi && res.rincian.jabatanKalkulasi !== res.jabatan) 
    ? ` <span style="font-size:12px; color:var(--text-muted); font-weight:normal;">(Perhitungan: ${escapeHtml(res.rincian.jabatanKalkulasi)})</span>` 
    : '';

  body.innerHTML = `
    <div class="grid-2">
      <div><strong>Nama</strong><br>${escapeHtml(res.nama)}</div>
      <div><strong>NIP</strong><br>${escapeHtml(res.nip)}</div>
      <div><strong>Golongan Sekarang</strong><br>${escapeHtml(res.golonganSekarang || '-')} (TMT: ${escapeHtml(formatTanggalIndoClient(res.tmtGolonganSaatIni))})</div>
      <div><strong>Jabatan</strong><br>${escapeHtml(res.jabatan)}${jabKalkulasiLabel} (TMT: ${escapeHtml(formatTanggalIndoClient(res.tmtJabatanSaatIni))})</div>
    </div>
    <hr style="border-color:var(--border); margin:14px 0;">
    <div style="font-size:13.5px; line-height:1.9;">
      AK Integrasi&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;: ${fmtNum(res.rincian.akIntegrasi)}${hangusText}<br>
      Total AK Konversi (SKP)&nbsp;&nbsp;: ${fmtNum(res.rincian.totalAkKonversi)}${res.rincian.jabatanKalkulasi ? ' (' + escapeHtml(res.rincian.jabatanKalkulasi) + ')' : ''}<br>
      Nilai Pendidikan Baru&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;: ${fmtNum(res.rincian.nilaiPendidikanBaru)}<br>
      Pengurangan Golongan&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;: - ${fmtNum(res.rincian.pengurangan)}<br>
      <strong style="font-size:15px;">Total AK Akhir&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;: ${fmtNum(res.totalAkAkhir)}</strong>
    </div>
    <div style="margin-top:14px; padding:12px 14px; border-radius:8px; background:${badgeColor}22; border:1px solid ${badgeColor}; color:${badgeColor}; font-weight:700;">
      ${escapeHtml(res.eligibility.status)}
      <div style="font-weight:400; font-size:12.5px; margin-top:4px; color:var(--text); line-height:1.5;">${escapeHtml(res.eligibility.message)}</div>
    </div>
  `;
  card.style.display = 'block';
  // Tombol "Ajukan Usulan" hanya tampil jika hasil perhitungan adalah Eligible
  const ajukanBtn = document.getElementById('ce_ajukanUsulanBtn');
  if (ajukanBtn) {
    ajukanBtn.style.display = (isEligible && SESSION.user.role !== 'normal') ? 'inline-flex' : 'none';
  }
}
function fmtNum(n) { return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 }); }

function lanjutKeOpsi(opt) {
  if (!ceSelectedEmployee) { showToast('error', 'Pilih & hitung data pegawai terlebih dahulu.'); return; }
  activateTab('kp', opt === 'A' ? 'tab-optA' : 'tab-optB');

  const container = document.getElementById(opt === 'A' ? 'optA_entriesContainer' : 'optB_entriesContainer');
  const firstCard = container.querySelector('.kp-entry-card');
  if (!firstCard) return;

  firstCard.querySelector('[data-field="subjectInput"]').value = `${ceSelectedEmployee.nama} (${ceSelectedEmployee.nip})`;
  firstCard.querySelector('[data-field="subjectNip"]').value = ceSelectedEmployee.nip;

  // Bawa override Golongan/Jabatan (kalau diubah manual di Check Eligible)
  // beserta TMT barunya, supaya admin tidak perlu mengetik ulang di Opsi A/B.
  const golonganCE = document.getElementById('ce_golongan').value;
  const jabatanCE = document.getElementById('ce_jabatan').value;
  const overrides = {
    golongan: golonganCE && golonganCE !== ceOriginalGolongan ? golonganCE : '',
    tmtGolonganBaru: document.getElementById('ce_tmtGolonganBaru').value,
    jabatan: jabatanCE && jabatanCE !== ceOriginalJabatan ? jabatanCE : '',
    tmtJabatanBaru: document.getElementById('ce_tmtJabatanBaru').value
  };

  onSelectDocGenSubjectCard(opt, firstCard, ceSelectedEmployee.nip, overrides);
}

/* ==================================================================
 * TAB 1 & 2: KENAIKAN PANGKAT — OPSI A & OPSI B (generate dokumen)
 * Form Opsi A & B berbentuk KARTU ENTRI yang bisa digandakan lewat
 * tombol "+ Tambah Data" — beberapa entri (mis. beberapa pegawai atau
 * beberapa periode) digabung jadi SATU file saat Preview/Generate.
 * ================================================================== */
let _isKenaikanPangkatFormsBound = false;

function bindKenaikanPangkatForms() {
  if (_isKenaikanPangkatFormsBound) return;
  _isKenaikanPangkatFormsBound = true;

  const optA_addBtn = document.getElementById('optA_addEntryBtn');
  if (optA_addBtn) optA_addBtn.onclick = () => addOptAEntry();

  const optA_copyBtn = document.getElementById('optA_copyEntryBtn');
  if (optA_copyBtn) optA_copyBtn.onclick = () => copyOptAEntry();

  const optB_addBtn = document.getElementById('optB_addEntryBtn');
  if (optB_addBtn) optB_addBtn.onclick = () => addOptBEntry();

  const optB_copyBtn = document.getElementById('optB_copyEntryBtn');
  if (optB_copyBtn) optB_copyBtn.onclick = () => copyOptBEntry();

  const optA_container = document.getElementById('optA_entriesContainer');
  if (optA_container) optA_container.innerHTML = '';
  const optB_container = document.getElementById('optB_entriesContainer');
  if (optB_container) optB_container.innerHTML = '';

  addOptAEntry(); // entri pertama default
  addOptBEntry(); // entri pertama default

  const optA_prevBtn = document.getElementById('optA_previewBtn');
  if (optA_prevBtn) optA_prevBtn.onclick = () => handlePreviewGenerate('A', 'preview');

  const optA_genBtn = document.getElementById('optA_generateBtn');
  if (optA_genBtn) optA_genBtn.onclick = () => handlePreviewGenerate('A', 'generate');

  const optB_prevBtn = document.getElementById('optB_previewBtn');
  if (optB_prevBtn) optB_prevBtn.onclick = () => handlePreviewGenerate('B', 'preview');

  const optB_genBtn = document.getElementById('optB_generateBtn');
  if (optB_genBtn) optB_genBtn.onclick = () => handlePreviewGenerate('B', 'generate');
}

/**
 * Saat admin pilih pegawai di satu kartu entri, isi otomatis dropdown
 * Jabatan & Golongan KARTU ITU SAJA (tetap bisa diubah manual).
 * @param {Object} [overrides] opsional -- dipakai lanjutKeOpsi() untuk
 *   membawa perubahan Golongan/Jabatan (+ TMT barunya) dari Check
 *   Eligible, diterapkan SETELAH auto-fill supaya tidak tertimpa.
 */
function onSelectDocGenSubjectCard(opt, cardEl, nip, overrides) {
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(emp => {
      const jabatanSel = cardEl.querySelector('[data-field="jabatan"]');
      const jabatanMatch = cariOpsiCocok_(FORM_OPTIONS.jabatan, emp.jabatan);
      jabatanSel.value = jabatanMatch;
      // Simpan sebagai baseline "data utama" untuk kartu ini — dipakai
      // updateTmtJabatanVisibility() untuk mendeteksi kapan admin BENAR-BENAR
      // mengubah jabatan (bukan sekadar auto-fill dari pegawai yang dipilih).
      cardEl.dataset.originalJabatan = jabatanMatch;
      updateTmtJabatanVisibility(cardEl);
      cardEl.querySelector('[data-field="jabatanAutoFillMsg"]').textContent =
        pesanStatusAutoFill_('Jabatan', emp.jabatan, jabatanMatch, 'Otomatis dari data utama, dapat diubah bila perlu — mengikuti pilihan ini pada hasil generate.');

      const golonganSel = cardEl.querySelector('[data-field="golongan"]');
      const golonganMatch = cariOpsiCocok_(FORM_OPTIONS.golongan, emp.golongan);
      golonganSel.value = golonganMatch;
      // Sama seperti jabatan: simpan baseline & cek apakah perlu tampilkan
      // "TMT Golongan (baru)".
      cardEl.dataset.originalGolongan = golonganMatch;
      updateTmtGolonganVisibility(cardEl);
      cardEl.querySelector('[data-field="golonganAutoFillMsg"]').textContent =
        pesanStatusAutoFill_('Golongan', emp.golongan, golonganMatch, 'Otomatis dari data pegawai, dapat diubah bila perlu (termasuk varian Set.).');

      // Override dari Check Eligible (kalau ada) -- diterapkan SETELAH
      // auto-fill di atas selesai, supaya tidak tertimpa data pegawai.
      if (overrides) {
        if (overrides.golongan) { golonganSel.value = overrides.golongan; updateTmtGolonganVisibility(cardEl); }
        if (overrides.tmtGolonganBaru) cardEl.querySelector('[data-field="tmtGolonganBaru"]').value = overrides.tmtGolonganBaru;
        if (overrides.jabatan) { jabatanSel.value = overrides.jabatan; updateTmtJabatanVisibility(cardEl); }
        if (overrides.tmtJabatanBaru) cardEl.querySelector('[data-field="tmtJabatan"]').value = overrides.tmtJabatanBaru;
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat data pegawai: ' + (err.message || err)))
    .getEmployeeFullData(SESSION.token, nip);
}

/**
 * Tampilkan/sembunyikan kolom "TMT Jabatan (baru)" pada satu kartu entri
 * berdasarkan apakah dropdown Jabatan kartu itu SEDANG BERBEDA dari nilai
 * data utama (originalJabatan). Saat disembunyikan, isiannya dikosongkan
 * lagi supaya tidak ikut terkirim sebagai override tak sengaja.
 */
function updateTmtJabatanVisibility(cardEl) {
  const jabatanSel = cardEl.querySelector('[data-field="jabatan"]');
  const wrap = cardEl.querySelector('[data-field="tmtJabatanWrap"]');
  const tmtInput = cardEl.querySelector('[data-field="tmtJabatan"]');
  const original = cardEl.dataset.originalJabatan || '';
  const berubah = jabatanSel.value !== '' && jabatanSel.value !== original;

  wrap.style.display = berubah ? 'block' : 'none';
  if (!berubah) tmtInput.value = '';
}

/** Sama seperti updateTmtJabatanVisibility di atas, tapi untuk Golongan. */
function updateTmtGolonganVisibility(cardEl) {
  const golonganSel = cardEl.querySelector('[data-field="golongan"]');
  const wrap = cardEl.querySelector('[data-field="tmtGolonganWrap"]');
  const tmtInput = cardEl.querySelector('[data-field="tmtGolonganBaru"]');
  const original = cardEl.dataset.originalGolongan || '';
  const berubah = golonganSel.value !== '' && golonganSel.value !== original;

  wrap.style.display = berubah ? 'block' : 'none';
  if (!berubah) tmtInput.value = '';
}

/** Nomori ulang badge "Data #N" & sembunyikan tombol Hapus bila cuma tersisa 1 entri. */
function renumberEntries(container) {
  const cards = container.querySelectorAll('.kp-entry-card');
  cards.forEach((card, i) => {
    card.querySelector('[data-field="entryBadge"]').textContent = 'Data #' + (i + 1);
    card.querySelector('[data-action="remove-entry"]').style.display = cards.length > 1 ? 'inline-flex' : 'none';
  });
}

/** Ambil value field bertanda data-field="name" di dalam satu kartu entri. */
function fv(card, name) {
  const el = card.querySelector(`[data-field="${name}"]`);
  return el ? el.value : '';
}

/* ---------- OPSI A: kartu entri ---------- */

  card.className = 'kp-entry-card';

      <input type="hidden" data-field="subjectNip" />
      <div class="autocomplete-list" data-field="subjectList"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="grid-2 kp-grid">
      <div class="stack-field">

      </div>
      <div class="stack-field">

      </div>
      <div class="stack-field">
        <label>Bulan Selesai Penilaian</label>
        <select class="field-input" data-field="bulanSelesai"></select>
      </div>
      <div class="stack-field">

        <input type="text" class="field-input" data-field="tanggalSurat" placeholder="Ketik manual, cth: 12 Juli 2026" />
      </div>
      <div class="stack-field">
        <label>Jabatan</label>
        <select class="field-input" data-field="jabatan"></select>
        <div class="helper-text" data-field="jabatanAutoFillMsg">Otomatis dari data utama, dapat diubah bila perlu — mengikuti pilihan ini pada hasil generate.</div>
      </div>
      <div class="stack-field">
        <label>Golongan</label>
        <select class="field-input" data-field="golongan"></select>
        <div class="helper-text" data-field="golonganAutoFillMsg">Otomatis dari data pegawai, dapat diubah bila perlu (termasuk varian Set.).</div>
      </div>
      <div class="stack-field grid-full" data-field="tmtJabatanWrap" style="display:none;">
        <label>TMT Jabatan (baru)</label>
        <input type="text" class="field-input" data-field="tmtJabatan" placeholder="Ketik manual, cth: 1 Agustus 2026" />
        <div class="helper-text">Wajib diisi karena Jabatan diubah dari data utama — TMT lama sudah tidak berlaku untuk jabatan baru ini.</div>
      </div>
      <div class="stack-field grid-full" data-field="tmtGolonganWrap" style="display:none;">
        <label>TMT Golongan (baru)</label>
        <input type="text" class="field-input" data-field="tmtGolonganBaru" placeholder="Ketik manual, cth: 1 Agustus 2026" />
        <div class="helper-text">Wajib diisi karena Golongan diubah dari data utama — TMT lama sudah tidak berlaku untuk golongan baru ini.</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
  container.appendChild(card);

  const bulanNames = FORM_OPTIONS.bulan.map(b => b.label);
  populateSelect(card.querySelector('[data-field="bulanAwal"]'), bulanNames);
  populateSelect(card.querySelector('[data-field="bulanSelesai"]'), bulanNames);
  populateSelect(card.querySelector('[data-field="predikat"]'), DOCGEN_FORM_OPTIONS.predikat);
  populateSelect(card.querySelector('[data-field="jabatan"]'), FORM_OPTIONS.jabatan, { placeholder: 'Pilih Jabatan...' });
  populateSelect(card.querySelector('[data-field="golongan"]'), FORM_OPTIONS.golongan, { placeholder: 'Pilih Golongan...' });
  card.dataset.originalJabatan = '';
  card.dataset.originalGolongan = '';
  card.querySelector('[data-field="jabatan"]').addEventListener('change', () => updateTmtJabatanVisibility(card));
  card.querySelector('[data-field="golongan"]').addEventListener('change', () => updateTmtGolonganVisibility(card));

  setupAutocompleteEl(
    card.querySelector('[data-field="subjectInput"]'),
    card.querySelector('[data-field="subjectList"]'),
    card.querySelector('[data-field="subjectNip"]'),
    nip => onSelectDocGenSubjectCard('A', card, nip)
  );

  card.querySelector('[data-action="remove-entry"]').addEventListener('click', () => {
    card.remove();
    renumberEntries(container);
  });

  renumberEntries(container);
}

function buildFormDataOptA_Card(card) {
  const data = {
    nomor_surat_pak_konversi: fv(card, 'nomorSurat'),
    bulan_awal_penilaian: fv(card, 'bulanAwal'),
    tahun_penilaian: fv(card, 'tahunPenilaian'),
    bulan_selesai_penilaian: fv(card, 'bulanSelesai'),
    predikat_skp: fv(card, 'predikat'),
    tanggal_surat_pak_konversi: fv(card, 'tanggalSurat'),
    jabatan: fv(card, 'jabatan'),
    golongan: fv(card, 'golongan')
  };
  // "tmt_jab"/"tmt_gol" HANYA disertakan kalau memang diisi (jabatan/golongan
  // diubah dari data utama) — supaya tidak menimpa nilai pegawai di data
  // utama dengan string kosong saat TIDAK ada perubahan (kasus mayoritas).
  const tmtJabatan = fv(card, 'tmtJabatan');
  if (tmtJabatan) data.tmt_jab = tmtJabatan;
  const tmtGolonganBaru = fv(card, 'tmtGolonganBaru');
  if (tmtGolonganBaru) data.tmt_gol = tmtGolonganBaru;
  return data;
}
