}

function pensiunGoToFormState() {
  const tabBtn = document.querySelector('[data-tab-group="pensiun"] [data-tab-target="tab-dpcp"]');
  if (tabBtn) tabBtn.click();
  document.getElementById('pensiunDashboardState').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('pensiunFormState').style.display = 'block';
}
function pensiunBackToDashboardState() {
  document.getElementById('pensiunFormState').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('pensiunDashboardState').style.display = 'block';
}

/* ---------- KONVERSI TANGGAL untuk <input type="date"> ---------- */
function pensiunToDateInputValue(value) {
  if (!value) return '';
  const isoMatch = String(value).match(/^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/);
  if (isoMatch) return isoMatch[1];
  const dmy = String(value).match(/^(\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{4})$/);
  if (dmy) return `${dmy[3]}-${dmy[2].padStart(2, '0')}-${dmy[1].padStart(2, '0')}`;
  const d = new Date(value);
  if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  return '';
}

/* ---------- PILIH PEGAWAI (dari dashboard ATAU search manual) ---------- */
function onSelectPensiunEmployee(nip, nama) {
  if (SESSION.user.role === 'user') {
    bukaModalAjukanUsulanPensiun(nip, nama);
    return;
  }
  pensiunGoToFormState();
  document.getElementById('subjectPensiun_input').value = `${nama} (${nip})`;
  document.getElementById('subjectPensiun_nip').value = nip;

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(emp => {
      document.getElementById('pensiun_nip').value = emp.nip || '';
      document.getElementById('pensiun_namaLengkap').value = emp.nama_lengkap || emp.nama || '';
      document.getElementById('pensiun_nama').value = emp.nama || '';
      document.getElementById('pensiun_tmpLhr').value = emp.tmp_lhr || '';
      document.getElementById('pensiun_tglLhr').value = pensiunToDateInputValue(emp.tgl_lhr);
      document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtCpns').value = pensiunToDateInputValue(emp.tmt_pengangkatan);
      document.getElementById('pensiun_pangkat').value = emp.pangkat || '';
      document.getElementById('pensiun_golongan').value = emp.golongan || '';
      document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtGol').value = pensiunToDateInputValue(emp.tmt_gol);
      document.getElementById('pensiun_jabatan').value = emp.jabatan || '';
      document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtJab').value = pensiunToDateInputValue(emp.tmt_jab);
      document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtPensiun').value = pensiunToDateInputValue(emp.tmt_pensiun_bup);
      document.getElementById('pensiun_unitEsIi').value = emp.unit_es_ii || '';

      // REVISI: alamat & pendidikan_pertama sebelumnya SELALU kosong
      // (harus diketik manual walau datanya ADA di Data Utama) -- sekarang
      // auto-fill dengan pola sama seperti Kontrak, tetap bisa diedit.
      const pensiunAlamatMsg = document.getElementById('pensiun_alamatMsg');
      if (emp.alamat) {
        document.getElementById('pensiun_alamat').value = emp.alamat;
        if (pensiunAlamatMsg) pensiunAlamatMsg.textContent = 'Otomatis dari data utama, dapat diubah bila perlu.';
      } else {
        document.getElementById('pensiun_alamat').value = '';
        if (pensiunAlamatMsg) pensiunAlamatMsg.textContent = 'Data utama tidak memiliki Alamat untuk pegawai ini — wajib diisi manual.';
      }
      // "Pendidikan Pertama" BISA berbeda dari Pendidikan saat ini (mis.
      // sudah studi lanjut sejak awal masuk) -- diisi otomatis dari
      // Pendidikan di Data Utama sebagai TITIK AWAL yang wajar, TETAP
      // harus dicek/diedit admin kalau memang berbeda.
      document.getElementById('pensiun_pendidikanPertama').value = emp.pendidikan || '';

      pensiunRunRealtimeCalculations();
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat data pegawai: ' + (err.message || err)))
    .getEmployeeFullData(SESSION.token, nip);

  // REF: fitur "gunakan data generate sebelumnya"
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(riwayat => {
      const msg = document.getElementById('pensiunRiwayatMsg');
      if (riwayat) {
        msg.style.display = 'block';
        msg.innerHTML = 'Ditemukan riwayat DPCP sebelumnya untuk pegawai ini. ' +
          '<button type="button" class="btn btn-outline" style="padding:4px 10px; font-size:11.5px; margin-left:6px;" id="pensiunPakaiRiwayatBtn">Gunakan Data Ini</button>';
        pensiunLatestRiwayat = riwayat;
        document.getElementById('pensiunPakaiRiwayatBtn').addEventListener('click', pensiunTerapkanRiwayat);
      } else {
        msg.style.display = 'none';
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(() => {})
    .getLatestSavedGenerateData(SESSION.token, nip);
}

let pensiunLatestRiwayat = null;
function pensiunSetTahunBulanFromText(tahunId, bulanId, text) {
  const s = String(text || '');
  const thMatch = s.match(/(\d+)\s*tah/i);
  const blMatch = s.match(/(\d+)\s*bul/i);
  document.getElementById(tahunId).value = thMatch ? thMatch[1] : '';
  document.getElementById(bulanId).value = blMatch ? blMatch[1] : '';
}
function pensiunTerapkanRiwayat() {
  const r = pensiunLatestRiwayat;
  if (!r) return;
  document.getElementById('pensiun_jenisPensiun').value = r.jenis_pensiun || '';
  pensiunToggleJenisPensiun();
  document.getElementById('pensiun_tmpLhr').value = r.tmp_lhr || '';
  if (r.tgl_lhr) document.getElementById('pensiun_tglLhr').value = r.tgl_lhr;
  if (r.tmt_cpns) document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtCpns').value = r.tmt_cpns;
  document.getElementById('pensiun_pangkat').value = r.pangkat || '';
  document.getElementById('pensiun_golongan').value = r.golongan || '';
  if (r.tmt_gol) document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtGol').value = r.tmt_gol;
  document.getElementById('pensiun_jabatan').value = r.jabatan || '';
  if (r.tmt_jab) document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtJab').value = r.tmt_jab;
  if (r.tmt_pensiun) document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtPensiun').value = r.tmt_pensiun;
  if (r.bulan_terakhir_bekerja) document.getElementById('pensiun_bulanTerakhirBekerja').value = r.bulan_terakhir_bekerja;
  document.getElementById('pensiun_unitEsIi').value = r.unit_es_ii || '';
  document.getElementById('pensiun_gajiPokok').value = r.gaji_pokok || '';
  document.getElementById('pensiun_pendidikanPertama').value = r.pendidikan_pertama || '';
  document.getElementById('pensiun_alamat').value = r.alamat || '';

  pensiunSetTahunBulanFromText('pensiun_mkKpTahun', 'pensiun_mkKpBulan', r.mk_kp_terakhir);
  pensiunSetTahunBulanFromText('pensiun_mkSebelumTahun', 'pensiun_mkSebelumBulan', r.mk_sebelum_cpns);
  pensiunSetTahunBulanFromText('pensiun_cltnTahun', 'pensiun_cltnBulan', r.cltn);
  pensiunSetTahunBulanFromText('pensiun_pmkTahun', 'pensiun_pmkBulan', r.pmk);

  document.getElementById('pensiunPasanganContainer').innerHTML = '';
  (r.pasangan || []).forEach(p => pensiunAddPasanganRow(p));
  document.getElementById('pensiunAnakContainer').innerHTML = '';
  (r.anak || []).forEach(a => pensiunAddAnakRow(a));

  pensiunRunRealtimeCalculations();
  showToast('success', 'Data riwayat berhasil diterapkan ke form.');
}

/* ---------- JENIS PENSIUN & AUTO TMT PENSIUN (REF: toggleJenisPensiunFields/calculateAutoTmtPensiun) ---------- */
function pensiunToggleJenisPensiun() {
  const type = document.getElementById('pensiun_jenisPensiun').value;
  const wrap = document.getElementById('pensiun_tglPeristiwaWrap');
  const label = document.getElementById('pensiun_tglPeristiwaLabel');
  if (type === 'BUP' || !type) {
    wrap.style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('pensiun_tglPeristiwa').value = '';
  } else {
    wrap.style.display = 'block';
    const labelMap = {
      'Meninggal': 'Tanggal Wafat Resmi',
      'Diberhentikan': 'Tanggal Dekrit Pemberhentian',
      'Pengunduran Diri': 'Tanggal Surat Pengunduran Diri',
      'Uzur': 'Tanggal Penetapan Uzur'
    };
    label.textContent = labelMap[type] || 'Tanggal Peristiwa';
  }
  pensiunCalculateAutoTmtPensiun();
}
function pensiunCalculateAutoTmtPensiun() {
  const type = document.getElementById('pensiun_jenisPensiun').value;
  const tglRaw = document.getElementById('pensiun_tglPeristiwa').value; // YYYY-MM-DD
  if (!tglRaw || type === 'BUP') { pensiunRunRealtimeCalculations(); return; }

  document.getElementById('pensiun_bulanTerakhirBekerja').value = tglRaw;

  const parts = tglRaw.split('-').map(Number);
  let nextMonth = parts[1] + 1, nextYear = parts[0];
  if (nextMonth > 12) { nextMonth = 1; nextYear++; }
  document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtPensiun').value = `${nextYear}-${String(nextMonth).padStart(2, '0')}-01`;

  pensiunRunRealtimeCalculations();
}

/* ---------- MASA KERJA REALTIME (REF: runRealtimeCalculations + helper parse/format) ---------- */
function pensiunMonthsFromPair(tahunId, bulanId) {
  const t = parseInt(document.getElementById(tahunId).value) || 0;
  const b = parseInt(document.getElementById(bulanId).value) || 0;
  return t * 12 + b;
}
function pensiunDateDiffMonths(dateStr1, dateStr2) {
  if (!dateStr1 || !dateStr2) return 0;
  const d1 = new Date(dateStr1 + 'T00:00:00'), d2 = new Date(dateStr2 + 'T00:00:00');
  if (isNaN(d1.getTime()) || isNaN(d2.getTime())) return 0;
  let months = (d2.getFullYear() - d1.getFullYear()) * 12 + (d2.getMonth() - d1.getMonth());
  if (d2.getDate() < d1.getDate()) months -= 1;
  return months <= 0 ? 0 : months;
}
function pensiunFormatMonths(totalMonths) {
  if (totalMonths <= 0 || isNaN(totalMonths)) return '0 TAHUN 0 BULAN';
  const y = Math.floor(totalMonths / 12);
  const m = totalMonths % 12;
  return `${y} TAHUN ${m} BULAN`;
}
function pensiunRunRealtimeCalculations() {
  const tmtGol = document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtGol').value;
  const tmtCpns = document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtCpns').value;
  const tmtPensiun = document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtPensiun').value;
  const bulanTerakhir = document.getElementById('pensiun_bulanTerakhirBekerja').value;

  const mkKpBulan = pensiunMonthsFromPair('pensiun_mkKpTahun', 'pensiun_mkKpBulan');
  const mkSebelumBulan = pensiunMonthsFromPair('pensiun_mkSebelumTahun', 'pensiun_mkSebelumBulan');
  const cltnBulan = pensiunMonthsFromPair('pensiun_cltnTahun', 'pensiun_cltnBulan');
  const pmkBulan = pensiunMonthsFromPair('pensiun_pmkTahun', 'pensiun_pmkBulan');

  const netMkGol = (tmtGol && bulanTerakhir) ? pensiunDateDiffMonths(tmtGol, bulanTerakhir) + mkKpBulan : 0;
  document.getElementById('pensiun_txtMkGolongan').textContent = pensiunFormatMonths(netMkGol);

  const netMkPns = (tmtCpns && bulanTerakhir) ? pensiunDateDiffMonths(tmtCpns, bulanTerakhir) - cltnBulan + pmkBulan : 0;
  document.getElementById('pensiun_txtMkPns').textContent = pensiunFormatMonths(netMkPns);

  const netMkPensiun = (tmtCpns && tmtPensiun) ? pensiunDateDiffMonths(tmtCpns, tmtPensiun) + mkSebelumBulan - cltnBulan + pmkBulan : 0;
  document.getElementById('pensiun_txtMkPensiun').textContent = pensiunFormatMonths(netMkPensiun);
}

/* ---------- RUPIAH & FOTO (REF: formatInputRupiah/processImageToBase64) ---------- */
function pensiunFormatRupiah(el) {
  const val = el.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
  if (!val) return;
  el.value = new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID', { style: 'currency', currency: 'IDR', minimumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(val);
}
function pensiunProcessImageToBase64(el) {
  const file = el.files[0];
  const msg = document.getElementById('pensiun_fotoMsg');
  if (!file) return;
  if (file.size > 1024 * 1024) {
    showToast('error', 'Ukuran foto melebihi 1 MB.');
    el.value = '';
    msg.style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = e => {
    pensiunFotoBase64String = e.target.result;
    msg.style.display = 'block';
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}

/* ---------- DATA KELUARGA: PASANGAN & ANAK (kartu entri, pola sama dgn Kenaikan Pangkat) ---------- */
function pensiunAddPasanganRow(prefill) {
  const container = document.getElementById('pensiunPasanganContainer');
  if (container.children.length >= 2) { showToast('error', 'Maksimal 2 data pasangan.'); return; }
  const card = document.createElement('div');
  card.className = 'kp-entry-card';
  card.innerHTML = `
    <div class="kp-entry-header">
      <span class="kp-entry-badge">Pasangan #${container.children.length + 1}</span>
      <button type="button" class="kp-entry-remove" data-action="remove">🗑 Hapus</button>
    </div>
    <div class="grid-2 kp-grid">
      <div class="stack-field"><label>NIK</label><input type="text" class="field-input" data-field="nik" /></div>
      <div class="stack-field"><label>Nama Lengkap</label><input type="text" class="field-input" data-field="nama_pasangan" /></div>
      <div class="stack-field"><label>Tanggal Lahir</label><input type="date" class="field-input" data-field="tgl_lahir_pasangan" /></div>
      <div class="stack-field"><label>Tanggal Menikah</label><input type="date" class="field-input" data-field="tgl_kawin" /></div>
      <div class="stack-field"><label>Tanggal Cerai/Meninggal Dunia</label><input type="date" class="field-input" data-field="tgl_cerai" /></div>
      <div class="stack-field"><label>Istri/Suami Ke-</label><input type="number" min="1" class="field-input" data-field="keterangan_pasangan" /></div>
    </div>`;
  container.appendChild(card);
  card.querySelector('[data-action="remove"]').addEventListener('click', () => {
    card.remove();
    Array.from(container.children).forEach((c, i) => { c.querySelector('.kp-entry-badge').textContent = 'Pasangan #' + (i + 1); });
  });
  if (prefill) {
    Object.keys(prefill).forEach(k => {
      const input = card.querySelector(`[data-field="${k}"]`);
      if (input) input.value = prefill[k];
    });
  }
}
function pensiunCollectPasangan() {
  return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#pensiunPasanganContainer .kp-entry-card')).map(card => ({
    nik: card.querySelector('[data-field="nik"]').value,
    nama_pasangan: card.querySelector('[data-field="nama_pasangan"]').value,
    tgl_lahir_pasangan: card.querySelector('[data-field="tgl_lahir_pasangan"]').value,
    tgl_kawin: card.querySelector('[data-field="tgl_kawin"]').value,
    tgl_cerai: card.querySelector('[data-field="tgl_cerai"]').value,
    keterangan_pasangan: card.querySelector('[data-field="keterangan_pasangan"]').value
  }));
}

function pensiunAddAnakRow(prefill) {
  const container = document.getElementById('pensiunAnakContainer');
  if (container.children.length >= 5) { showToast('error', 'Maksimal 5 data anak.'); return; }
  const card = document.createElement('div');
  card.className = 'kp-entry-card';

      <span class="kp-entry-badge">Anak #${container.children.length + 1}</span>

    </div>
    <div class="grid-2 kp-grid">
      <div class="stack-field"><label>NIK</label><input type="text" class="field-input" data-field="nik" /></div>
      <div class="stack-field"><label>Nama Lengkap Anak</label><input type="text" class="field-input" data-field="nama_anak" /></div>

      <div class="stack-field"><label>Nama Orang Tua Kandung</label><input type="text" class="field-input" data-field="nama_ortu" /></div>
      <div class="stack-field">
        <label>Hubungan Hukum</label>
        <select class="field-input" data-field="keterangan_anak">
          <option value="AK">AK (Anak Kandung Sah)</option>
          <option value="AT">AT (Anak Tiri)</option>
          <option value="AA">AA (Anak Angkat)</option>
        </select>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  container.appendChild(card);
  card.querySelector('[data-action="remove"]').addEventListener('click', () => {
    card.remove();
    Array.from(container.children).forEach((c, i) => { c.querySelector('.kp-entry-badge').textContent = 'Anak #' + (i + 1); });
  });
  if (prefill) {
    Object.keys(prefill).forEach(k => {
      const input = card.querySelector(`[data-field="${k}"]`);
      if (input) input.value = prefill[k];
    });
  }
}
function pensiunCollectAnak() {
  return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#pensiunAnakContainer .kp-entry-card')).map(card => ({
    nik: card.querySelector('[data-field="nik"]').value,
    nama_anak: card.querySelector('[data-field="nama_anak"]').value,
    tg_lahir_anak: card.querySelector('[data-field="tg_lahir_anak"]').value,
    nama_ortu: card.querySelector('[data-field="nama_ortu"]').value,
    keterangan_anak: card.querySelector('[data-field="keterangan_anak"]').value
  }));
}

/* ---------- PAYLOAD & PREVIEW/GENERATE (REF: payload di executeFinalFormSubmission) ---------- */
function pensiunBuildFormData() {
  return {
    isSaveActive: document.getElementById('pensiun_isSaveActive').checked,
    jenis_pensiun: document.getElementById('pensiun_jenisPensiun').value,
    nip: document.getElementById('pensiun_nip').value,
    nama_lengkap: document.getElementById('pensiun_namaLengkap').value,
    nama: document.getElementById('pensiun_nama').value,
    tmp_lhr: document.getElementById('pensiun_tmpLhr').value,
    tgl_lhr: document.getElementById('pensiun_tglLhr').value,
    tmt_cpns: document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtCpns').value,
    pangkat: document.getElementById('pensiun_pangkat').value,
    golongan: document.getElementById('pensiun_golongan').value,
    tmt_gol: document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtGol').value,
    jabatan: document.getElementById('pensiun_jabatan').value,
    tmt_jab: document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtJab').value,
    tmt_pensiun: document.getElementById('pensiun_tmtPensiun').value,
    bulan_terakhir_bekerja: document.getElementById('pensiun_bulanTerakhirBekerja').value,
    unit_es_ii: document.getElementById('pensiun_unitEsIi').value,
    gaji_pokok: document.getElementById('pensiun_gajiPokok').value,
    mk_kp_terakhir: pensiunFormatMonths(pensiunMonthsFromPair('pensiun_mkKpTahun', 'pensiun_mkKpBulan')),
    mk_sebelum_cpns: pensiunFormatMonths(pensiunMonthsFromPair('pensiun_mkSebelumTahun', 'pensiun_mkSebelumBulan')),
    alamat: document.getElementById('pensiun_alamat').value,
    pendidikan_pertama: document.getElementById('pensiun_pendidikanPertama').value,
    cltn: pensiunFormatMonths(pensiunMonthsFromPair('pensiun_cltnTahun', 'pensiun_cltnBulan')),
    pmk: pensiunFormatMonths(pensiunMonthsFromPair('pensiun_pmkTahun', 'pensiun_pmkBulan')),
    fotoBase64: pensiunFotoBase64String,
    mk_golongan: document.getElementById('pensiun_txtMkGolongan').textContent,
    mk_pns: document.getElementById('pensiun_txtMkPns').textContent,
    mk_pensiun: document.getElementById('pensiun_txtMkPensiun').textContent,
    tgl_peristiwa_asli: document.getElementById('pensiun_tglPeristiwa').value,
    nomor_super_tidak_hukdis_sedangberat: document.getElementById('pensiun_nomorSuperHukdis').value,
    nomor_super_pidana: document.getElementById('pensiun_nomorSuperPidana').value,
    pasangan: pensiunCollectPasangan(),
    anak: pensiunCollectAnak()
  };
}

function openPensiunResultModal(res, label) {
  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'Dokumen ' + label + ' Siap';
  const isDocx = res.outputType === 'docx' || (res.fileName && String(res.fileName).toLowerCase().endsWith('.docx')) || res.base64;

  if (isDocx && res.base64) {
    const dataUrl = 'data:' + (res.mimeType || 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document') + ';base64,' + res.base64;
    const fileName = res.fileName || `${label}_${res.nip || 'dokumen'}.docx`;

    // Otomatis download file Word jika ms word
    try {
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = dataUrl;
      link.download = fileName;
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      document.body.removeChild(link);
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Auto download docx error:', e);
    }

    document.getElementById('modalBody').innerHTML = `
      <div style="padding:24px; text-align:center;">
        <div style="font-size:40px; margin-bottom:12px;">🎉</div>
        <p style="margin-bottom:18px; font-size:14.5px;">Dokumen <strong>${label}</strong> berhasil dibuat sebagai berkas <strong>MS Word (.docx)</strong>.</p>
        <p style="font-size:12.5px; color:var(--text-muted); margin-bottom:20px;">Unduhan berkas otomatis dimulai. Jika belum terunduh, klik tombol di bawah:</p>
        <div style="display:flex; justify-content:center; gap:10px;">
          <a class="btn btn-primary" style="text-decoration:none; padding:10px 20px; font-size:14px; font-weight:600;" href="${dataUrl}" download="${fileName}">⬇ Unduh Dokumen (.docx)</a>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  } else {
    const viewUrl = res.docUrl || res.viewUrl || (res.fileId ? `https://docs.google.com/document/d/${res.fileId}/edit` : '#');

    // Buka otomatis ke tab baru jika template GDocs
    if (viewUrl && viewUrl !== '#') {
      try {
        window.open(viewUrl, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Auto window.open blocked:', e);
      }
    }

    document.getElementById('modalBody').innerHTML = `
      <div style="padding:24px; text-align:center;">
        <div style="font-size:40px; margin-bottom:12px;">🎉</div>
        <p style="margin-bottom:18px; font-size:14.5px;">Dokumen <strong>${label}</strong> berhasil dibuat dan tersimpan di <strong>Google Docs</strong>.</p>
        <div style="display:flex; justify-content:center; gap:10px;">
          <a class="btn btn-primary" style="text-decoration:none; padding:10px 20px; font-size:14px; font-weight:600;" href="${viewUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">📄 Buka Dokumen di Tab Baru ↗</a>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }
  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').style.display = 'flex';
}

function pensiunRunPreviewDpcp() {
  const fd = pensiunBuildFormData();
  if (!fd.nip) { showToast('error', 'Pilih pegawai terlebih dahulu.'); return; }

  const selectEl = document.getElementById('pensiun_dpcpTemplateSelect');
  if (!selectEl || !selectEl.value) {
    showToast('error', 'Pilih template DPCP terlebih dahulu.');
    return;
  }
  const selectedOption = selectEl.options[selectEl.selectedIndex];
  const tipe = selectedOption?.dataset?.tipe || (document.querySelector('input[name="pensiun_dpcpTemplateSelectType"]:checked')?.value || 'gdocs');
  const templateId = selectEl.value;

  const btn = document.getElementById('pensiun_previewDpcpBtn');
  setBtnLoading(btn, true, 'Membuat preview DPCP...');

  const payload = {
    templateId,
    templateFileId: templateId,
    layanan: 'Pensiun',
    subLayanan: 'DPCP',
    formData: fd,
    entries: [{ formData: fd, targetNip: fd.nip }]
  };

  if (tipe === 'docx') {
    showToast('info', 'Membuat preview DPCP (.docx)...');
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(res => {
        setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Preview DPCP');
        if (res && res.success) {
          if (res.outputType === 'pdf') {
            openPreviewModal(res.pdfUrl);
          } else {
            openDocxPreviewModal(res.base64, res.fileName || 'Preview_DPCP.docx');
          }
        } else {
          showToast('error', (res && res.message) || 'Gagal preview DPCP.');
        }
      })
      .withFailureHandler(err => {
        setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Preview DPCP');
        showToast('error', 'Gagal preview DPCP: ' + (err.message || err));
      })
      .previewDocumentVercel(SESSION.token, payload);
  } else {
    showToast('info', 'Membuat preview DPCP (Google Docs)...');
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(res => {
        setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Preview DPCP');
        if (res && res.success) {
          if (res.base64) {
            const blobUrl = convertBase64ToBlobUrl_(res.base64, 'application/pdf');
            openPreviewModal(blobUrl);
          } else if (res.previewUrl) {
            openPreviewModal(res.previewUrl);
          } else {
            showToast('error', 'URL preview DPCP tidak ditemukan.');
          }
        } else {
          showToast('error', (res && res.message) || 'Gagal preview DPCP.');
        }
      })
      .withFailureHandler(err => {
        setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Preview DPCP');
        showToast('error', 'Gagal preview DPCP: ' + (err.message || err));
      })
      .previewDocument(SESSION.token, payload);
  }
}

function pensiunRunGenerateDpcp() {
  const fd = pensiunBuildFormData();
  if (!fd.nip) { showToast('error', 'Pilih pegawai terlebih dahulu.'); return; }
  if (!fd.jenis_pensiun) { showToast('error', 'Jenis Pensiun wajib dipilih.'); return; }

  const selectEl = document.getElementById('pensiun_dpcpTemplateSelect');
  if (!selectEl || !selectEl.value) {
    showToast('error', 'Pilih template DPCP terlebih dahulu.');
    return;
  }
  const selectedOption = selectEl.options[selectEl.selectedIndex];
  const tipe = selectedOption?.dataset?.tipe || (document.querySelector('input[name="pensiun_dpcpTemplateSelectType"]:checked')?.value || 'gdocs');
  const templateId = selectEl.value;

  const btn = document.getElementById('pensiun_generateDpcpBtn');
  setBtnLoading(btn, true, 'Memproses DPCP...');

  const payload = {
    templateId,
    templateFileId: templateId,
    layanan: 'Pensiun',
    subLayanan: 'DPCP',
    formData: fd,
    entries: [{ formData: fd, targetNip: fd.nip }]
  };

  showToast('info', 'Memproses dokumen DPCP, mohon tunggu...');
  if (tipe === 'docx') {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(res => {
        setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Generate DPCP');
        if (res && res.success) {
          showToast('success', res.message || 'Dokumen DPCP berhasil dibuat.');
          openPensiunResultModal(res, 'DPCP');
        } else {
          showToast('error', (res && res.message) || 'Gagal generate DPCP.');
        }
      })
      .withFailureHandler(err => {
        setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Generate DPCP');
        showToast('error', 'Gagal generate DPCP: ' + (err.message || err));
      })
      .generateDocumentVercel(SESSION.token, payload);
  } else {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(res => {
        setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Generate DPCP');
        if (res && res.success) {
          showToast('success', res.message || 'Dokumen DPCP berhasil dibuat.');
          openPensiunResultModal(res, 'DPCP');
        } else {
          showToast('error', (res && res.message) || 'Gagal generate DPCP.');
        }
      })
      .withFailureHandler(err => {
        setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Generate DPCP');
        showToast('error', 'Gagal generate DPCP: ' + (err.message || err));
      })
      .generateDocument(SESSION.token, payload);
  }
}

/* ---------- BIND SEMUA EVENT MODUL PENSIUN ---------- */
let _isPensiunFormBound = false;

function bindPensiunForm() {
  const defaultJenisPensiun = [
    'DPCP',
    'SUPER',
    'SK Pensiun BUP Pegawai Undip Non ASN',
    'SK Pensiun Meninggal Pegawai Undip Non ASN',
    'SK Pensiun Uzur Pegawai Undip Non ASN',
    'SK Pensiun Undur Diri Pegawai Undip Non ASN'
  ];
  populateSelect('pensiun_jenisPensiun', defaultJenisPensiun, { placeholder: 'Pilih Jenis Pensiun...' });

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(opts => {
      if (opts && opts.jenisPensiun) {
        populateSelect('pensiun_jenisPensiun', opts.jenisPensiun, { placeholder: 'Pilih Jenis Pensiun...' });
      }
    })
    .getPensiunFormOptions();

  if (_isPensiunFormBound) return;
  _isPensiunFormBound = true;

  setupAutocomplete('subjectPensiun_input', 'subjectPensiun_list', 'subjectPensiun_nip', onSelectPensiunEmployee);

  const pensiunGoToForm = document.getElementById('pensiunGoToFormBtn');
  if (pensiunGoToForm) {
    pensiunGoToForm.onclick = () => {
      if (SESSION.user.role === 'user') bukaModalAjukanUsulanPensiun();
      else pensiunGoToFormState();
    };
  }

  const pensiunBackDash = document.getElementById('pensiunBackToDashboard');
  if (pensiunBackDash) pensiunBackDash.onclick = pensiunBackToDashboardState;
  document.getElementById('pensiunBackToUnit').addEventListener('click', () => {
    document.getElementById('pensiunDashboardLevel1').style.display = 'grid';
    document.getElementById('pensiunDashboardLevel2').style.display = 'none';
  });

  document.getElementById('pensiun_jenisPensiun').addEventListener('change', pensiunToggleJenisPensiun);
  document.getElementById('pensiun_tglPeristiwa').addEventListener('change', pensiunCalculateAutoTmtPensiun);
  ['pensiun_tmtGol', 'pensiun_tmtCpns', 'pensiun_tmtPensiun', 'pensiun_bulanTerakhirBekerja'].forEach(id => {
    document.getElementById(id).addEventListener('input', pensiunRunRealtimeCalculations);
  });
  ['pensiun_mkKpTahun', 'pensiun_mkKpBulan', 'pensiun_mkSebelumTahun', 'pensiun_mkSebelumBulan',
   'pensiun_cltnTahun', 'pensiun_cltnBulan', 'pensiun_pmkTahun', 'pensiun_pmkBulan'].forEach(id => {
    document.getElementById(id).addEventListener('input', pensiunRunRealtimeCalculations);
  });
  document.getElementById('pensiun_gajiPokok').addEventListener('blur', e => pensiunFormatRupiah(e.target));
  document.getElementById('pensiun_fotoUpload').addEventListener('change', e => pensiunProcessImageToBase64(e.target));

  document.getElementById('pensiun_addPasangan').addEventListener('click', () => pensiunAddPasanganRow());
  document.getElementById('pensiun_addAnak').addEventListener('click', () => pensiunAddAnakRow());

  document.getElementById('pensiun_previewDpcpBtn').addEventListener('click', pensiunRunPreviewDpcp);
  document.getElementById('pensiun_generateDpcpBtn').addEventListener('click', pensiunRunGenerateDpcp);
  document.getElementById('pensiun_previewSuperBtn').addEventListener('click', pensiunRunPreviewSuper);
  document.getElementById('pensiun_generateSuperBtn').addEventListener('click', pensiunRunGenerateSuper);
}

/* ---------- USULAN MASUK (peran admin/super_admin) ---------- */
let usulanPieChartInstance = null;

async function loadUsulanMasukBadge() {
  try {
    const res = await rpc('getUsulanNotifikasiSummary', [SESSION.token]);
    if (!res || !res.success) return;
    const badge = document.getElementById('usulanMasukBadge');
    if (badge) {
      if (res.totalUsulanBaru > 0) {
        badge.textContent = res.totalUsulanBaru;
        badge.style.display = 'inline-block';
      } else {
        badge.style.display = 'none';
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(res => {
          if (!res || !res.success) return;
          const badge = document.getElementById('usulanMasukBadge');
          if (badge) {
            if (res.totalUsulanBaru > 0) {
              badge.textContent = res.totalUsulanBaru;
              badge.style.display = 'inline-block';
            } else {
              badge.style.display = 'none';
            }
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(() => {})
        .getUsulanNotifikasiSummary(SESSION.token);
    }
  }
}

function hitungProyeksiGolonganBaru(gol) {
  if (!gol) return '';
  const str = String(gol).trim();

  // Standar hirarki pangkat/golongan PNS & Non-ASN:
  const ranks = [
    'I/a', 'I/b', 'I/c', 'I/d',
    'II/a', 'II/b', 'II/c', 'II/d',
    'III/a', 'III/b', 'III/c', 'III/d',
    'IV/a', 'IV/b', 'IV/c', 'IV/d', 'IV/e'
  ];

  const match = str.match(/(I|II|III|IV)\/([a-e])/i);
  if (match) {
    const cleanRank = match[1].toUpperCase() + '/' + match[2].toLowerCase();
    const idx = ranks.indexOf(cleanRank);
    if (idx !== -1 && idx < ranks.length - 1) {
      const nextRank = ranks[idx + 1];
      if (str.length > 8) {
        return str.replace(match[0], nextRank);
      }
      return nextRank;
    }
    if (idx === ranks.length - 1) return cleanRank;
  }
  return str;
}

function recalculateGolBaruProjection() {
  const inputGolLama = document.getElementById('umInputGolLama');
  const inputGolBaru = document.getElementById('umInputGolBaru');
  if (!inputGolLama || !inputGolBaru) return;
  const golVal = inputGolLama.value.trim();
  if (golVal) {
    const projected = hitungProyeksiGolonganBaru(golVal);
    inputGolBaru.value = projected;

    const wrap = document.getElementById('umProyeksiRankWrap');
    const elLama = document.getElementById('umProyeksiGolLamaText');
    const elBaru = document.getElementById('umProyeksiGolBaruText');
    if (wrap && elLama && elBaru) {
      wrap.style.display = 'block';
      elLama.textContent = golVal;
      elBaru.textContent = projected;
    }
  }
}

function loadUsulanMasukDashboard() {
  loadUsulanMasukTmtDashboard();
}

let umGlobalCache = null;

async function loadUsulanMasukTmtDashboard(forceRefresh = false) {
  const container = document.getElementById('usulanMasukTmtContainer');
  if (container && (!umGlobalCache || forceRefresh)) {
    container.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Memuat dashboard dan data usulan TMT...</div>';
  }

  const inputNama = document.getElementById('umInputNama');
  const listNama = document.getElementById('umInputNama_list');
  const hiddenNip = document.getElementById('umInputNip');
  if (inputNama && listNama && hiddenNip && !inputNama.dataset.acBound) {
    inputNama.dataset.acBound = 'true';
    setupAutocompleteEl(inputNama, listNama, hiddenNip, async (nip, nama, item) => {
      const inputUnit = document.getElementById('umInputUnit');
      const inputJabatan = document.getElementById('umInputJabatan');
      const inputGolLama = document.getElementById('umInputGolLama');
      const inputGolBaru = document.getElementById('umInputGolBaru');
      const inputJenis = document.getElementById('umInputJenisPegawai');
      const inputStatus = document.getElementById('umInputStatusKepegawaian');

      let emp = item || {};
      if (nip && (!emp.unit_es_ii || !emp.golongan)) {
        try {
          const res = await rpc('getEmployeeFullData', [SESSION.token, nip]);
          if (res && res.nip) emp = Object.assign({}, emp, res);
        } catch (e) {
          // ignore error
        }
      }

      const unitVal = emp.unit_es_ii || emp.unit_kerja || emp.unit || '';
      if (inputUnit && unitVal) inputUnit.value = unitVal;

      const jabVal = emp.jabatan || emp.jabatan_struktural || emp.jabatan_fungsional || '';
      if (inputJabatan && jabVal) inputJabatan.value = jabVal;

      const golVal = emp.golongan || emp.gol || '';
      if (inputGolLama && golVal) inputGolLama.value = golVal;

      if (inputJenis && emp.jenis_peg) {
        const j = String(emp.jenis_peg).toLowerCase();
        if (j.includes('dosen')) inputJenis.value = 'Dosen';
        else if (j.includes('tendik')) inputJenis.value = 'Tendik';
      }

      if (inputStatus && (emp.status_pegawai || emp.status_kepegawaian)) {
        const st = String(emp.status_pegawai || emp.status_kepegawaian).toUpperCase();
        if (st.includes('PNS')) inputStatus.value = 'PNS';
        else if (st.includes('PPPK')) inputStatus.value = 'PPPK';
        else if (st.includes('NON-PNS') || st.includes('NON PNS')) inputStatus.value = 'Tetap Non-PNS';
        else if (st.includes('KONTRAK')) inputStatus.value = 'Kontrak';
      }

      if (golVal) {
        const projected = hitungProyeksiGolonganBaru(golVal);
        if (inputGolBaru) inputGolBaru.value = projected;

        const wrap = document.getElementById('umProyeksiRankWrap');
        const elLama = document.getElementById('umProyeksiGolLamaText');
        const elBaru = document.getElementById('umProyeksiGolBaruText');
        if (wrap && elLama && elBaru) {
          wrap.style.display = 'block';
          elLama.textContent = golVal;
          elBaru.textContent = projected;
        }
      }
    });
  }

  loadUsulanMasukBadge();

  if (umGlobalCache && !forceRefresh) {
    applyUsulanMasukDashboardData(umGlobalCache, container);
  }

  try {
    const res = await rpc('getUsulanMasukTmtGrouped', [SESSION.token]);
    if (res && res.success && res.list && res.list.length > 0) {
      umGlobalCache = res;
      applyUsulanMasukDashboardData(res, container);
      if (forceRefresh) loadPromosiDashboard(currentSelectedTmt);
    } else if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(gasRes => {
          if (gasRes && gasRes.success && gasRes.list && gasRes.list.length > 0) {
            umGlobalCache = gasRes;
            applyUsulanMasukDashboardData(gasRes, container);
          } else {
            umGlobalCache = res || gasRes;
            applyUsulanMasukDashboardData(res || gasRes || { success: true, total: 0, list: [] }, container);
          }
          if (forceRefresh) loadPromosiDashboard(currentSelectedTmt);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(() => {
          applyUsulanMasukDashboardData(res || { success: true, total: 0, list: [] }, container);
          if (forceRefresh) loadPromosiDashboard(currentSelectedTmt);
        })
        .getUsulanMasukTmtGrouped(SESSION.token);
    } else {
      applyUsulanMasukDashboardData(res || { success: true, total: 0, list: [] }, container);
      if (forceRefresh) loadPromosiDashboard(currentSelectedTmt);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(res => {
          umGlobalCache = res;
          applyUsulanMasukDashboardData(res, container);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => {
          showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat dashboard usulan: ' + (e.message || e));
          if (container) container.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Gagal memuat data dari server.</div>';
        })
        .getUsulanMasukTmtGrouped(SESSION.token);
    } else if (!umGlobalCache && container) {
      container.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Gagal memuat data dari server.</div>';
    }
  }
}

function applyUsulanMasukDashboardData(res, container) {
  const sc = res.statusCounts || {};
  const dsdm = sc['Diterima DSDM'] || 0;
  const siasn = sc['Diajukan SIASN'] || 0;
  const pertek = sc['TTD Pertek'] || 0;
  const sk = sc['SK Berhasil'] || 0;
  const perbaikan = sc['Perbaikan'] || 0;
  const belumEligible = sc['Belum Eligible'] || 0;
  const pensiun = sc['Pensiun'] || 0;
  const total = res.total || 0;

  const elDsdm = document.getElementById('statCountDSDM');
  const elSiasn = document.getElementById('statCountSIASN');
  const elPertek = document.getElementById('statCountPertek');
  const elSk = document.getElementById('statCountSK');
  const elPerbaikan = document.getElementById('statCountPerbaikan');
  const elBelum = document.getElementById('statCountBelumEligible');
  const elPensiun = document.getElementById('statCountPensiun');
  const elTotal = document.getElementById('statCountTotal');

  if (elDsdm) elDsdm.textContent = dsdm;
  if (elSiasn) elSiasn.textContent = siasn;
  if (elPertek) elPertek.textContent = pertek;
  if (elSk) elSk.textContent = sk;
  if (elPerbaikan) elPerbaikan.textContent = perbaikan;
  if (elBelum) elBelum.textContent = belumEligible;
  if (elPensiun) elPensiun.textContent = pensiun;
  if (elTotal) elTotal.textContent = total;

  renderUsulanPieChart(dsdm, siasn, pertek, sk, perbaikan, belumEligible, pensiun);

  const fullList = res.list || [];
  window.umGlobalList = fullList;
  window.umMasterRawList = fullList;
  umMasterRawList = fullList;

  // Populate Year Select Options
  const yearSelect = document.getElementById('umYearFilterSelect');
  if (yearSelect) {
    const availableYearsSet = new Set();
    fullList.forEach(item => {
      const parsed = parseTmt(item.tmt);
      if (parsed && parsed.year && parsed.year !== 'Tanpa TMT' && parsed.year !== 'Lainnya') {
        availableYearsSet.add(parsed.year);
      }
    });
    const sortedAvailableYears = Array.from(availableYearsSet).sort((a,b) => b.localeCompare(a));
    const currentSelectedVal = yearSelect.value || 'ALL';
    let newHtml = `<option value="ALL" ${currentSelectedVal === 'ALL' ? 'selected' : ''}>Semua Tahun</option>`;
    sortedAvailableYears.forEach(yr => {
      newHtml += `<option value="${yr}" ${yr === currentSelectedVal ? 'selected' : ''}>Tahun ${yr}</option>`;
    });
    yearSelect.innerHTML = newHtml;
  }

  // Populate Unit Select Options
  const unitSelect = document.getElementById('umUnitFilterSelect');
  if (unitSelect) {
    const unitsSet = new Set();
    fullList.forEach(item => {
      if (item.unit && item.unit.trim()) {
        unitsSet.add(item.unit.trim());
      }
    });
    const sortedUnits = Array.from(unitsSet).sort();
    const currentUnitVal = unitSelect.value || 'ALL';
    let unitHtml = `<option value="ALL" ${currentUnitVal === 'ALL' ? 'selected' : ''}>Semua Unit</option>`;
    sortedUnits.forEach(u => {
      unitHtml += `<option value="${escapeHtml(u)}" ${u === currentUnitVal ? 'selected' : ''}>${escapeHtml(u)}</option>`;
    });
    unitSelect.innerHTML = unitHtml;
  }

  if (!fullList.length) {
    if (container) container.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Belum ada usulan masuk. Silakan gunakan form di atas untuk menginput usulan baru atau import dari Excel/Spreadsheet.</div>';
    return;
  }

  if (typeof onUmYearFilterChange === 'function') {
    onUmYearFilterChange();
  } else {
    renderGroupedTmtTables(fullList, container);
  }
}

async function ensureChartJsLoaded() {
  if (typeof Chart !== 'undefined') return true;
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    const existing = document.querySelector('script[src*="chart.js"]');
    if (existing) {
      existing.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(typeof Chart !== 'undefined'));
      existing.addEventListener('error', () => resolve(false));
      setTimeout(() => resolve(typeof Chart !== 'undefined'), 1500);
      return;
    }
    const script = document.createElement('script');
    script.src = 'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js';
    script.onload = () => resolve(typeof Chart !== 'undefined');
    script.onerror = () => resolve(false);
    document.head.appendChild(script);
  });
}

async function renderUsulanPieChart(dsdm, siasn, pertek, sk, perbaikan, belumEligible, pensiun) {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('usulanStatusPieChart');
  if (!canvas) return;

  if (typeof Chart === 'undefined') {
    const isLoaded = await ensureChartJsLoaded();
    if (!isLoaded || typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
  }

  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  if (usulanPieChartInstance) {
    usulanPieChartInstance.destroy();
  }

  const labels = ['Diterima DSDM', 'Diajukan SIASN', 'TTD Pertek', 'SK Berhasil', 'Perbaikan', 'Belum Eligible', 'Pensiun'];
  const data = [dsdm || 0, siasn || 0, pertek || 0, sk || 0, perbaikan || 0, belumEligible || 0, pensiun || 0];
  const colors = ['#3b82f6', '#f59e0b', '#8b5cf6', '#10b981', '#f97316', '#ef4444', '#06b6d4'];

  usulanPieChartInstance = new Chart(ctx, {
    type: 'pie',
    data: {
      labels: labels,
      datasets: [{
        data: data,
        backgroundColor: colors,
        borderWidth: 2,
        borderColor: '#ffffff'
      }]
    },
    options: {
      responsive: true,
      maintainAspectRatio: false,
      onClick: (evt, activeElements) => {
        if (activeElements && activeElements.length > 0) {
          const index = activeElements[0].index;
          const clickedLabel = labels[index];
          openStatusDetailModal(clickedLabel);
        }
      },
      plugins: {
        legend: {
          position: 'bottom',
          labels: { font: { size: 11 }, boxWidth: 12 }
        },
        tooltip: {
          callbacks: {
            label: function(context) {
              const label = context.label || '';
              const value = context.parsed || 0;
              const total = context.dataset.data.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
              const percentage = total > 0 ? ((value / total) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '0%';
              return `${label}: ${value} usulan (${percentage})`;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  });
}

/* ---------- POP-UP MODAL STATUS DETAIL & DIRECT UPDATE LOGIC ---------- */
window.umGlobalList = [];
window.umCurrentModalStatus = 'ALL';

function openStatusDetailModal(targetStatus) {
  window.umCurrentModalStatus = targetStatus;
  const modal = document.getElementById('umStatusDetailModal');
  const title = document.getElementById('umModalTitle');
  const searchInput = document.getElementById('umModalSearchInput');

  if (searchInput) searchInput.value = '';

  if (title) {
    title.textContent = targetStatus === 'ALL' ? '📋 Seluruh Daftar Usulan Masuk' : `📋 Daftar Usulan Status: ${targetStatus}`;
  }

  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'block';
  renderStatusDetailModalContent();
}

function closeStatusDetailModal() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('umStatusDetailModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
}

function filterStatusDetailModalRows() {
  renderStatusDetailModalContent();
}

function renderStatusDetailModalContent() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('umModalTbody');
  const countEl = document.getElementById('umModalCount');
  const searchInput = document.getElementById('umModalSearchInput');
  const q = searchInput ? searchInput.value.toLowerCase().trim() : '';

  if (!tbody) return;

  const targetStatus = window.umCurrentModalStatus;
  let filtered = (window.umGlobalList || []).filter(u => {
    const st = String(u.status || 'Diterima DSDM').trim();
    if (targetStatus === 'ALL') return true;
    if (targetStatus === 'Diajukan SIASN') return st === 'Diajukan SIASN' || st === 'Diajukan ke SIASN';
    if (targetStatus === 'Belum Eligible') return st === 'Belum Eligible' || st === 'Tidak Eligible';
    return st.toLowerCase() === targetStatus.toLowerCase();
  });

  if (q) {
    filtered = filtered.filter(u => 
      String(u.nama || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      String(u.nip || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      String(u.unit || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      String(u.jabatan || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      String(u.tmt || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }

  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = filtered.length;

  if (!filtered.length) {
    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align:center; padding:18px;">Tidak ada pegawai dengan status "${targetStatus}" ${q ? 'yang cocok dengan pencarian' : ''}.td></tr>`;
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML = filtered.map((u, idx) => {
    const currentStatus = String(u.status || 'Diterima DSDM').trim();
    let badgeClass = 'dsdm';
    if (currentStatus === 'Diajukan SIASN' || currentStatus === 'Diajukan ke SIASN') badgeClass = 'siasn';
    else if (currentStatus === 'TTD Pertek') badgeClass = 'pertek';
    else if (currentStatus === 'SK Berhasil') badgeClass = 'sk';
    else if (currentStatus === 'Perbaikan') badgeClass = 'perbaikan';
    else if (currentStatus === 'Belum Eligible' || currentStatus === 'Tidak Eligible') badgeClass = 'belumbisa';
    else if (currentStatus === 'Pensiun') badgeClass = 'pensiun';

    const golLamaStr = u.golongan_lama || u.golLama || '-';
    const golBaruStr = u.golongan_baru || u.golBaru || (u.golongan_lama ? hitungProyeksiGolonganBaru(u.golongan_lama) : '-');

    return `
      <tr>
        <td>${idx + 1}</td>
        <td>
          <strong style="font-size:14px;">${escapeHtml(u.nama)}</strong>
          ${u.nama_pengaju ? `<div style="font-size:11.5px; color:var(--text-muted);">Diajukan oleh: ${escapeHtml(u.nama_pengaju)}</div>` : ''}
        </td>
        <td>
          <div>${u.nip ? `NIP ${escapeHtml(u.nip)}` : '<span style="color:var(--text-muted); font-style:italic;">-</span>'}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11.5px; color:var(--text-muted);">${escapeHtml(u.unit || '-')}</div>
          ${u.jabatan ? `<div style="font-size:11px; color:var(--text-muted); font-style:italic;">${escapeHtml(u.jabatan)}</div>` : ''}
        </td>
        <td>
          <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:6px;">
            <span class="cdli-badge" style="background:var(--surface-alt); color:var(--text-main); border:1px solid var(--border);">${escapeHtml(golLamaStr)}</span>
            <span style="font-weight:800; color:#059669;">➔</span>
            <span class="cdli-badge" style="background:rgba(16,185,129,0.15); color:#059669; border:1px solid #10b981;">${escapeHtml(golBaruStr)}</span>
          </div>
        </td>
        <td><strong>${escapeHtml(u.tmt || '-')}</strong></td>
        <td><span class="badge-status ${badgeClass}">${escapeHtml(currentStatus)}</span></td>
        <td>
          <select class="status-select" onchange="updateStatusFromModal('${u.id}', this.value)">
            <option value="Diterima DSDM" ${currentStatus === 'Diterima DSDM' ? 'selected' : ''}>Diterima DSDM</option>
            <option value="Diajukan SIASN" ${currentStatus === 'Diajukan SIASN' || currentStatus === 'Diajukan ke SIASN' ? 'selected' : ''}>Diajukan SIASN</option>
            <option value="TTD Pertek" ${currentStatus === 'TTD Pertek' ? 'selected' : ''}>TTD Pertek</option>
            <option value="SK Berhasil" ${currentStatus === 'SK Berhasil' ? 'selected' : ''}>SK Berhasil</option>
            <option value="Perbaikan" ${currentStatus === 'Perbaikan' ? 'selected' : ''}>Perbaikan</option>
            <option value="Belum Eligible" ${currentStatus === 'Belum Eligible' || currentStatus === 'Tidak Eligible' ? 'selected' : ''}>Belum Eligible</option>
            <option value="Pensiun" ${currentStatus === 'Pensiun' ? 'selected' : ''}>Pensiun</option>
          </select>
        </td>
      </tr>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

async function updateStatusFromModal(id, newStatus) {
  if (!id || !newStatus) return;

  const item = (window.umGlobalList || []).find(x => String(x.id) === String(id));
  const oldStatus = item ? item.status : '';
  if (item) item.status = newStatus;

  const masterItem = (window.umMasterRawList || []).find(x => String(x.id) === String(id));
  if (masterItem) masterItem.status = newStatus;

  showToast('info', 'Memperbarui status usulan...');

  const handleSuccess = (res) => {
    if (!res || !res.success) {
      if (item) item.status = oldStatus;
      if (masterItem) masterItem.status = oldStatus;
      showToast('error', (res && res.message) || 'Gagal memperbarui status.');
      renderStatusDetailModalContent();
      return;
    }
    showToast('success', `Status usulan diperbarui menjadi "${newStatus}".`);
    loadUsulanMasukTmtDashboard();
    setTimeout(() => {
      renderStatusDetailModalContent();
    }, 350);
  };

  const handleError = (err) => {
    if (item) item.status = oldStatus;
    if (masterItem) masterItem.status = oldStatus;
    showToast('error', 'Gagal memperbarui status: ' + (err?.message || err));
    renderStatusDetailModalContent();
  };

  try {
    const res = await rpc('updateUsulanMasukStatus', [SESSION.token, { id, status: newStatus }]);
    if (res && res.success) {
      handleSuccess(res);
      return;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[updateStatusFromModal] rpc catch:', err);
  }

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(handleSuccess)
      .withFailureHandler(handleError)
      .updateUsulanMasukStatus(SESSION.token, { id, status: newStatus });
  } else {
    handleError('Koneksi ke server gagal.');
  }
}

function parseTmt(tmtStr) {
  if (!tmtStr) return { year: 'Tanpa TMT', month: 'Lainnya', full: 'Tanpa TMT' };
  const str = String(tmtStr).trim();
  
  const monthsIndo = ['Januari','Februari','Maret','April','Mei','Juni','Juli','Agustus','September','Oktober','November','Desember'];
  for (let m of monthsIndo) {
    if (str.toLowerCase().includes(m.toLowerCase())) {
      const matchYear = str.match(/\b(20\d{2})\b/);
      const year = matchYear ? matchYear[1] : 'Lainnya';
      return { year, month: `${m} ${year}`, full: str };
    }
  }

  const matchIso = str.match(/^(20\d{2})[-/](\d{1,2})/);
  if (matchIso) {
    const year = matchIso[1];
    const mIdx = parseInt(matchIso[2], 10) - 1;
    const monthName = (mIdx >= 0 && mIdx < 12) ? monthsIndo[mIdx] : 'Bulan ' + matchIso[2];
    return { year, month: `${monthName} ${year}`, full: `${monthName} ${year}` };
  }

  const matchAnyYear = str.match(/\b(20\d{2})\b/);
  if (matchAnyYear) {
    return { year: matchAnyYear[1], month: str, full: str };
  }

  return { year: 'Lainnya', month: str, full: str };
}

/* ---------- ACCORDION TOGGLE & YEAR FILTER LOGIC ---------- */
function toggleTmtAccordion(elementId) {
  const el = document.getElementById(elementId);
  if (!el) return;
  const isHidden = el.style.display === 'none';
  el.style.display = isHidden ? 'block' : 'none';
  
  const iconEl = document.getElementById(elementId + '_icon');
  if (iconEl) {
    iconEl.textContent = isHidden ? '▲' : '▼';
  }
}

let umMasterRawList = [];
let umCurrentGroupMode = 'by_tmt';

function setUsulanGroupMode(mode) {
  umCurrentGroupMode = mode;
  const btnTmt = document.getElementById('umGroupBtnTmt');
  const btnUnit = document.getElementById('umGroupBtnUnit');
  const btnJenis = document.getElementById('umGroupBtnJenis');
  const headingText = document.getElementById('umGroupHeadingText');

  if (btnTmt) btnTmt.className = mode === 'by_tmt' ? 'btn btn-primary kp-small-btn' : 'btn btn-outline kp-small-btn';
  if (btnUnit) btnUnit.className = mode === 'by_unit' ? 'btn btn-primary kp-small-btn' : 'btn btn-outline kp-small-btn';
  if (btnJenis) btnJenis.className = mode === 'by_jenis_status' ? 'btn btn-primary kp-small-btn' : 'btn btn-outline kp-small-btn';

  if (headingText) {
    if (mode === 'by_unit') headingText.textContent = '🏢 Daftar Usulan Masuk (Dikelompokkan Berdasarkan Unit / Fakultas)';
    else if (mode === 'by_jenis_status') headingText.textContent = '👥 Daftar Usulan Masuk (Dikelompokkan Berdasarkan Jenis & Status Pegawai)';
    else headingText.textContent = '📅 Daftar Usulan Masuk (Dikelompokkan Berdasarkan TMT)';
  }

  const container = document.getElementById('usulanMasukTmtContainer');
  if (container && window.umGlobalList) {
    renderGroupedTmtTables(window.umGlobalList, container, umCurrentGroupMode);
  }
}

function onUmYearFilterChange() {
  const masterList = (umMasterRawList && umMasterRawList.length) ? umMasterRawList : (window.umMasterRawList || []);
  if (!masterList) return;

  const yearSelect = document.getElementById('umYearFilterSelect');
  const jenisSelect = document.getElementById('umJenisFilterSelect');
  const statusKepSelect = document.getElementById('umStatusKepFilterSelect');
  const unitSelect = document.getElementById('umUnitFilterSelect');

  const selYear = yearSelect ? yearSelect.value : 'ALL';
  const selJenis = jenisSelect ? jenisSelect.value : 'ALL';
  const selStatusKep = statusKepSelect ? statusKepSelect.value : 'ALL';
  const selUnit = unitSelect ? unitSelect.value : 'ALL';

  let filteredList = masterList;

  if (selYear !== 'ALL') {
    filteredList = filteredList.filter(item => {
      const parsed = parseTmt(item.tmt);
      return String(parsed.year) === String(selYear);
    });
  }

  if (selJenis !== 'ALL') {
    filteredList = filteredList.filter(item => {
      const j = String(item.jenis_pegawai || item.jenisPegawai || 'Tendik').trim().toLowerCase();
      return j === selJenis.toLowerCase();
    });
  }

  if (selStatusKep !== 'ALL') {
    filteredList = filteredList.filter(item => {
      const sk = String(item.status_kepegawaian || item.statusKepegawaian || 'PNS').trim().toLowerCase();
      return sk === selStatusKep.toLowerCase();
    });
  }

  if (selUnit !== 'ALL') {
    filteredList = filteredList.filter(item => {
      const u = String(item.unit || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      return u === selUnit.toLowerCase();
    });
  }

  window.umGlobalList = filteredList;

  const sc = {
    'Diterima DSDM': 0, 'Diajukan SIASN': 0, 'TTD Pertek': 0,
    'SK Berhasil': 0, 'Perbaikan': 0, 'Belum Eligible': 0, 'Pensiun': 0
  };

  filteredList.forEach(u => {
    const st = String(u.status || '').trim();
    if (st === 'Diterima DSDM') sc['Diterima DSDM']++;
    else if (st === 'Diajukan SIASN' || st === 'Diajukan ke SIASN') sc['Diajukan SIASN']++;
    else if (st === 'TTD Pertek') sc['TTD Pertek']++;
    else if (st === 'SK Berhasil') sc['SK Berhasil']++;
    else if (st === 'Perbaikan') sc['Perbaikan']++;
    else if (st === 'Belum Eligible' || st === 'Tidak Eligible') sc['Belum Eligible']++;
    else if (st === 'Pensiun') sc['Pensiun']++;
  });

  const dsdm = sc['Diterima DSDM'];
  const siasn = sc['Diajukan SIASN'];
  const pertek = sc['TTD Pertek'];
  const sk = sc['SK Berhasil'];
  const perbaikan = sc['Perbaikan'];
  const belumEligible = sc['Belum Eligible'];
  const pensiun = sc['Pensiun'];
  const total = filteredList.length;

  const elDsdm = document.getElementById('statCountDSDM');
  const elSiasn = document.getElementById('statCountSIASN');
  const elPertek = document.getElementById('statCountPertek');
  const elSk = document.getElementById('statCountSK');
  const elPerbaikan = document.getElementById('statCountPerbaikan');
  const elBelum = document.getElementById('statCountBelumEligible');
  const elPensiun = document.getElementById('statCountPensiun');
  const elTotal = document.getElementById('statCountTotal');

  if (elDsdm) elDsdm.textContent = dsdm;
  if (elSiasn) elSiasn.textContent = siasn;
  if (elPertek) elPertek.textContent = pertek;
  if (elSk) elSk.textContent = sk;
  if (elPerbaikan) elPerbaikan.textContent = perbaikan;
  if (elBelum) elBelum.textContent = belumEligible;
  if (elPensiun) elPensiun.textContent = pensiun;
  if (elTotal) elTotal.textContent = total;

  renderUsulanPieChart(dsdm, siasn, pertek, sk, perbaikan, belumEligible, pensiun);

  const container = document.getElementById('usulanMasukTmtContainer');
  if (!filteredList.length) {
    if (container) container.innerHTML = `<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Belum ada usulan masuk untuk kriteria filter yang dipilih. Silakan sesuaikan filter di atas.</div>`;
    return;
  }

  if (container) renderGroupedTmtTables(filteredList, container, umCurrentGroupMode);
}



function renderGroupedTmtTables(list, container, mode = 'by_tmt') {
  if (!list || !list.length) {
    container.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Tidak ada data usulan untuk ditampilkan.</div>';
    return;
  }

  let html = '';

  if (mode === 'by_unit') {
    const groupedByUnit = {};
    list.forEach(item => {
      const u = item.unit || 'Lainnya / Tanpa Unit';
      if (!groupedByUnit[u]) groupedByUnit[u] = [];
      groupedByUnit[u].push(item);
    });

    const units = Object.keys(groupedByUnit).sort();

    units.forEach((uName, uIdx) => {
      const items = groupedByUnit[uName];
      const uBodyId = `tmt_unit_body_${uIdx}`;

      html += `
        <div class="tmt-year-card">
          <div class="tmt-year-header" onclick="toggleTmtAccordion('${uBodyId}')" style="cursor:pointer; user-select:none; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
            <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px;">
              <span id="${uBodyId}_icon" style="font-size:14px; color:var(--primary); font-weight:bold;">▼</span>
              <span style="font-size:15px; font-weight:700;">🏢 ${escapeHtml(uName)}</span>
            </div>
            <span class="cdli-badge" style="background:var(--primary); color:#ffffff !important; font-size:12px; font-weight:700; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:999px;">${items.length} Usulan</span>
          </div>
          <div id="${uBodyId}" class="tmt-year-body" style="display:none; overflow-x:auto; margin-top:8px;">
            ${buildUsulanTableHtml(items)}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    });
  } else if (mode === 'by_jenis_status') {
    const groupedByJenis = {};
    list.forEach(item => {
      const j = item.jenis_pegawai || item.jenisPegawai || 'Tendik';
      const sk = item.status_kepegawaian || item.statusKepegawaian || 'PNS';
      const key = `${j} - ${sk}`;
      if (!groupedByJenis[key]) groupedByJenis[key] = [];
      groupedByJenis[key].push(item);
    });

    const keys = Object.keys(groupedByJenis).sort();

    keys.forEach((keyName, kIdx) => {
      const items = groupedByJenis[keyName];
      const kBodyId = `tmt_jenis_body_${kIdx}`;

      html += `
        <div class="tmt-year-card">
          <div class="tmt-year-header" onclick="toggleTmtAccordion('${kBodyId}')" style="cursor:pointer; user-select:none; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
            <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px;">
              <span id="${kBodyId}_icon" style="font-size:14px; color:var(--primary); font-weight:bold;">▼</span>
              <span style="font-size:15px; font-weight:700;">👥 Kelompok Pegawai: ${escapeHtml(keyName)}</span>
            </div>
            <span class="cdli-badge" style="background:var(--primary); color:#ffffff !important; font-size:12px; font-weight:700; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:999px;">${items.length} Usulan</span>
          </div>
          <div id="${kBodyId}" class="tmt-year-body" style="display:none; overflow-x:auto; margin-top:8px;">
            ${buildUsulanTableHtml(items)}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    });
  } else {
    // Mode by_tmt (Default: Year -> Month)
    const grouped = {};

    list.forEach(item => {
      const parsed = parseTmt(item.tmt);
      const yr = parsed.year;
      const mn = parsed.month;
      if (!grouped[yr]) grouped[yr] = {};
      if (!grouped[yr][mn]) grouped[yr][mn] = [];
      grouped[yr][mn].push(item);
    });

    const years = Object.keys(grouped).sort((a, b) => {
      if (a === 'Tanpa TMT' || a === 'Lainnya') return 1;
      if (b === 'Tanpa TMT' || b === 'Lainnya') return -1;
      return b.localeCompare(a);
    });

    years.forEach((yr, yrIdx) => {
      const monthsObj = grouped[yr];
      const monthNames = Object.keys(monthsObj);
      const yrBodyId = `tmt_yr_body_${yrIdx}`;
      
      html += `
        <div class="tmt-year-card">
          <div class="tmt-year-header" onclick="toggleTmtAccordion('${yrBodyId}')" style="cursor:pointer; user-select:none; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
            <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px;">
              <span id="${yrBodyId}_icon" style="font-size:14px; color:var(--primary); font-weight:bold;">▼</span>
              <span style="font-size:15px; font-weight:700;">🗓️ TMT Tahun ${escapeHtml(yr)}</span>
            </div>
            <span class="cdli-badge" style="background:var(--primary); color:#ffffff !important; font-size:12px; font-weight:700; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:999px;">${monthNames.length} Periode TMT</span>
          </div>
          <div id="${yrBodyId}" class="tmt-year-body" style="display:none;">
      `;

      monthNames.forEach((mn, mnIdx) => {
        const items = monthsObj[mn] || [];
        const mnBodyId = `tmt_mn_body_${yrIdx}_${mnIdx}`;
        
        html += `
          <div class="tmt-month-card">
            <div class="tmt-month-title" onclick="toggleTmtAccordion('${mnBodyId}')" style="cursor:pointer; user-select:none; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; padding:4px 0;">
              <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px;">
                <span id="${mnBodyId}_icon" style="font-size:12px; color:var(--primary); font-weight:bold;">▼</span>
                <span style="font-size:14px; font-weight:700; color:var(--primary);">📌 TMT ${escapeHtml(mn)}</span>
              </div>
              <span class="cdli-badge" style="background:var(--surface-alt); color:var(--text-main) !important; border:1px solid var(--border); font-size:11.5px; font-weight:600; padding:2px 8px; border-radius:999px;">(${items.length} usulan)</span>
            </div>
            <div id="${mnBodyId}" class="tmt-month-body" style="display:none; overflow-x:auto; margin-top:8px;">
              ${buildUsulanTableHtml(items)}
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      });

      html += `
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    });
  }

  container.innerHTML = html;
}

function buildUsulanTableHtml(items) {
  if (!items || !items.length) return '<div style="padding:12px; font-style:italic;">Tidak ada data usulan.</div>';

  let rowsHtml = items.map((u, idx) => {
    const currentStatus = String(u.status || 'Diterima DSDM').trim();
    let badgeClass = 'dsdm';
    if (currentStatus === 'Diajukan SIASN' || currentStatus === 'Diajukan ke SIASN') badgeClass = 'siasn';
    else if (currentStatus === 'TTD Pertek') badgeClass = 'pertek';
    else if (currentStatus === 'SK Berhasil') badgeClass = 'sk';
    else if (currentStatus === 'Perbaikan') badgeClass = 'perbaikan';
    else if (currentStatus === 'Belum Eligible' || currentStatus === 'Tidak Eligible') badgeClass = 'belumbisa';
    else if (currentStatus === 'Pensiun') badgeClass = 'pensiun';

    const golLamaStr = u.golongan_lama || u.golLama || '-';
    const golBaruStr = u.golongan_baru || u.golBaru || (u.golongan_lama ? hitungProyeksiGolonganBaru(u.golongan_lama) : '-');
    const jenisPeg = u.jenis_pegawai || u.jenisPegawai || 'Tendik';
    const statusKep = u.status_kepegawaian || u.statusKepegawaian || 'PNS';

    return `
      <tr>
        <td>${idx + 1}</td>
        <td>
          <strong style="font-size:14px;">${escapeHtml(u.nama)}</strong>
          ${u.nama_pengaju ? `<div style="font-size:11.5px; color:var(--text-muted);">Diajukan oleh: ${escapeHtml(u.nama_pengaju)}</div>` : ''}
        </td>
        <td>
          <div>${u.nip ? `NIP ${escapeHtml(u.nip)}` : '<span style="color:var(--text-muted); font-style:italic;">-</span>'}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11.5px; color:var(--primary); font-weight:600;">🏢 ${escapeHtml(u.unit || '-')}</div>
          ${u.jabatan ? `<div style="font-size:11px; color:var(--text-muted); font-style:italic;">${escapeHtml(u.jabatan)}</div>` : ''}
        </td>
        <td>
          <span class="cdli-badge" style="background:var(--primary); color:#fff; font-size:11px;">${escapeHtml(jenisPeg)}</span>
          <span class="cdli-badge" style="background:var(--surface-alt); border:1px solid var(--border); font-size:11px; margin-top:2px; display:inline-block;">${escapeHtml(statusKep)}</span>
        </td>
        <td>
          <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:6px;">
            <span class="cdli-badge" style="background:var(--surface-alt); color:var(--text-main); border:1px solid var(--border);">${escapeHtml(golLamaStr)}</span>
            <span style="font-weight:800; color:#059669;">➔</span>
            <span class="cdli-badge" style="background:rgba(16,185,129,0.15); color:#059669; border:1px solid #10b981;">${escapeHtml(golBaruStr)}</span>
          </div>
        </td>
        <td>
          <strong>${escapeHtml(u.tmt || '-')}</strong>
        </td>
        <td>
          <span class="badge-status ${badgeClass}">${escapeHtml(currentStatus)}</span>
        </td>
        <td>
          <select class="status-select" onchange="updateUsulanStatusInline('${u.id}', this.value)">
            <option value="Diterima DSDM" ${currentStatus === 'Diterima DSDM' ? 'selected' : ''}>Diterima DSDM</option>
            <option value="Diajukan SIASN" ${currentStatus === 'Diajukan SIASN' || currentStatus === 'Diajukan ke SIASN' ? 'selected' : ''}>Diajukan SIASN</option>
            <option value="TTD Pertek" ${currentStatus === 'TTD Pertek' ? 'selected' : ''}>TTD Pertek</option>
            <option value="SK Berhasil" ${currentStatus === 'SK Berhasil' ? 'selected' : ''}>SK Berhasil</option>
            <option value="Perbaikan" ${currentStatus === 'Perbaikan' ? 'selected' : ''}>Perbaikan</option>
            <option value="Belum Eligible" ${currentStatus === 'Belum Eligible' || currentStatus === 'Tidak Eligible' ? 'selected' : ''}>Belum Eligible</option>
            <option value="Pensiun" ${currentStatus === 'Pensiun' ? 'selected' : ''}>Pensiun</option>
          </select>
        </td>
        <td style="text-align:center;">
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline kp-small-btn" onclick="deleteUsulanMasukInline('${u.id}')" style="color:var(--danger); border-color:var(--danger); padding:3px 8px;" title="Hapus Usulan">🗑️</button>
        </td>
      </tr>
    `;
  }).join('');

  return `
    <table class="tmt-table">
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th style="width:40px;">No</th>
          <th>Nama Pegawai</th>
          <th>NIP / Unit / Jabatan</th>
          <th>Jenis &amp; Status</th>
          <th>Proyeksi Pangkat/Golongan</th>
          <th>TMT</th>
          <th>Status Saat Ini</th>
          <th>Ubah Status</th>
          <th style="width:70px; text-align:center;">Aksi</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        ${rowsHtml}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  `;
}

/* ---------- IMPORT SPREADSHEET & EXCEL MULTI-SHEET LOGIC ---------- */
let umImportWorkbook = null;
let umParsedImportRows = [];

function openUmImportModal() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('umImportModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'flex';
}

function closeUmImportModal() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('umImportModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
}

function ensureXlsxLoaded(callback) {
  if (typeof XLSX !== 'undefined') {
    callback();
    return;
  }
  showToast('info', 'Memuat modul pembaca Spreadsheet (XLSX)...');
  const script = document.createElement('script');
  script.src = 'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/xlsx@0.18.5/dist/xlsx.full.min.js';
  script.onload = () => {
    callback();
  };
  script.onerror = () => {
    showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat pustaka XLSX. Silakan periksa koneksi internet Anda.');
  };
  document.head.appendChild(script);
}

function handleExcelFileUpload(e) {
  const file = e.target.files[0];
  if (!file) return;

  ensureXlsxLoaded(() => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = function(evt) {
      try {
        const data = new Uint8Array(evt.target.result);
        umImportWorkbook = XLSX.read(data, { type: 'array' });
        populateImportSheetSelect(umImportWorkbook.SheetNames);
        showToast('success', `File Excel berhasil dibaca! Ditemukan ${umImportWorkbook.SheetNames.length} sheet.`);
      } catch (err) {
        showToast('error', 'Gagal membaca file Excel: ' + err.message);
      }
    };
    reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
  });
}

function handleGoogleSheetUrlFetch() {
  const url = val('umImportSheetUrl');
  if (!url) {
    showToast('error', 'Masukkan URL Google Spreadsheet.');
    return;
  }

  const match = url.match(/\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9-_]+)/);
  if (!match) {
    showToast('error', 'URL Google Spreadsheet tidak valid.');
    return;
  }

  ensureXlsxLoaded(() => {
    const id = match[1];
    const exportUrl = `https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/${id}/export?format=xlsx`;
    showToast('info', 'Mengambil data dari Google Spreadsheet...');

    fetch(exportUrl)
      .then(res => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Gagal mengambil file Google Sheet. Pastikan spreadsheet dapat diakses (Anyone with link / Organisasi).');
        return res.arrayBuffer();
      })
      .then(buf => {
        const data = new Uint8Array(buf);
        umImportWorkbook = XLSX.read(data, { type: 'array' });
        populateImportSheetSelect(umImportWorkbook.SheetNames);
        showToast('success', `Google Spreadsheet berhasil dibaca! Ditemukan ${umImportWorkbook.SheetNames.length} sheet.`);
      })
      .catch(err => {
        showToast('error', 'Gagal membaca Google Sheet: ' + (err.message || err));
      });
  });
}

function populateImportSheetSelect(sheetNames) {
  const wrap = document.getElementById('umSheetSelectWrap');
  const select = document.getElementById('umSheetSelect');
  const badge = document.getElementById('umSheetCountBadge');
  if (!wrap || !select) return;

  select.innerHTML = sheetNames.map((s, i) => `<option value="${escapeHtml(s)}" ${i === 0 ? 'selected' : ''}>📌 Sheet: ${escapeHtml(s)}</option>`).join('');
  if (badge) badge.textContent = `${sheetNames.length} Sheet Ditemukan`;

  wrap.style.display = 'block';
  setTimeout(() => {
    wrap.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });
  }, 100);

  onSelectImportSheetChange();
}

function onSelectImportSheetChange() {
  if (!umImportWorkbook) return;
  const select = document.getElementById('umSheetSelect');
  const sheetName = select ? select.value : umImportWorkbook.SheetNames[0];
  
  const selectedSheetTitleEl = document.getElementById('umSelectedSheetName');
  if (selectedSheetTitleEl) {
    selectedSheetTitleEl.textContent = sheetName ? `"${sheetName}"` : '-';
  }

  const worksheet = umImportWorkbook.Sheets[sheetName];
  if (!worksheet) return;

  const rawData = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(worksheet, { header: 1 });
  if (!rawData || !rawData.length) {
    showToast('warning', 'Sheet ' + sheetName + ' kosong.');
    return;
  }

  let headerRowIndex = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(rawData.length, 10); i++) {
    const rowStr = (rawData[i] || []).join(' ').toLowerCase();
    if (rowStr.includes('nama') || rowStr.includes('nip') || rowStr.includes('status')) {
      headerRowIndex = i;
      break;
    }
  }

  const headers = (rawData[headerRowIndex] || []).map(h => String(h || '').trim().toLowerCase());
  
  const idxNama = headers.findIndex(h => h.includes('nama lengkap') || h === 'nama');
  const idxNip = headers.findIndex(h => h.includes('nip'));
  const idxUnit = headers.findIndex(h => h.includes('unit') || h.includes('fakultas'));
  const idxJabatan = headers.findIndex(h => h.includes('jabatan'));
  const idxJenis = headers.findIndex(h => h.includes('jenis') || h.includes('kelompok'));
  const idxStatusKep = headers.findIndex(h => (h.includes('status') && (h.includes('pegawai') || h.includes('kepegawaian'))) || h.includes('kepegawaian'));
  const idxGolLama = headers.findIndex(h => h.includes('gol') && (h.includes('lama') || h.includes('saat ini')));
  const idxStatus = headers.findIndex(h => h.includes('status') || h.includes('siasn'));
  const idxGolBaru = headers.findIndex(h => h.includes('gol') && h.includes('baru'));
  const idxKet = headers.findIndex(h => h.includes('keterangan') || h.includes('tmt') || h.includes('bulan'));

  const targetJenisInput = document.getElementById('umImportJenisTarget') ? document.getElementById('umImportJenisTarget').value : 'AUTO';
  const targetStatusKepInput = document.getElementById('umImportStatusKepTarget') ? document.getElementById('umImportStatusKepTarget').value : 'AUTO';

  umParsedImportRows = [];

  for (let i = headerRowIndex + 1; i < rawData.length; i++) {
    const row = rawData[i];
    if (!row || !row.length) continue;

    const nama = idxNama >= 0 ? String(row[idxNama] || '').trim() : '';
    const nip = idxNip >= 0 ? String(row[idxNip] || '').trim() : '';

    if (!nama && !nip) continue;

    const unit = idxUnit >= 0 ? String(row[idxUnit] || '').trim() : '';
    const jabatan = idxJabatan >= 0 ? String(row[idxJabatan] || '').trim() : '';
    
    let jenis_pegawai = targetJenisInput !== 'AUTO' ? targetJenisInput : (idxJenis >= 0 ? String(row[idxJenis] || '').trim() : '');
    if (!jenis_pegawai) {
      const jLower = jabatan.toLowerCase();
      if (jLower.includes('dosen') || jLower.includes('lektor') || jLower.includes('guru besar') || jLower.includes('asisten ahli')) {
        jenis_pegawai = 'Dosen';
      } else {
        jenis_pegawai = 'Tendik';
      }
    }

    let status_kepegawaian = targetStatusKepInput !== 'AUTO' ? targetStatusKepInput : (idxStatusKep >= 0 ? String(row[idxStatusKep] || '').trim() : '');
    if (!status_kepegawaian) {
      status_kepegawaian = 'PNS';
    }

    const golLama = idxGolLama >= 0 ? String(row[idxGolLama] || '').trim() : '';
    let rawStatus = idxStatus >= 0 ? String(row[idxStatus] || '').trim() : 'Diterima DSDM';
    
    let status = 'Diterima DSDM';
    const sLower = rawStatus.toLowerCase();
    if (sLower.includes('siasn')) status = 'Diajukan SIASN';
    else if (sLower.includes('pertek')) status = 'TTD Pertek';
    else if (sLower.includes('sk')) status = 'SK Berhasil';
    else if (sLower.includes('perbaikan')) status = 'Perbaikan';
    else if (sLower.includes('eligible')) status = 'Belum Eligible';
    else if (sLower.includes('pensiun')) status = 'Pensiun';
    else if (rawStatus) status = rawStatus;

    let golBaru = idxGolBaru >= 0 ? String(row[idxGolBaru] || '').trim() : '';
    if (!golBaru && golLama) {
      golBaru = hitungProyeksiGolonganBaru(golLama);
    }

    const targetYearInput = document.getElementById('umImportYearTarget') ? document.getElementById('umImportYearTarget').value : '2026';
    let rawTmt = idxKet >= 0 ? String(row[idxKet] || '').trim() : '';
    let tmt = rawTmt;

    if (targetYearInput !== 'KEEP_EXACT') {
      if (rawTmt) {
        const matchYear = rawTmt.match(/\b(20\d{2})\b/);
        if (!matchYear) {
          tmt = `${rawTmt} ${targetYearInput}`;
        } else {
          tmt = rawTmt.replace(/\b(20\d{2})\b/, targetYearInput);
        }
      } else {
        tmt = `Oktober ${targetYearInput}`;
      }
    } else if (!rawTmt) {
      tmt = 'Oktober 2026';
    }

    umParsedImportRows.push({
      nama, nip, unit, jabatan, jenis_pegawai, status_kepegawaian, golLama, golBaru, status, tmt
    });
  }

  renderImportPreviewTable(umParsedImportRows);
}

function renderImportPreviewTable(rows) {
  const wrap = document.getElementById('umImportPreviewWrap');
  const tbody = document.getElementById('umImportPreviewTbody');
  const countEl = document.getElementById('umImportRowCount');

  if (!wrap || !tbody) return;

  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = rows.length;
  if (!rows.length) {
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="11" style="text-align:center; padding:16px;">Tidak ada data usulan ditemukan pada sheet ini.</td></tr>';
    wrap.style.display = 'block';
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML = rows.map((r, i) => `
    <tr>
      <td>${i + 1}</td>
      <td><strong>${escapeHtml(r.nama || '-')}</strong></td>
      <td>${escapeHtml(r.nip || '-')}</td>
      <td>${escapeHtml(r.unit || '<span style="color:var(--text-muted); font-style:italic;">[Auto-SIMPEG]</span>')}</td>
      <td>${escapeHtml(r.jabatan || '<span style="color:var(--text-muted); font-style:italic;">[Auto-SIMPEG]</span>')}</td>
      <td>
        <span class="cdli-badge" style="background:var(--primary); color:#fff; font-size:11px;">${escapeHtml(r.jenis_pegawai || 'Tendik')}</span>
        <span class="cdli-badge" style="background:var(--surface-alt); border:1px solid var(--border); font-size:11px; margin-top:2px; display:inline-block;">${escapeHtml(r.status_kepegawaian || 'PNS')}</span>
      </td>
      <td>${escapeHtml(r.golLama || '<span style="color:var(--text-muted); font-style:italic;">[Auto-SIMPEG]</span>')}</td>
      <td><strong style="color:#059669;">${escapeHtml(r.golBaru || hitungProyeksiGolonganBaru(r.golLama) || '-')}</strong></td>
      <td><span class="cdli-badge" style="background:var(--surface-alt); border:1px solid var(--border);">${escapeHtml(r.status)}</span></td>
      <td>${escapeHtml(r.tmt)}</td>
    </tr>
  `).join('');

  wrap.style.display = 'block';
}

function submitBulkImportData() {
  if (!umParsedImportRows || !umParsedImportRows.length) {
    showToast('warning', 'Tidak ada data usulan yang siap diimpor.');
    return;
  }

  const btn = document.getElementById('umSubmitImportBtn');
  if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
  showToast('info', `Mengimpor ${umParsedImportRows.length} data usulan masuk...`);

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
      if (!res || !res.success) {
        showToast('error', (res && res.message) || 'Gagal mengimpor data.');
        return;
      }
      showToast('success', res.message || `Berhasil mengimpor ${umParsedImportRows.length} usulan.`);
      closeUmImportModal();
      loadUsulanMasukTmtDashboard();
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => {
