      if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
      showToast('error', 'Gagal mengimpor massal: ' + (err.message || err));
    })
    .bulkInsertUsulanMasuk(SESSION.token, umParsedImportRows);
}

function clearImportPreviewData() {
  umImportWorkbook = null;
  umParsedImportRows = [];
  
  const fileInput = document.getElementById('umImportFileInput');
  if (fileInput) fileInput.value = '';
  
  const urlInput = document.getElementById('umImportSheetUrl');
  if (urlInput) urlInput.value = '';

  const sheetWrap = document.getElementById('umSheetSelectWrap');
  if (sheetWrap) sheetWrap.style.display = 'none';

  const previewWrap = document.getElementById('umImportPreviewWrap');
  if (previewWrap) previewWrap.style.display = 'none';

  const tbody = document.getElementById('umImportPreviewTbody');
  if (tbody) tbody.innerHTML = '';

  showToast('info', '🗑️ Data pratinjau import spreadsheet berhasil dibersihkan.');
}

function deleteAllUsulanMasukBulk() {
  if (!confirm('Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus SELURUH data usulan masuk yang ada pada database? Tindakan ini tidak dapat dibatalkan.')) return;

  showToast('info', 'Menghapus seluruh data usulan...');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (!res || !res.success) {
        showToast('error', (res && res.message) || 'Gagal menghapus data.');
        return;
      }
      showToast('success', 'Seluruh data usulan berhasil dihapus dari database.');
      loadUsulanMasukTmtDashboard();
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => {
      showToast('error', 'Gagal menghapus massal: ' + (err.message || err));
    })
    .deleteAllUsulanMasuk(SESSION.token);
}

async function handleInputUsulanMasuk(e) {
  e.preventDefault();
  const nama = val('umInputNama');
  const nip = val('umInputNip');
  const status = val('umInputStatus');
  const golLama = val('umInputGolLama');
  const golBaru = val('umInputGolBaru') || hitungProyeksiGolonganBaru(golLama);
  const tmtBulan = val('umInputTmtBulan');
  const tmtTahun = val('umInputTmtTahun');
  const unit = val('umInputUnit');
  const jabatan = val('umInputJabatan');
  const jenis_pegawai = val('umInputJenisPegawai') || 'Tendik';
  const status_kepegawaian = val('umInputStatusKepegawaian') || 'PNS';

  if (!nama) { showToast('error', 'Nama pegawai wajib diisi.'); return; }

  const btn = document.getElementById('umSubmitBtn');
  if (btn) btn.disabled = true;

  const tmt = `${tmtBulan} ${tmtTahun}`;
  const payload = { nama, nip, status, golLama, golBaru, tmt, unit, jabatan, jenis_pegawai, status_kepegawaian };

  try {
    const res = await rpc('insertUsulanMasukSingle', [SESSION.token, payload]);
    if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
    if (res && res.success) {
      showToast('success', 'Usulan baru berhasil disimpan.');
      document.getElementById('formInputUsulanMasuk').reset();
      const wrap = document.getElementById('umProyeksiRankWrap');
      if (wrap) wrap.style.display = 'none';
      loadUsulanMasukTmtDashboard(true);
    } else {
      showToast('error', res?.message || 'Gagal menyimpan usulan.');
    }
  } catch (err) {
    if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(res => {
          if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
          if (!res || !res.success) {
            showToast('error', (res && res.message) || 'Gagal menyimpan usulan.');
            return;
          }
          showToast('success', 'Usulan baru berhasil disimpan.');
          document.getElementById('formInputUsulanMasuk').reset();
          const wrap = document.getElementById('umProyeksiRankWrap');
          if (wrap) wrap.style.display = 'none';
          loadUsulanMasukTmtDashboard(true);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => {
          if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
          showToast('error', 'Gagal menyimpan usulan: ' + (e.message || e));
        })
        .insertUsulanMasukSingle(SESSION.token, payload);
    } else {
      if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
      showToast('error', 'Gagal menyimpan usulan: ' + (err.message || err));
    }
  }
}

async function updateUsulanStatusInline(id, newStatus) {
  showToast('info', 'Memperbarui status...');
  try {
    const res = await rpc('updateUsulanMasukStatus', [SESSION.token, { id, status: newStatus }]);
    if (res && res.success) {
      showToast('success', 'Status usulan berhasil diperbarui.');
      loadUsulanMasukTmtDashboard(true);
    } else {
      showToast('error', res?.message || 'Gagal memperbarui status.');
    }
  } catch (err) {
    if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(res => {
          if (!res || !res.success) {
            showToast('error', (res && res.message) || 'Gagal memperbarui status.');
            return;
          }
          showToast('success', 'Status usulan berhasil diperbarui.');
          loadUsulanMasukTmtDashboard(true);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => showToast('error', 'Gagal memperbarui status: ' + (e.message || e)))
        .updateUsulanMasukStatus(SESSION.token, { id, status: newStatus });
    } else {
      showToast('error', 'Gagal memperbarui status: ' + (err.message || err));
    }
  }
}

async function deleteUsulanMasukInline(id) {
  if (!confirm('Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus usulan ini?')) return;

  showToast('info', 'Menghapus usulan...');
  try {
    const res = await rpc('deleteUsulanMasukSingle', [SESSION.token, id]);
    if (res && res.success) {
      showToast('success', 'Usulan berhasil dihapus.');
      loadUsulanMasukTmtDashboard(true);
    } else {
      showToast('error', res?.message || 'Gagal menghapus usulan.');
    }
  } catch (err) {
    if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(res => {
          if (!res || !res.success) {
            showToast('error', (res && res.message) || 'Gagal menghapus usulan.');
            return;
          }
          showToast('success', 'Usulan berhasil dihapus.');
          loadUsulanMasukTmtDashboard(true);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => showToast('error', 'Gagal menghapus usulan: ' + (e.message || e)))
        .deleteUsulanMasukSingle(SESSION.token, id);
    } else {
      showToast('error', 'Gagal menghapus usulan: ' + (err.message || err));
    }
  }
}

function toggleUsulanUnitView() {
  const container = document.getElementById('usulanMasukTmtContainer');
  const level1 = document.getElementById('usulanMasukLevel1');
  const level2 = document.getElementById('usulanMasukLevel2');

  if (container.style.display === 'none') {
    container.style.display = 'block';
    level1.style.display = 'none';
    level2.style.display = 'none';
  } else {
    container.style.display = 'none';
    loadUsulanMasukLegacyUnitDashboard();
  }
}

function loadUsulanMasukLegacyUnitDashboard() {
  const level1 = document.getElementById('usulanMasukLevel1');
  level1.style.display = 'grid';
  level1.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Memuat daftar unit...</div>';

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (!res.success) { showToast('error', res.message); return; }
      if (!res.daftarUnit.length) {
        level1.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Belum ada usulan masuk.</div>';
        return;
      }
      level1.innerHTML = res.daftarUnit.map(u => `
        <button type="button" class="ce-dashboard-card" onclick="showUsulanMasukLevel2('${escapeHtml(u.unit).replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">
          <div class="cdc-title">${escapeHtml(u.unit)}</div>
          <div class="cdc-count">${u.jumlah}</div>
          <div class="cdc-sub">usulan</div>
        </button>
      `).join('');
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat daftar unit: ' + (err.message || err)))
    .getUsulanMasukSummaryPerUnit(SESSION.token);
}

function showUsulanMasukLevel2(unit) {
  document.getElementById('usulanMasukLevel1').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('usulanMasukLevel2').style.display = 'block';

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (!res.success) { showToast('error', res.message); return; }
      const listEl = document.getElementById('usulanMasukLevel2List');
      if (!res.daftar.length) {
        listEl.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Tidak ada data usulan di unit ini.</div>';
        return;
      }
      listEl.innerHTML = res.daftar.map(u => `
        <div class="ce-dashboard-list-item" onclick="onSelectUsulanMasukEmployee('${escapeHtml(u.nip)}','${escapeHtml(u.nama).replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">
          <div>
            <div class="cdli-name">${escapeHtml(u.nama)}</div>
            <div class="cdli-meta">${escapeHtml(u.jabatan || '-')} &middot; NIP ${escapeHtml(u.nip)} &middot; ${escapeHtml(u.tanggalDiajukan)}</div>
          </div>
          <span class="cdli-badge">${escapeHtml(u.status || 'Diajukan')}</span>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat daftar usulan: ' + (err.message || err)))
    .getUsulanMasukListByUnit(SESSION.token, unit);
}

/* =====================================================================
 * ALUR USULAN KENAIKAN PANGKAT (User -> Admin)
 * REF: UsulanKPService.gs. Modal "Ajukan Usulan" memakai modal umum yang
 * sama dipakai fitur preview/hasil lain (modalOverlay/modalTitle/modalBody).
 * ===================================================================== */
let usulanModalDaftar = []; // {nip, nama} pegawai yang ditambahkan di modal
let usulanModalFileBase64 = '';

function bukaModalAjukanUsulan() {
  usulanModalDaftar = [];
  usulanModalFileBase64 = '';
  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'Ajukan Usulan Kenaikan Pangkat';
  document.getElementById('modalBody').innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding:22px;">
      <div class="stack-field autocomplete-wrap">
        <label class="kp-label">Cari &amp; Tambah Pegawai</label>
        <input type="text" id="usulanModal_search" class="field-input" placeholder="Cari nama atau NIP..." autocomplete="off" />
        <input type="hidden" id="usulanModal_searchNip" />
        <div class="autocomplete-list" id="usulanModal_searchList"></div>
      </div>
      <div id="usulanModal_daftarPegawai" style="margin:14px 0; display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:8px;"></div>
      <div class="stack-field grid-full">
        <label>Nomor Surat Usulan Unit <span style="color:#ef4444;">*</span></label>
        <input type="text" id="usulanModal_nomorSurat" class="field-input" placeholder="Contoh: B-123/UN7/2025" />
      </div>
      <div class="stack-field grid-full">
        <label>Tanggal Surat Usulan <span style="color:#ef4444;">*</span></label>
        <input type="date" id="usulanModal_tglSurat" class="field-input" />
      </div>
      <div class="stack-field grid-full">
        <label>Upload Surat Usulan (PDF, maks. 1 MB) <span style="color:#ef4444;">*</span></label>
        <input type="file" id="usulanModal_file" accept=".pdf,.jpg,.jpeg,.png" />
        <div class="helper-text" id="usulanModal_fileMsg" style="display:none;">✓ File dimuat.</div>
        <div class="helper-text" style="color:#f59e0b;">⚠️ Maksimal ukuran file 1 MB</div>
      </div>
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" id="usulanModal_submitBtn" style="margin-top:18px; width:100%;">Ajukan Usulan</button>
    </div>
  `;
  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').style.display = 'flex';
  renderUsulanModalDaftar();

  setupAutocompleteEl(
    document.getElementById('usulanModal_search'),
    document.getElementById('usulanModal_searchList'),
    document.getElementById('usulanModal_searchNip'),
    (nip, nama) => {
      if (usulanModalDaftar.some(p => p.nip === nip)) { showToast('error', 'Pegawai ini sudah ada di daftar.'); return; }
      usulanModalDaftar.push({ nip, nama });
      document.getElementById('usulanModal_search').value = '';
      document.getElementById('usulanModal_searchNip').value = '';
      renderUsulanModalDaftar();
    }
  );

  document.getElementById('usulanModal_file').addEventListener('change', e => {
    const file = e.target.files[0];
    const msg = document.getElementById('usulanModal_fileMsg');
    if (!file) return;
    if (file.size > 1 * 1024 * 1024) {
      showToast('error', 'Ukuran file melebihi 1 MB. Kompres atau perkecil file terlebih dahulu.');
      e.target.value = ''; msg.style.display = 'none';
      return;
    }
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = ev => { usulanModalFileBase64 = ev.target.result; msg.style.display = 'block'; };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });

  document.getElementById('usulanModal_submitBtn').addEventListener('click', submitUsulanModal);
}

function renderUsulanModalDaftar() {
  const el = document.getElementById('usulanModal_daftarPegawai');
  if (!usulanModalDaftar.length) {
    el.innerHTML = '<div class="helper-text">Belum ada pegawai ditambahkan.</div>';
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = usulanModalDaftar.map((p, i) => `
    <div class="ce-dashboard-list-item" style="cursor:default;">
      <div class="cdli-name">${escapeHtml(p.nama)} <span class="cdli-meta">(NIP ${escapeHtml(p.nip)})</span></div>
      <button type="button" class="kp-entry-remove" data-idx="${i}">🗑 Hapus</button>
    </div>
  `).join('');
  el.querySelectorAll('[data-idx]').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      usulanModalDaftar.splice(parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-idx'), 10), 1);
      renderUsulanModalDaftar();
    });
  });
}

function submitUsulanModal() {
  if (!usulanModalDaftar.length) { showToast('error', 'Tambahkan minimal 1 pegawai.'); return; }
  if (!usulanModalFileBase64) { showToast('error', 'Unggah surat usulan terlebih dahulu.'); return; }
  const nomorSurat = (document.getElementById('usulanModal_nomorSurat').value || '').trim();
  const tglSurat   = (document.getElementById('usulanModal_tglSurat').value || '').trim();
  if (!nomorSurat) { showToast('error', 'Nomor Surat Usulan Unit wajib diisi.'); return; }
  if (!tglSurat)   { showToast('error', 'Tanggal Surat Usulan wajib diisi.'); return; }

  const fileInput = document.getElementById('usulanModal_file');
  const namaFile = fileInput.files[0] ? fileInput.files[0].name : 'surat_usulan';
  const btn = document.getElementById('usulanModal_submitBtn');
  setBtnLoading(btn, true, 'Mengirim...');

  const rpcCall = (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script)
    ? google.script.run
    : window.rpc;

  const doCall = (rpc) => rpc
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Ajukan Usulan');
      if (res.success) {
        showToast('success', res.message);
        document.getElementById('modalOverlay').style.display = 'none';
      } else {
        showToast('error', res.message);
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => {
      setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Ajukan Usulan');
      showToast('error', 'Gagal mengajukan usulan: ' + (err.message || err));
    })
    .ajukanUsulanKP(SESSION.token, {
      daftarPegawai: usulanModalDaftar,
      suratUsulanBase64: usulanModalFileBase64,
      namaFileSuratUsulan: namaFile,
      nomor_surat_usul_unit: nomorSurat,
      tgl_surat_usul: tglSurat
    });

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script) {
    doCall(google.script.run);
  } else {
    window.rpc('ajukanUsulanKP', [SESSION.token, {
      daftarPegawai: usulanModalDaftar,
      suratUsulanBase64: usulanModalFileBase64,
      namaFileSuratUsulan: namaFile,
      nomor_surat_usul_unit: nomorSurat,
      tgl_surat_usul: tglSurat
    }]).then(res => {
      setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Ajukan Usulan');
      if (res.success) { showToast('success', res.message); document.getElementById('modalOverlay').style.display = 'none'; }
      else showToast('error', res.message);
    }).catch(err => { setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Ajukan Usulan'); showToast('error', err.message); });
  }
}

/* ---------- STATUS USULAN SAYA (peran user) ---------- */
function loadStatusUsulanSaya() {
  const el = document.getElementById('statusUsulanList');
  el.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Memuat...</div>';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (!res.success) { showToast('error', res.message); return; }
      if (!res.daftar.length) { el.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Anda belum pernah mengajukan usulan.</div>'; return; }
      el.innerHTML = res.daftar.map(u => {
        let actionHtml = '';
        if (u.skPdfUrl) {
          actionHtml = `<a href="${u.skPdfUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="btn btn-primary kp-small-btn" style="text-decoration:none;">⬇️ Download SK</a>`;
        }
        return `
        <div class="ce-dashboard-list-item" style="cursor:default; flex-wrap:wrap;">
          <div>
            <div class="cdli-name">${escapeHtml(u.nama)} ${u.opsiASelesai ? '<span class="cdli-warn" style="background:#d1fae5; color:#065f46;">Opsi A ✓</span>' : ''}${u.opsiBSelesai ? ' <span class="cdli-warn" style="background:#d1fae5; color:#065f46;">Opsi B ✓</span>' : ''}</div>
            <div class="cdli-meta">NIP ${escapeHtml(u.nip)} &middot; Diajukan ${escapeHtml(u.tanggalDiajukan)}</div>
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px;">
            ${actionHtml}
            <span class="cdli-badge">${escapeHtml(u.status)}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        `;
      }).join('');
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat status usulan: ' + (err.message || err)))
    .getUsulanSayaForUser(SESSION.token);
}

/* ---------- USULAN MASUK (peran admin/super_admin) ---------- */
function loadUsulanMasukBadge() {
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (!res.success) return;
      const badge = document.getElementById('usulanMasukBadge');
      if (res.totalUsulanBaru > 0) {
        badge.textContent = res.totalUsulanBaru;
        badge.style.display = 'inline-block';
      } else {
        badge.style.display = 'none';
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(() => {})
    .getUsulanNotifikasiSummary(SESSION.token);
}

function loadUsulanMasukDashboard() {
  const el = document.getElementById('usulanMasukLevel1');
  el.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Memuat...</div>';
  document.getElementById('usulanMasukLevel1').style.display = 'grid';
  document.getElementById('usulanMasukLevel2').style.display = 'none';

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (!res.success) { showToast('error', res.message); return; }
      loadUsulanMasukBadge();
      if (!res.daftarUnit.length) {
        el.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Tidak ada usulan baru saat ini.</div>';
        return;
      }
      el.innerHTML = res.daftarUnit.map(u => `
        <button type="button" class="ce-dashboard-card" onclick="showUsulanMasukLevel2('${escapeHtml(u.unit).replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">
          <div class="cdc-title">${escapeHtml(u.unit)}</div>
          <div class="cdc-count">${u.jumlah}</div>
          <div class="cdc-sub">usulan baru</div>
        </button>
      `).join('');
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat usulan masuk: ' + (err.message || err)))
    .getUsulanNotifikasiSummary(SESSION.token);
}

function showUsulanMasukLevel2(unit) {
  document.getElementById('usulanMasukLevel1').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('usulanMasukLevel2').style.display = 'block';

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (!res.success) { showToast('error', res.message); return; }
      const listEl = document.getElementById('usulanMasukLevel2List');
      if (!res.daftar.length) { listEl.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Tidak ada data.</div>'; return; }
      listEl.innerHTML = res.daftar.map(u => `
        <div class="ce-dashboard-list-item" style="cursor:default; flex-wrap:wrap;">
          <div>
            <div class="cdli-name">${escapeHtml(u.nama)} ${u.opsiASelesai ? '<span class="cdli-warn" style="background:#d1fae5; color:#065f46;">Opsi A ✓</span>' : ''}${u.opsiBSelesai ? ' <span class="cdli-warn" style="background:#d1fae5; color:#065f46;">Opsi B ✓</span>' : ''}</div>
            <div class="cdli-meta">NIP ${escapeHtml(u.nip)} &middot; Diajukan oleh ${escapeHtml(u.namaPengaju)} &middot; ${escapeHtml(u.tanggalDiajukan)}</div>
            <div class="cdli-meta" style="margin-top:2px;">Status akhir baru muncul setelah Opsi A DAN Opsi B sama-sama diproses.</div>
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex; gap:8px; align-items:center;">
            ${u.fileUrl ? `<a href="${u.fileUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="btn btn-outline kp-small-btn">Lihat Surat</a>` : ''}
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline kp-small-btn" data-nip="${escapeHtml(u.nip)}" data-nama="${escapeHtml(u.nama).replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}" data-action="proses-a">Proses Opsi A</button>
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary kp-small-btn" data-nip="${escapeHtml(u.nip)}" data-nama="${escapeHtml(u.nama).replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}" data-action="proses-b">Proses Opsi B</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('');

      listEl.querySelectorAll('[data-action="proses-a"]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => prosesUsulanKeOpsi(btn.getAttribute('data-nip'), btn.getAttribute('data-nama'), 'A'));
      });
      listEl.querySelectorAll('[data-action="proses-b"]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => prosesUsulanKeOpsi(btn.getAttribute('data-nip'), btn.getAttribute('data-nama'), 'B'));
      });
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat daftar usulan: ' + (err.message || err)))
    .getUsulanListByUnit(SESSION.token, unit);

  document.getElementById('usulanMasukBackBtn').onclick = loadUsulanMasukDashboard;
}

/**
 * Admin pilih "Proses Opsi A/B" dari daftar Usulan Masuk -> lompat ke tab
 * terkait & pre-fill kartu entri PERTAMA dengan pegawai itu (mekanisme
 * SAMA persis dengan onSelectDocGenSubjectCard yang sudah ada). Status
 * usulan akan otomatis ikut diperbarui oleh backend begitu admin benar-
 * benar menekan "Generate" (lihat perbaruiStatusUsulanSetelahGenerate_) --
 * TIDAK perlu penghubung id usulan eksplisit, cukup lewat kecocokan NIP.
 */
function prosesUsulanKeOpsi(nip, nama, opt) {
  activateTab('kp', opt === 'A' ? 'tab-optA' : 'tab-optB');
  const container = document.getElementById(opt === 'A' ? 'optA_entriesContainer' : 'optB_entriesContainer');
  const firstCard = container.querySelector('.kp-entry-card');
  if (!firstCard) return;

  firstCard.querySelector('[data-field="subjectInput"]').value = `${nama} (${nip})`;
  firstCard.querySelector('[data-field="subjectNip"]').value = nip;
  onSelectDocGenSubjectCard(opt, firstCard, nip);
  showToast('info', `${nama} dimuat ke Opsi ${opt}. Lengkapi data lalu klik Generate untuk memperbarui status usulannya.`);
}

/* =====================================================================
 * ALUR USULAN PENSIUN (User -> Admin)
 * REF: PensiunUsulanService.gs. Satu usulan = SATU pegawai + Jenis
 * Pensiun + dokumen (surat pengantar wajib selalu, dokumen tambahan
 * kondisional sesuai Jenis Pensiun -- lihat CONFIG.USULAN_PENSIUN_DOKUMEN_TAMBAHAN).
 * ===================================================================== */
let usulanPensiunFileUtamaBase64 = '';
let usulanPensiunFileTambahan1Base64 = '';
let usulanPensiunFileTambahan2Base64 = '';

/** Helper umum: baca <input type="file"> jadi base64 data URL, validasi ukuran maks. 5 MB. */
function handleFileInputBase64_(inputEl, onLoaded, msgElId) {
  const file = inputEl.files[0];
  const msg = msgElId ? document.getElementById(msgElId) : null;
  if (!file) return;
  if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
    showToast('error', 'Ukuran file melebihi 5 MB.');
    inputEl.value = '';
    if (msg) msg.style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = e => {
    onLoaded(e.target.result);
    if (msg) msg.style.display = 'block';
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}

/**
 * Buka modal Ajukan Usulan Pensiun. nip/nama OPSIONAL -- kalau diisi
 * (dipanggil dari baris dashboard), field pencarian pegawai langsung
 * terisi; kalau kosong (dipanggil dari tombol "Cari Pegawai Manual"),
 * admin/user mengisi sendiri lewat pencarian di dalam modal.
 */
function bukaModalAjukanUsulanPensiun(nip, nama) {
  usulanPensiunFileUtamaBase64 = '';
  usulanPensiunFileTambahan1Base64 = '';
  usulanPensiunFileTambahan2Base64 = '';

  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'Ajukan Usulan Pensiun';
  document.getElementById('modalBody').innerHTML = `
    <div style="padding:22px;">
      <div class="stack-field autocomplete-wrap">
        <label class="kp-label">Pegawai yang Diajukan</label>
        <input type="text" id="usulanPensiunModal_search" class="field-input" placeholder="Cari nama atau NIP..." autocomplete="off" value="${nama ? escapeHtml(nama) + ' (' + escapeHtml(nip) + ')' : ''}" />
        <input type="hidden" id="usulanPensiunModal_nip" value="${nip || ''}" />
        <div class="autocomplete-list" id="usulanPensiunModal_searchList"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="stack-field">
        <label class="kp-label">Jenis Pensiun</label>
        <select id="usulanPensiunModal_jenis" class="field-input"></select>
      </div>
      <div class="stack-field grid-full">
        <label>Surat Pengantar (wajib)</label>
        <input type="file" id="usulanPensiunModal_fileUtama" accept=".pdf,.jpg,.jpeg,.png" />
        <div class="helper-text" id="usulanPensiunModal_fileUtamaMsg" style="display:none;">✓ File dimuat.</div>
      </div>
      <div id="usulanPensiunModal_dokumenTambahan"></div>
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" id="usulanPensiunModal_submitBtn" style="margin-top:18px; width:100%;">Ajukan Usulan</button>
    </div>
  `;
  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').style.display = 'flex';

  setupAutocompleteEl(
    document.getElementById('usulanPensiunModal_search'),
    document.getElementById('usulanPensiunModal_searchList'),
    document.getElementById('usulanPensiunModal_nip'),
    () => {}
  );

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(opts => {
      populateSelect('usulanPensiunModal_jenis', opts.jenisPensiun, { placeholder: 'Pilih Jenis Pensiun...' });
      document.getElementById('usulanPensiunModal_jenis').addEventListener('change', renderDokumenTambahanUsulanPensiun);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat opsi: ' + (err.message || err)))
    .getPensiunFormOptions();

  document.getElementById('usulanPensiunModal_fileUtama').addEventListener('change', e => {
    handleFileInputBase64_(e.target, dataUrl => { usulanPensiunFileUtamaBase64 = dataUrl; }, 'usulanPensiunModal_fileUtamaMsg');
  });

  document.getElementById('usulanPensiunModal_submitBtn').addEventListener('click', submitUsulanPensiunModal);
}

/** Tampilkan input file dokumen tambahan sesuai Jenis Pensiun terpilih (REF: CONFIG.USULAN_PENSIUN_DOKUMEN_TAMBAHAN). */
function renderDokumenTambahanUsulanPensiun() {
  const jenis = document.getElementById('usulanPensiunModal_jenis').value;
  const map = {
    'BUP': [],
    'Meninggal': ['Surat Bukti Kematian'],
    'Uzur': ['Bukti Tes Kesehatan'],
    'Pengunduran Diri': ['Surat Pernyataan Pengunduran Diri', 'Surat Pengantar Pimpinan Unit']
  };
  const dokumen = map[jenis] || [];
  usulanPensiunFileTambahan1Base64 = '';
  usulanPensiunFileTambahan2Base64 = '';

  const el = document.getElementById('usulanPensiunModal_dokumenTambahan');
  el.innerHTML = dokumen.map((label, i) => `
    <div class="stack-field grid-full">
      <label>${escapeHtml(label)} (wajib)</label>
      <input type="file" id="usulanPensiunModal_fileTambahan${i + 1}" accept=".pdf,.jpg,.jpeg,.png" />
      <div class="helper-text" id="usulanPensiunModal_fileTambahan${i + 1}Msg" style="display:none;">✓ File dimuat.</div>
    </div>
  `).join('');

  dokumen.forEach((_, i) => {
    document.getElementById(`usulanPensiunModal_fileTambahan${i + 1}`).addEventListener('change', e => {
      handleFileInputBase64_(e.target, dataUrl => {
        if (i === 0) usulanPensiunFileTambahan1Base64 = dataUrl;
        else usulanPensiunFileTambahan2Base64 = dataUrl;
      }, `usulanPensiunModal_fileTambahan${i + 1}Msg`);
    });
  });
}

function submitUsulanPensiunModal() {
  const nip = document.getElementById('usulanPensiunModal_nip').value;
  const searchVal = document.getElementById('usulanPensiunModal_search').value;
  const nama = searchVal.replace(/\s*\([^)]*\)\s*$/, '').trim();
  const jenis = document.getElementById('usulanPensiunModal_jenis').value;

  if (!nip) { showToast('error', 'Pilih pegawai terlebih dahulu.'); return; }
  if (!jenis) { showToast('error', 'Jenis Pensiun wajib dipilih.'); return; }
  if (!usulanPensiunFileUtamaBase64) { showToast('error', 'Surat pengantar wajib diunggah.'); return; }

  const fileUtamaInput = document.getElementById('usulanPensiunModal_fileUtama');
  const fileTambahan1Input = document.getElementById('usulanPensiunModal_fileTambahan1');
  const fileTambahan2Input = document.getElementById('usulanPensiunModal_fileTambahan2');

  const btn = document.getElementById('usulanPensiunModal_submitBtn');
  setBtnLoading(btn, true, 'Mengirim...');

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Ajukan Usulan');
      if (res.success) { showToast('success', res.message); document.getElementById('modalOverlay').style.display = 'none'; }
      else showToast('error', res.message);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => {
      setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Ajukan Usulan');
      showToast('error', 'Gagal mengajukan usulan pensiun: ' + (err.message || err));
    })
    .ajukanUsulanPensiun(SESSION.token, {
      nip: nip, nama: nama, jenisPensiun: jenis,
      suratPengantarBase64: usulanPensiunFileUtamaBase64,
      namaFileSuratPengantar: fileUtamaInput.files[0] ? fileUtamaInput.files[0].name : 'surat_pengantar',
      fileTambahan1Base64: usulanPensiunFileTambahan1Base64,
      namaFileTambahan1: (fileTambahan1Input && fileTambahan1Input.files[0]) ? fileTambahan1Input.files[0].name : '',
      fileTambahan2Base64: usulanPensiunFileTambahan2Base64,
      namaFileTambahan2: (fileTambahan2Input && fileTambahan2Input.files[0]) ? fileTambahan2Input.files[0].name : ''
    });
}

function loadUsulanMasukPensiunDashboard() {
  const el = document.getElementById('usulanMasukPensiunLevel1');
  el.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Memuat...</div>';
  document.getElementById('usulanMasukPensiunLevel1').style.display = 'grid';
  document.getElementById('usulanMasukPensiunLevel2').style.display = 'none';

  google.script.run

      loadUsulanMasukPensiunBadge();
      if (!res.daftarUnit.length) { el.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Tidak ada usulan pensiun baru.</div>'; return; }
      el.innerHTML = res.daftarUnit.map(u => `
        <button type="button" class="ce-dashboard-card" onclick="showUsulanMasukPensiunLevel2('${escapeHtml(u.unit).replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">
          <div class="cdc-title">${escapeHtml(u.unit)}</div>

          <div class="cdc-sub">usulan baru</div>
        </button>
      `).join('');
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat usulan pensiun masuk: ' + (err.message || err)))

function showUsulanMasukPensiunLevel2(unit) {
  document.getElementById('usulanMasukPensiunLevel1').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('usulanMasukPensiunLevel2').style.display = 'block';

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (!res.success) { showToast('error', res.message); return; }
      const listEl = document.getElementById('usulanMasukPensiunLevel2List');
      if (!res.daftar.length) { listEl.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Tidak ada data.</div>'; return; }
      listEl.innerHTML = res.daftar.map(u => {
        const dokumenLinks = [
          u.fileUrl ? `<a href="${u.fileUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="btn btn-outline kp-small-btn">Surat Pengantar</a>` : '',
          u.fileTambahan1Url ? `<a href="${u.fileTambahan1Url}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="btn btn-outline kp-small-btn">${escapeHtml(u.fileTambahan1Label)}</a>` : '',
          u.fileTambahan2Url ? `<a href="${u.fileTambahan2Url}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="btn btn-outline kp-small-btn">${escapeHtml(u.fileTambahan2Label)}</a>` : ''
        ].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
        return `
        <div class="ce-dashboard-list-item" style="cursor:default; flex-wrap:wrap;">
          <div>
            <div class="cdli-name">${escapeHtml(u.nama)} ${u.dpcpSelesai ? '<span class="cdli-warn" style="background:#d1fae5; color:#065f46;">DPCP ✓</span>' : ''}${u.superSelesai ? ' <span class="cdli-warn" style="background:#d1fae5; color:#065f46;">SUPER ✓</span>' : ''}</div>
            <div class="cdli-meta">NIP ${escapeHtml(u.nip)} &middot; Jenis: ${escapeHtml(u.jenisPensiun)} &middot; Diajukan oleh ${escapeHtml(u.namaPengaju)} &middot; ${escapeHtml(u.tanggalDiajukan)}</div>
            <div style="margin-top:6px; display:flex; gap:6px; flex-wrap:wrap;">${dokumenLinks}</div>
          </div>
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary kp-small-btn" data-nip="${escapeHtml(u.nip)}" data-nama="${escapeHtml(u.nama).replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}" data-action="proses-pensiun">Proses DPCP/SUPER</button>
        </div>
      `;
      }).join('');

      listEl.querySelectorAll('[data-action="proses-pensiun"]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          // Admin/super_admin sudah pasti perannya (tab ini memang khusus
          // mereka) -- onSelectPensiunEmployee tetap lanjut ke form DPCP
          // lengkap seperti biasa (bukan modal usulan, itu khusus 'user').
          onSelectPensiunEmployee(btn.getAttribute('data-nip'), btn.getAttribute('data-nama'));
        });
      });
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat daftar usulan pensiun: ' + (err.message || err)))
    .getUsulanPensiunListByUnit(SESSION.token, unit);

  document.getElementById('usulanMasukPensiunBackBtn').onclick = loadUsulanMasukPensiunDashboard;
}

/* =====================================================================
 * ALUR SK PENSIUN PEGAWAI UNDIP NON ASN (Admin / Super Admin)
 * ===================================================================== */
let skNonAsnUsulanListCache = [];
let skNonAsnNonAsnEmployeesCache = [];
let skNonAsnSelectedEmployee = null;

let _isSkPensiunNonAsnFormBound = false;

function bindSkPensiunNonAsnForm() {
  if (_isSkPensiunNonAsnFormBound) return;
  _isSkPensiunNonAsnFormBound = true;

  const btn = document.getElementById('skNonAsn_generateBtn');
  if (btn) {
    btn.onclick = submitGenerateSkPensiunNonAsn;
  }
  const previewBtn = document.getElementById('skNonAsn_previewBtn');
  if (previewBtn) {
    previewBtn.onclick = submitPreviewSkPensiunNonAsn;
  }

  // Setup Real-time Autocomplete Manual Search via RPC searchEmployees
  const manualInput = document.getElementById('skNonAsn_manualInput');
  const manualList = document.getElementById('skNonAsn_manualList');
  const manualNip = document.getElementById('skNonAsn_manualNip');
  if (manualInput && manualList && manualNip) {
    setupAutocompleteEl(manualInput, manualList, manualNip, (nip, nama) => {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(emp => {
          skNonAsnSelectedEmployee = emp;
          onSkNonAsnFormChange();
        })
        .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat data pegawai: ' + (err.message || err)))
        .getEmployeeFullData(SESSION.token, nip);
    });
  }

  // Pre-load data usulan
  loadSkNonAsnUsulanList();
}

function toggleSkNonAsnSearchMode(mode) {
  const usulanWrap = document.getElementById('skNonAsn_usulanWrap');
  const manualWrap = document.getElementById('skNonAsn_manualWrap');

  if (mode === 'usulan') {
    if (usulanWrap) usulanWrap.style.display = 'block';
    if (manualWrap) manualWrap.style.display = 'none';
    skNonAsnSelectedEmployee = null;
    onSelectSkNonAsnUsulan();
  } else {
    if (usulanWrap) usulanWrap.style.display = 'none';
    if (manualWrap) manualWrap.style.display = 'block';
    skNonAsnSelectedEmployee = null;
    document.getElementById('skNonAsn_manualInput').value = '';
    document.getElementById('skNonAsn_manualNip').value = '';
    onSkNonAsnFormChange();
  }
}

function loadSkNonAsnUsulanList() {
  const select = document.getElementById('skNonAsn_usulanSelect');
  if (!select) return;
  select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Memuat daftar usulan Non-ASN...</option>';

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (!res.success) {
        select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Gagal memuat daftar usulan</option>';
        return;
      }
      skNonAsnUsulanListCache = res.daftar || [];
      if (!skNonAsnUsulanListCache.length) {
        select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Tidak ada usulan Pensiun Non-ASN baru --</option>';
        return;
      }

      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Pegawai dari Usulan User --</option>' +
        skNonAsnUsulanListCache.map(u => `
          <option value="${u.id}">${escapeHtml(u.nama)} (${escapeHtml(u.nip)}) - Jenis: ${escapeHtml(u.jenisPensiun)} - Unit: ${escapeHtml(u.unit || '-')}</option>
        `).join('');
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => {
      select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Gagal memuat usulan</option>';
    })
    .getUsulanPensiunNonAsnList(SESSION.token);
}

function loadSkNonAsnEmployeesList() {
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (res.success) {
        skNonAsnNonAsnEmployeesCache = res.daftar || [];
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(() => {})
    .getNonAsnEmployeesList(SESSION.token);
}

function onSelectSkNonAsnUsulan() {
  const select = document.getElementById('skNonAsn_usulanSelect');
  const infoEl = document.getElementById('skNonAsn_usulanInfo');
  if (!select) return;

  const usulanId = select.value;
  const usulan = skNonAsnUsulanListCache.find(u => String(u.id) === String(usulanId));

  if (usulan) {
    if (infoEl) {
      infoEl.innerHTML = `Diajukan pada: <strong>${escapeHtml(usulan.tanggalDiajukan)}</strong> | Status: <strong>${escapeHtml(usulan.status)}</strong>`;
      infoEl.style.display = 'block';
    }

    if (usulan.jenisPensiun) {
      const jenisEl = document.getElementById('skNonAsn_jenisPensiun');
      if (jenisEl) jenisEl.value = usulan.jenisPensiun;
    }

    // Fetch employee details from DB
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(emp => {
        skNonAsnSelectedEmployee = emp;
        onSkNonAsnFormChange();
      })
      .getEmployeeFullData(SESSION.token, usulan.nip);
  } else {
    if (infoEl) infoEl.style.display = 'none';
    skNonAsnSelectedEmployee = null;
    onSkNonAsnFormChange();
  }
}

function onJenisPensiunChangeSkNonAsn() {
  const jenis = document.getElementById('skNonAsn_jenisPensiun')?.value || '';
  let subMenuTarget = 'Buat SK Pensiun Pegawai Undip Non ASN';
  if (jenis === 'BUP') subMenuTarget = 'SK Pensiun BUP Pegawai Undip Non ASN';
  else if (jenis === 'Meninggal') subMenuTarget = 'SK Pensiun Meninggal Pegawai Undip Non ASN';
  else if (jenis === 'Uzur') subMenuTarget = 'SK Pensiun Uzur Pegawai Undip Non ASN';
  else if (jenis === 'Undur Diri') subMenuTarget = 'SK Pensiun Undur Diri Pegawai Undip Non ASN';

  loadServiceTemplates('Pensiun', subMenuTarget, 'skNonAsn_templateSelect');
  onSkNonAsnFormChange();
}

function onSelectSkNonAsnTemplate() {
  onSkNonAsnFormChange();
}

function onSkNonAsnFormChange() {
  const container = document.getElementById('skNonAsn_dynamicContainer');
  const fieldsDiv = document.getElementById('skNonAsn_dynamicFields');
  const templateId = document.getElementById('skNonAsn_templateSelect')?.value;

  if (!skNonAsnSelectedEmployee || !templateId) {
    if (container) container.style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }

  if (container) container.style.display = 'block';
  fieldsDiv.innerHTML = '<div style="grid-column: 1 / -1; padding:12px; color:var(--text-muted);">Memuat kolom isian template...</div>';

  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      const placeholders = (res && res.placeholders && res.placeholders.length) ? res.placeholders : [
        'nip', 'nama_lengkap', 'unit_es_ii', 'jabatan', 'golongan', 'pangkat',
        'tmp_lhr', 'tgl_lhr', 'tmt_pengangkatan', 'tmt_pensiun_bup', 'status_kepegawaian',
        'nomor_sk', 'foto'
      ];
      renderSkNonAsnDynamicFormFields(placeholders);
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => {
      const fallbackPlaceholders = [
        'nip', 'nama_lengkap', 'unit_es_ii', 'jabatan', 'golongan', 'pangkat',
        'tmp_lhr', 'tgl_lhr', 'tmt_pengangkatan', 'tmt_pensiun_bup', 'status_kepegawaian',
        'nomor_sk', 'foto'
      ];
      renderSkNonAsnDynamicFormFields(fallbackPlaceholders);
    })
    .getTemplatePlaceholders(SESSION.token, templateId);
}

/* =====================================================================
 * HELPER FOTO PLACEHOLDER (Foto Upload)
 * ===================================================================== */
function isPhotoPlaceholder(key) {
  const k = String(key || '').toLowerCase();
  return k === 'foto' || k === 'pas_foto' || k === 'pasfoto' || k === 'foto_pegawai' || k === 'foto_resmi';
}

function handleSkPhotoUpload(fileInput, key) {
  const file = fileInput.files[0];
  if (!file) return;
  if (file.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
    showToast('error', 'Ukuran file foto maksimal 2 MB.');
    fileInput.value = '';
    return;
  }
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = (e) => {
    const hidden = document.getElementById('sk_foto_hidden_' + key);
    const img = document.getElementById('sk_foto_preview_' + key);
    const wrap = document.getElementById('sk_foto_preview_wrap_' + key);
    if (hidden) hidden.value = e.target.result;
    if (img) img.src = e.target.result;
    if (wrap) wrap.style.display = 'block';
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}
