  const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('[data-kontrak-tab]');
  tabs.forEach(tab => {
    tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
      tabs.forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
      tab.classList.add('active');
      const target = tab.getAttribute('data-kontrak-tab');
      
      const adminDirectSec = document.getElementById('kontrakAdminDirectSection');
      const adminReviewSec = document.getElementById('kontrakAdminReviewSection');
      const atasanReviewSec = document.getElementById('kontrakAtasanReviewSection');
      const userPropSec = document.getElementById('kontrakUserProposalSection');
      const userStatusState = document.getElementById('kontrakUserStatusState');
      const userFormState = document.getElementById('kontrakUserFormState');

      if (adminDirectSec) adminDirectSec.style.display = 'none';
      if (adminReviewSec) adminReviewSec.style.display = 'none';
      if (atasanReviewSec) atasanReviewSec.style.display = 'none';
      if (userPropSec) userPropSec.style.display = 'none';

      if (target === 'buat') {
        if (adminDirectSec) adminDirectSec.style.display = 'block';
      } else if (target === 'review') {
        if (adminReviewSec) adminReviewSec.style.display = 'block';
        loadKontrakReviewUnits();
      } else if (target === 'atasan') {
        if (atasanReviewSec) atasanReviewSec.style.display = 'block';
        loadKontrakAtasanReviewList();
      } else if (target === 'user-status') {
        if (userPropSec) userPropSec.style.display = 'block';
        loadUsulanKontrakSaya();
      }
    });
  });
}

async function initKontrakViewForUser() {
  const role = (SESSION.user && SESSION.user.role) || 'normal';
  const isAdmin = (role === 'admin' || role === 'super_admin');
  
  const adminTabs = document.getElementById('kontrakAdminTabs');
  const btnAdminBuat = document.getElementById('tabBtnKontrakAdminBuat');
  const btnAdminReview = document.getElementById('tabBtnKontrakAdminReview');
  const btnAtasan = document.getElementById('tabBtnKontrakAtasan');
  const btnUserStatus = document.getElementById('tabBtnKontrakUserStatus');
  const adminDirectSec = document.getElementById('kontrakAdminDirectSection');
  const userPropSec = document.getElementById('kontrakUserProposalSection');

  if (adminTabs) adminTabs.style.display = 'flex';

  // Set synchronously based on known session role to prevent UI flicker
  if (btnAdminBuat) btnAdminBuat.style.display = isAdmin ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
  if (btnAdminReview) btnAdminReview.style.display = isAdmin ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
  if (btnUserStatus) btnUserStatus.style.display = 'inline-block';

  if (!isAdmin) {
    if (adminDirectSec) adminDirectSec.style.display = 'none';
    if (userPropSec) userPropSec.style.display = 'block';
    
    document.querySelectorAll('#kontrakAdminTabs .tab-btn').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
    if (btnUserStatus) btnUserStatus.classList.add('active');
    
    loadUsulanKontrakSaya();
  } else {
    if (adminDirectSec) adminDirectSec.style.display = 'block';
    if (userPropSec) userPropSec.style.display = 'none';
    
    document.querySelectorAll('#kontrakAdminTabs .tab-btn').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
    if (btnAdminBuat) btnAdminBuat.classList.add('active');
    
    loadKontrakReviewBadge();
  }

  // Request menu access details from backend (for Atasan Langsung role/badge)
  try {
    const resAkses = await rpc('getMenuAksesKontrak', [SESSION.token]);
    if (resAkses && resAkses.success) {
      KONTRAK_AKSES_INFO = resAkses;
      const isAtasan = !!resAkses.isAtasanLangsung;
      const countAtasan = resAkses.countUsulanAtasan || 0;

      if (btnAtasan) {
        if (isAtasan || isAdmin) {
          btnAtasan.style.display = 'inline-block';
          const badge = document.getElementById('kontrakAtasanBadge');
          if (badge) {
            badge.textContent = countAtasan;
            badge.style.display = countAtasan > 0 ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
          }
        } else {
          btnAtasan.style.display = 'none';
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (errAkses) {
    console.warn('[initKontrakViewForUser] notice:', errAkses.message);
  }
}

function openKontrakUserNewForm() {
  const statusState = document.getElementById('kontrakUserStatusState');
  const formState = document.getElementById('kontrakUserFormState');
  const btnBack = document.getElementById('btnBackToKontrakStatus');
  if (statusState) statusState.style.display = 'none';
  if (formState) formState.style.display = 'block';
  if (btnBack) btnBack.style.display = 'inline-block';
  populateKontrakUserFormProfile();
  toggleEvaluasiKinerjaUser();
  onKontrakUserLayananChange();
}

function backToKontrakUserStatus() {
  const statusState = document.getElementById('kontrakUserStatusState');
  const formState = document.getElementById('kontrakUserFormState');
  const btnBack = document.getElementById('btnBackToKontrakStatus');
  if (statusState) statusState.style.display = 'block';
  if (formState) formState.style.display = 'none';
  if (btnBack) btnBack.style.display = 'none';
  loadUsulanKontrakSaya();
}

function toggleEvaluasiKinerjaUser() {
  const jenis = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_jenisUsulan').value;
  const wrap = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_evaluasiWrap');
  if (wrap) {
    wrap.style.display = (jenis === 'Perpanjangan') ? 'block' : 'none';
  }
}

function onKontrakUserLayananChange() {
  const layanan = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_layanan').value;
  const subMenuSelect = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_subMenu');
  const atasanWrap = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_atasanWrap');

  if (atasanWrap) {
    atasanWrap.style.display = (layanan === 'Kontrak Tendik') ? 'block' : 'none';
    if (layanan === 'Kontrak Tendik') {
      bindAtasanLangsungSearch();
    }
  }
  
  if (!subMenuSelect) return;
  
  if (!layanan) {
    subMenuSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Pilih Kategori Pegawai Dahulu...</option>';
    return;
  }
  
  const list = (layanan === 'Kontrak Tendik') 
    ? (kontrakFormOptionsCache.subMenuTendik || ['Tenaga Profesional', 'Kontrak Penuh Waktu', 'Kontrak Paruh Waktu', 'Tenaga Kontrak Penghargaan']) 
    : (kontrakFormOptionsCache.subMenuDosen || ['Kontrak Penuh Waktu', 'Kontrak Paruh Waktu', 'Tenaga Kontrak Penghargaan']);
    
  subMenuSelect.innerHTML = list.map(item => `<option value="${escapeHtml(item)}">${escapeHtml(item)}</option>`).join('');
}

function bindAtasanLangsungSearch() {
  const input = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_atasanSearch');
  const listEl = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_atasanSearchList');
  if (!input || !listEl || input._bound) return;
  input._bound = true;

  input.addEventListener('input', e => {
    clearTimeout(atasanSearchTimer);
    const q = e.target.value.trim();
    if (q.length < 2) {
      listEl.style.display = 'none';
      listEl.innerHTML = '';
      return;
    }
    atasanSearchTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        const res = await rpc('searchAtasanLangsung', [SESSION.token, q]);
        if (res && res.success && res.data && res.data.length > 0) {
          listEl.innerHTML = res.data.map(at => `
            <div class="autocomplete-item" style="padding:10px; cursor:pointer; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;" onclick="pilihAtasanLangsung('${escapeHtml(at.nip)}', '${escapeHtml(at.nama)}', '${escapeHtml(at.unit)}')">
              <strong>${escapeHtml(at.nama)}</strong>
              <div style="font-size:12px; color:#666;">NIP: ${escapeHtml(at.nip)} &bull; ${escapeHtml(at.unit)}</div>
            </div>
          `).join('');
          listEl.style.display = 'block';
        } else {
          listEl.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:10px; color:#999; font-size:12px;">Tidak ditemukan pegawai dengan NIP/Nama tersebut.</div>';
          listEl.style.display = 'block';
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('searchAtasanLangsung error:', err);
      }
    }, 250);
  });
}

function pilihAtasanLangsung(nip, nama, unit) {
  document.getElementById('kontrakUser_atasanNip').value = nip;
  document.getElementById('kontrakUser_atasanNama').value = nama;
  document.getElementById('kontrakUser_atasanSearch').value = `${nama} (${nip})`;
  document.getElementById('kontrakUser_atasanSearchList').style.display = 'none';
  
  const selDiv = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_atasanSelected');
  if (selDiv) {
    selDiv.innerHTML = `✅ Atasan Langsung Terpilih: <strong>${escapeHtml(nama)}</strong> (NIP: ${escapeHtml(nip)}) - ${escapeHtml(unit)}`;
    selDiv.style.display = 'block';
  }
}

// Check medical keywords
function isJabatanMedis(jabatan) {
  const medKeywords = ["dokter", "perawat", "bidan", "apoteker", "perekam", "radiografer", "sanitarian", "psikolog", "fisioterapis", "nutrisionis", "terapis", "medis"];
  const job = String(jabatan || '').toLowerCase();
  return medKeywords.some(kw => job.includes(kw));
}

function fileToBase64(file) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result);
    reader.onerror = error => reject(error);
  });
}

async function submitUsulanKontrak(e) {
  e.preventDefault();
  const btn = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_submitBtn');
  setBtnLoading(btn, true, 'Mengajukan...');
  
  try {
    const nip = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_nip').value;
    const nama = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_nama').value;
    const unit = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_unit').value;
    const email = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_email').value;
    const tahun = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_tahun').value;
    const jenis_usulan = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_jenisUsulan').value;
    const evaluasi_kinerja = (jenis_usulan === 'Perpanjangan') ? document.getElementById('kontrakUser_evaluasiKinerja').value : '';
    const layanan = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_layanan').value;
    const sub_menu = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_subMenu').value;
    const atasan_nip = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_atasanNip') ? document.getElementById('kontrakUser_atasanNip').value : '';

    if (layanan === 'Kontrak Tendik' && !atasan_nip) {
      setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Ajukan Usulan');
      showToast('error', 'Silakan cari dan pilih Atasan Langsung penilai evaluasi kinerja Anda terlebih dahulu.');
      return;
    }
    
    // File elements
    const ktpInput = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_file_ktp');
    const kkInput = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_file_kk');
    const pasFotoInput = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_file_pas_foto');
    const ijazahInput = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_file_ijazah');
    const suratPengantarInput = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_file_surat_pengantar');
    const suratLamaranInput = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_file_surat_lamaran');
    const ketSehatInput = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_file_ket_sehat');
    const simInput = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_file_sim');
    const strInput = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_file_str');
    
    const payload = {
      nip, nama, unit, email, tahun, jenis_usulan, evaluasi_kinerja, layanan, sub_menu, atasan_nip,
      form_data: {
        nip, nama_lengkap: nama, unit_es_ii: unit, jabatan: document.getElementById('kontrakUser_jabatan').value, email, atasan_nip
      }
    };
    
    // Read files
    if (ktpInput && ktpInput.files.length) {
      payload.ktpBase64 = await fileToBase64(ktpInput.files[0]);
      payload.ktpNama = ktpInput.files[0].name;
    }
    if (kkInput && kkInput.files.length) {
      payload.kkBase64 = await fileToBase64(kkInput.files[0]);
      payload.kkNama = kkInput.files[0].name;
    }
    if (pasFotoInput && pasFotoInput.files.length) {
      payload.pasFotoBase64 = await fileToBase64(pasFotoInput.files[0]);
      payload.pasFotoNama = pasFotoInput.files[0].name;
    }
    if (ijazahInput && ijazahInput.files.length) {
      payload.ijazahBase64 = await fileToBase64(ijazahInput.files[0]);
      payload.ijazahNama = ijazahInput.files[0].name;
    }
    if (suratPengantarInput && suratPengantarInput.files.length) {
      payload.suratPengantarBase64 = await fileToBase64(suratPengantarInput.files[0]);
      payload.suratPengantarNama = suratPengantarInput.files[0].name;
    }
    if (suratLamaranInput && suratLamaranInput.files.length) {
      payload.suratLamaranBase64 = await fileToBase64(suratLamaranInput.files[0]);
      payload.suratLamaranNama = suratLamaranInput.files[0].name;
    }
    if (ketSehatInput && ketSehatInput.files.length) {
      payload.ketSehatBase64 = await fileToBase64(ketSehatInput.files[0]);
      payload.ketSehatNama = ketSehatInput.files[0].name;
    }
    if (simInput && simInput.files.length) {
      payload.simAbBase64 = await fileToBase64(simInput.files[0]);
      payload.simAbNama = simInput.files[0].name;
    }
    if (strInput && strInput.files.length) {
      payload.strAktifBase64 = await fileToBase64(strInput.files[0]);
      payload.strAktifNama = strInput.files[0].name;
    }

    const rpcMethod = (layanan === 'Kontrak Tendik') ? 'ajukanUsulanKontrakTendik' : 'ajukanUsulanKontrak';
    const res = await rpc(rpcMethod, [SESSION.token, payload]);
    setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Ajukan Usulan');
    if (res && res.success) {
      showToast('success', res.message);
      const tabStatus = document.getElementById('tabBtnKontrakUserStatus');
      if (tabStatus) tabStatus.click();
      else loadUsulanKontrakSaya();
    } else {
      showToast('error', res?.message || 'Gagal mengajukan usulan kontrak.');
    }
  } catch (err) {
    setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Ajukan Usulan');
    showToast('error', 'Terjadi kesalahan: ' + (err.message || err));
  }
}

async function populateKontrakUserFormProfile() {
  const role = (SESSION.user && SESSION.user.role) ? SESSION.user.role : 'normal';
  const isNormalUser = role === 'normal' || role === 'user' || !['admin', 'super_admin'].includes(role);
  
  // Hide search box for normal user
  const searchWrap = document.querySelector('#kontrakUserForm .autocomplete-wrap');
  if (searchWrap) {
    searchWrap.style.display = isNormalUser ? 'none' : 'block';
  }

  let profile = SESSION.user || {};
  const userNip = profile.nip || SESSION.nip || '';

  if (userNip) {
    try {
      const res = await rpc('getEmployeeFullData', [SESSION.token, userNip]);
      if (res && (res.data || res.employee || res.nip)) {
        const empData = res.data || res.employee || res;
        profile = Object.assign({}, profile, empData);
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  const nipEl = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_nip');
  const namaEl = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_nama');
  const unitEl = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_unit');
  const jabEl = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_jabatan');
  const emailEl = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_email');
  const tahunEl = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_tahun');
  const layEl = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_layanan');
  const subMenuEl = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_subMenu');

  const nipVal = profile.nip || profile.username || '';
  const namaVal = profile.nama || profile.nama_lengkap || '';
  const unitVal = profile.unit_es_ii || profile.unit || profile.unit_kerja || '';
  const jabVal = profile.jabatan || '';
  const emailVal = profile.email || profile.email_kontak || '';

  function setFieldLock(el, val, shouldLock) {
    if (!el) return;
    el.value = val || '';
    if (shouldLock && val) {
      el.readOnly = true;
      el.style.backgroundColor = 'var(--surface-alt)';
      el.style.color = '#000000';
      el.style.webkitTextFillColor = '#000000';
      el.style.fontWeight = '700';
      el.style.cursor = 'not-allowed';
    } else {
      el.readOnly = false;
      el.style.backgroundColor = '';
      el.style.color = '';
      el.style.webkitTextFillColor = '';
      el.style.fontWeight = '';
      el.style.cursor = '';
    }
  }

  setFieldLock(nipEl, nipVal, isNormalUser);
  setFieldLock(namaEl, namaVal, isNormalUser);
  setFieldLock(unitEl, unitVal, isNormalUser);
  setFieldLock(jabEl, jabVal, isNormalUser);
  setFieldLock(emailEl, emailVal, isNormalUser);

  if (tahunEl) {
    tahunEl.value = new Date().getFullYear();
  }

  // Auto-detect & Lock Kategori Pegawai (Layanan = jenis_peg) & Jenis Perjanjian Kerja (SubMenu = status_kepegawaian)
  const jenisPeg = String(profile.jenis_peg || profile.jenis_pegawai || jabVal || '').toLowerCase();
  const statusKep = String(profile.status_kepegawaian || profile.status_pegawai || profile.status_bekerja || '').toLowerCase();

  if (layEl) {
    if (jenisPeg.includes('dosen')) {
      layEl.value = 'Kontrak Dosen';
    } else {
      layEl.value = 'Kontrak Tendik';
    }
    onKontrakUserLayananChange();

    if (isNormalUser && (profile.jenis_peg || profile.jenis_pegawai || jabVal)) {
      layEl.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
      layEl.style.backgroundColor = 'var(--surface-alt)';
      layEl.style.color = '#000000';
      layEl.style.webkitTextFillColor = '#000000';
      layEl.style.fontWeight = '700';
    } else {
      layEl.style.pointerEvents = '';
      layEl.style.backgroundColor = '';
      layEl.style.color = '';
      layEl.style.webkitTextFillColor = '';
      layEl.style.fontWeight = '';
    }
  }

  if (subMenuEl && subMenuEl.options.length > 0) {
    if (statusKep) {
      for (let i = 0; i < subMenuEl.options.length; i++) {
        const optText = subMenuEl.options[i].text.toLowerCase();
        const optVal = subMenuEl.options[i].value.toLowerCase();
        if (optText.includes(statusKep) || optVal.includes(statusKep) || statusKep.includes(optVal)) {
          subMenuEl.selectedIndex = i;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (isNormalUser && (profile.status_kepegawaian || profile.status_pegawai || profile.status_bekerja)) {
      subMenuEl.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
      subMenuEl.style.backgroundColor = 'var(--surface-alt)';
      subMenuEl.style.color = '#000000';
      subMenuEl.style.webkitTextFillColor = '#000000';
      subMenuEl.style.fontWeight = '700';
    } else {
      subMenuEl.style.pointerEvents = '';
      subMenuEl.style.backgroundColor = '';
      subMenuEl.style.color = '';
      subMenuEl.style.webkitTextFillColor = '';
      subMenuEl.style.fontWeight = '';
    }
  }

  // Sim & Str wraps
  const isDriver = String(jabVal || '').toLowerCase().includes('pengemudi');
  const isMedis = isJabatanMedis(jabVal);

  const simWrap = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_simWrap');
  const simInput = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_file_sim');
  if (simWrap) {
    simWrap.style.display = isDriver ? 'block' : 'none';
    if (simInput) {
      if (isDriver) simInput.setAttribute('required', 'required');
      else simInput.removeAttribute('required');
    }
  }

  const strWrap = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_strWrap');
  const strInput = document.getElementById('kontrakUser_file_str');
  if (strWrap) {
    strWrap.style.display = isMedis ? 'block' : 'none';
    if (strInput) {
      if (isMedis) strInput.setAttribute('required', 'required');
      else strInput.removeAttribute('required');
    }
  }
}

async function loadUsulanKontrakSaya() {
  const statusState = document.getElementById('kontrakUserStatusState');
  const formState = document.getElementById('kontrakUserFormState');
  if (!statusState || !formState) return;
  
  statusState.style.display = 'none';
  formState.style.display = 'none';
  
  try {
    const res = await rpc('getUsulanKontrakSaya', [SESSION.token]);
    if (res && res.success) {
      handleUsulanKontrakSayaResult(res);
      return;
    }
  } catch (err) {}

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(res => handleUsulanKontrakSayaResult(res))
      .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', err.message || err))
      .getUsulanKontrakSaya(SESSION.token);
  }
}

function handleUsulanKontrakSayaResult(res) {
  const statusState = document.getElementById('kontrakUserStatusState');
  const formState = document.getElementById('kontrakUserFormState');
  if (!statusState || !formState) return;

  if (!res || !res.success) {
    showToast('error', res?.message || 'Gagal memuat usulan kontrak.');
    return;
  }
  
  if (!res.usulan) {
    // Show Form & Populate Profile with locks
    formState.style.display = 'block';
    populateKontrakUserFormProfile();
    toggleEvaluasiKinerjaUser();
    onKontrakUserLayananChange();
  } else {
    // Show Status
    statusState.style.display = 'block';
    const card = document.getElementById('kontrakUserStatusCard');
    
    let statusBadge = `<span class="badge" style="background:#ff9800; color:#fff; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:6px; font-weight:600;">Diajukan</span>`;
    const st = res.usulan.status;
    if (st === 'submitted_to_atasan') {
      statusBadge = `<span class="badge" style="background:#f59e0b; color:#fff; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:6px; font-weight:600;">⏳ Menunggu Evaluasi Atasan Langsung</span>`;
    } else if (st === 'under_atasan_review') {
      statusBadge = `<span class="badge" style="background:#3b82f6; color:#fff; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:6px; font-weight:600;">🔍 Sedang Ditinjau Atasan Langsung</span>`;
    } else if (st === 'evaluated_extend') {
      statusBadge = `<span class="badge" style="background:#8b5cf6; color:#fff; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:6px; font-weight:600;">📋 Dievaluasi Atasan (Rekomendasi Diperpanjang) - Menunggu Validasi Admin</span>`;
    } else if (st === 'evaluated_not_extend') {
      statusBadge = `<span class="badge" style="background:#ef4444; color:#fff; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:6px; font-weight:600;">❌ Evaluasi Selesai (Rekomendasi Tidak Diperpanjang)</span>`;
    } else if (st === 'validated_by_admin' || st === 'Disetujui') {
      statusBadge = `<span class="badge" style="background:#10b981; color:#fff; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:6px; font-weight:600;">✅ Divalidasi Admin (Siap Buat Dokumen Perjanjian)</span>`;
    } else if (st === 'contract_generated' || st === 'Selesai' || !!res.usulan.perjanjian_dibuat) {
      statusBadge = `<span class="badge" style="background:#2563eb; color:#fff; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:6px; font-weight:600;">🎉 Perjanjian Kerja Selesai Dibuat</span>`;
    } else if (st === 'closed_not_extended' || st === 'Ditolak') {
      statusBadge = `<span class="badge" style="background:#64748b; color:#fff; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:6px; font-weight:600;">🚫 Selesai (Tidak Diperpanjang)</span>`;
    }
    
    let generateBtnHtml = '';
    let readOnlyCardHtml = '';

    const isApprovedOrCompleted = (st === 'validated_by_admin' || st === 'Disetujui' || st === 'Selesai' || st === 'contract_generated' || !!res.usulan.perjanjian_dibuat);
    if (isApprovedOrCompleted) {
      const u = res.usulan;
      const fd = u.form_data || {};

      let savedTmtBulan = normalizeMonth(u.tmt_bulan || fd.tmt_bulan);
      let savedTmtTahun = u.tmt_tahun || fd.tmt_tahun || '';
      let savedTstBulan = normalizeMonth(u.tst_bulan || fd.tst_bulan);
      let savedTstTahun = u.tst_tahun || fd.tst_tahun || '';

      if (!savedTmtBulan || !savedTmtTahun || !savedTstBulan || !savedTstTahun) {
        const parsed = (typeof parsePeriodeKontrakString === 'function') ? parsePeriodeKontrakString(u.periode_kontrak || fd.periode_kontrak || '') : { tmtBulan:'', tmtTahun:'', tstBulan:'', tstTahun:'' };
        if (!savedTmtBulan) savedTmtBulan = normalizeMonth(parsed.tmtBulan);
        if (!savedTmtTahun) savedTmtTahun = parsed.tmtTahun;
        if (!savedTstBulan) savedTstBulan = normalizeMonth(parsed.tstBulan);
        if (!savedTstTahun) savedTstTahun = parsed.tstTahun;
      }

      const BULAN_NAMES = [
        'Januari', 'Februari', 'Maret', 'April', 'Mei', 'Juni',
        'Juli', 'Agustus', 'September', 'Oktober', 'November', 'Desember'
      ];
      const years = [];
      const currentYr = new Date().getFullYear();
      for (let y = currentYr - 2; y <= currentYr + 8; y++) {
        years.push(y);
      }

      let jangkaWaktuStr = 'Otomatis dari TMT & TST di atas';
      if (savedTmtBulan && savedTmtTahun && savedTstBulan && savedTstTahun) {
        const selisihBulan = (parseInt(savedTstTahun, 10) - parseInt(savedTmtTahun, 10)) * 12 + (parseInt(savedTstBulan, 10) - parseInt(savedTmtBulan, 10)) + 1;
        if (selisihBulan > 0) {
          jangkaWaktuStr = (selisihBulan === 12) ? '1 (satu) tahun' : `${selisihBulan} bulan`;
        }
      }

      const pendStr = String(fd.pendidikan || u.pendidikan || '').toUpperCase().replace(/[\s.\-]/g, '');
      let besaranUpahStr = 'Pendidikan belum dikenali (harus S2/Sp1 atau S3/Sp2) — besaran upah tidak bisa dihitung otomatis.';
      if (pendStr === 'S2' || pendStr === 'SP1') {
        besaranUpahStr = 'Rp 2.903.600,- (dua juta sembilan ratus tiga ribu enam ratus rupiah)';
      } else if (pendStr === 'S3' || pendStr === 'SP2') {
        besaranUpahStr = 'Rp 3.026.400,- (tiga juta dua puluh enam ribu empat ratus rupiah)';
      }

      const isDosenParuhWaktu = (u.layanan === 'Kontrak Dosen' && u.sub_menu === 'Kontrak Paruh Waktu');
      const besaranUpahHtml = isDosenParuhWaktu ? `
            <div class="stack-field">
              <label>BESARAN UPAH</label>
              <input type="text" value="${escapeHtml(besaranUpahStr)}" readonly disabled class="field-input-dark" />
            </div>` : '';

      const isAlreadySelesai = (res.usulan.status === 'Selesai' || res.usulan.status === 'contract_generated' || !!res.usulan.perjanjian_dibuat);
      const genBtnText = isAlreadySelesai ? '🔄 Generate Ulang Dokumen Perjanjian Kerja' : '🚀 Buat Dokumen Perjanjian Kerja Final';

      readOnlyCardHtml = `
        <div class="kontrak-form-wrap" style="margin-top:24px; padding:24px; background:var(--surface); border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:12px;">
          <h3 style="margin-top:0; margin-bottom:18px; color:var(--text); border-bottom:1px solid var(--border); padding-bottom:10px;">
            📝 Formulir Perjanjian Kerja / Kontrak
          </h3>

          <!-- CARI PEGAWAI (INFO DISPLAY) -->
          <div class="stack-field" style="margin-bottom:16px;">
            <label>CARI PEGAWAI</label>
            <input type="text" value="${escapeHtml((u.nama || SESSION.user?.nama || SESSION.user?.nama_lengkap || '') + ' (' + (u.nip || SESSION.user?.nip || '') + ')')}" readonly disabled class="field-input-dark" />
          </div>

          <div class="grid-2 kp-grid" style="display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(260px, 1fr)); gap:16px; margin-bottom:16px;">
            <div class="stack-field">
              <label>NIP/NIK</label>
              <input type="text" value="${escapeHtml(u.nip || SESSION.user?.nip || '')}" readonly disabled class="field-input-dark" />
            </div>
            <div class="stack-field">
              <label>NAMA LENGKAP</label>
              <input type="text" value="${escapeHtml(u.nama || SESSION.user?.nama || SESSION.user?.nama_lengkap || '')}" readonly disabled class="field-input-dark" />
            </div>
            <div class="stack-field">
              <label>TEMPAT LAHIR</label>
              <input type="text" value="${escapeHtml(fd.tmp_lhr || u.tempat_lahir || SESSION.user?.tmp_lhr || SESSION.user?.tempat_lahir || '-')}" readonly disabled class="field-input-dark" />
            </div>
            <div class="stack-field">
              <label>TANGGAL LAHIR</label>
              <input type="text" value="${escapeHtml(fd.tgl_lhr || u.tanggal_lahir || SESSION.user?.tgl_lhr || SESSION.user?.tanggal_lahir || '-')}" readonly disabled class="field-input-dark" />
            </div>
            <div class="stack-field">
              <label>PENDIDIKAN</label>
              <input type="text" value="${escapeHtml(fd.pendidikan || u.pendidikan || SESSION.user?.pendidikan || '-')}" readonly disabled class="field-input-dark" />
            </div>
            <div class="stack-field">
              <label>JURUSAN</label>
              <input type="text" value="${escapeHtml(fd.jurusan || u.jurusan || SESSION.user?.jurusan || '-')}" readonly disabled class="field-input-dark" />
            </div>
            <div class="stack-field">
              <label>UNIT KERJA</label>
              <input type="text" value="${escapeHtml(u.unit || SESSION.user?.unit_es_ii || '-')}" readonly disabled class="field-input-dark" />
            </div>
            <div class="stack-field">
              <label>JABATAN</label>
              <input type="text" value="${escapeHtml(fd.jabatan || u.jabatan || '-')}" readonly disabled class="field-input-dark" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- ALAMAT (EDITABLE BY USER) -->
          <div class="stack-field" style="margin-bottom:16px;">
            <label>ALAMAT <span style="color:#ef4444;">*</span></label>
            <textarea id="usulan_alamat_${u.id}" class="field-input-dark" rows="2" placeholder="Ketik alamat lengkap...">${escapeHtml(fd.alamat || u.alamat || '')}</textarea>
            <div style="font-size:11px; color:#64748b; margin-top:4px;">*Dapat Anda ubah/lengkapi bila perlu sebelum generate.</div>
          </div>

          <!-- NOMOR TELEPON & EMAIL (EDITABLE BY USER) & NOMOR SURAT -->
          <div class="grid-2 kp-grid" style="display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(260px, 1fr)); gap:16px; margin-bottom:16px;">
            <div class="stack-field">
              <label>NOMOR TELEPON</label>
              <input type="text" id="usulan_nomorTelepon_${u.id}" class="field-input-dark" value="${escapeHtml(fd.nomor_telepon || u.nomor_telepon || '')}" placeholder="Ketik nomor telepon..." />
            </div>
            <div class="stack-field">
              <label>EMAIL</label>
              <input type="text" id="usulan_email_${u.id}" class="field-input-dark" value="${escapeHtml(fd.email || u.email || SESSION.user?.email || '')}" placeholder="Ketik email..." />
            </div>
            <div class="stack-field grid-full" style="grid-column: 1 / -1;">
              <label>NOMOR SURAT PERJANJIAN (OPSIONAL)</label>
              <input type="text" id="usulan_nomorSurat_${u.id}" class="field-input-dark" value="${escapeHtml(u.nomor_surat || fd.nomor_surat || fd.nomor_surat_perjanjian || '')}" placeholder="Kosongkan bila belum ada" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- TMT & TST KONTRAK (PRESET ADMIN) -->
          <div class="grid-2 kp-grid" style="display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(260px, 1fr)); gap:16px; margin-bottom:16px;">
            <div class="stack-field">
              <label>TMT KONTRAK (MULAI)</label>
              <div style="display:flex; gap:10px;">
                <select id="usulan_tmtBulan_${u.id}" class="kontrak-select-dark" disabled style="opacity:1; cursor:not-allowed;">
                  <option value="">Bulan...</option>
                  ${BULAN_NAMES.map((b, i) => `<option value="${i+1}" ${String(savedTmtBulan) === String(i+1) ? 'selected' : ''}>${b}</option>`).join('')}
                </select>
                <select id="usulan_tmtTahun_${u.id}" class="kontrak-select-dark" disabled style="opacity:1; cursor:not-allowed;">
                  <option value="">Tahun...</option>
                  ${years.map(y => `<option value="${y}" ${String(savedTmtTahun) === String(y) ? 'selected' : ''}>${y}</option>`).join('')}
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="stack-field">
              <label>TST KONTRAK (SELESAI)</label>
              <div style="display:flex; gap:10px;">
                <select id="usulan_tstBulan_${u.id}" class="kontrak-select-dark" disabled style="opacity:1; cursor:not-allowed;">
                  <option value="">Bulan...</option>
                  ${BULAN_NAMES.map((b, i) => `<option value="${i+1}" ${String(savedTstBulan) === String(i+1) ? 'selected' : ''}>${b}</option>`).join('')}
                </select>
                <select id="usulan_tstTahun_${u.id}" class="kontrak-select-dark" disabled style="opacity:1; cursor:not-allowed;">
                  <option value="">Tahun...</option>
                  ${years.map(y => `<option value="${y}" ${String(savedTstTahun) === String(y) ? 'selected' : ''}>${y}</option>`).join('')}
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="stack-field">
              <label>JANGKA WAKTU KONTRAK</label>
              <input type="text" value="${escapeHtml(jangkaWaktuStr)}" readonly disabled class="field-input-dark" />
            </div>
            ${besaranUpahHtml}
          </div>

          <!-- TEMPLATE SELECTOR & ACTIONS -->
          <div class="stack-field" style="margin-top:20px; margin-bottom:16px;">
            <label style="font-weight:700; margin-bottom:8px; display:block;">PILIH TIPE & TEMPLATE KONTRAK</label>
            <div id="usulan_tplSelectorWrap_${u.id}"></div>
          </div>

          <div class="actions-bar" style="display:flex; justify-content:flex-end; gap:12px; margin-top:20px; border-top:1px solid var(--border); padding-top:16px;">
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline" style="padding:10px 20px; font-weight:600;" onclick="previewKontrakUserSelf('${u.id}')">👁️ Preview</button>
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" id="usulan_genBtn_${u.id}" style="padding:10px 24px; font-weight:600;" onclick="generateKontrakUserSelf('${u.id}')">${genBtnText}</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    const nipVal = SESSION.user?.nip || res.usulan.nip || '';
    const namaVal = SESSION.user?.nama || SESSION.user?.nama_lengkap || res.usulan.nama || '';

    const evaluasiInfoHtml = res.usulan.evaluasi_skor ? `
      <div style="background:var(--surface-alt); border:2px solid var(--primary); border-radius:10px; padding:16px; margin-top:16px; margin-bottom:16px;">
        <div style="font-weight:700; color:var(--primary); font-size:15px; margin-bottom:8px;">
          📋 Hasil Evaluasi Kinerja (Atasan Langsung: ${escapeHtml(res.usulan.atasan_nama || res.usulan.atasan_nip || '-')})
        </div>
        <div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr)); gap:10px; font-size:13.5px;">
          <div><strong>Total Skor (7 Kriteria):</strong> <span style="font-size:16px; font-weight:800; color:var(--primary);">${escapeHtml(res.usulan.evaluasi_skor)} / 21</span></div>
          <div><strong>Rekomendasi:</strong> <span style="font-weight:700; color:${res.usulan.evaluasi_skor > 10 ? '#16a34a' : '#dc2626'};">${escapeHtml(res.usulan.evaluasi_rekomendasi || '-')}</span></div>
        </div>
        ${res.usulan.evaluasi_doc_url ? `
          <div style="margin-top:12px;">
            <a href="${res.usulan.evaluasi_doc_url}" target="_blank" class="btn btn-outline kp-small-btn" style="color:var(--primary); border-color:var(--primary); font-weight:600;">
              📥 Unduh Dokumen Formulir Evaluasi (.docx)
            </a>
          </div>` : ''}
      </div>
    ` : '';
    
    card.innerHTML = `
      <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; border-bottom:1px solid #eee; padding-bottom:10px; margin-bottom:14px; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:8px;">
        <div><strong>Tahun Kontrak:</strong> ${escapeHtml(res.usulan.tahun)}</div>
        <div>${statusBadge}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="grid-2" style="font-size:14px; gap:10px; margin-bottom:16px;">
        <div><strong>NIP:</strong> ${escapeHtml(nipVal)}</div>
        <div><strong>Nama:</strong> ${escapeHtml(namaVal)}</div>
        <div><strong>Jenis Usulan:</strong> ${escapeHtml(res.usulan.jenis_usulan)}</div>
        <div><strong>Tanggal Diajukan:</strong> ${escapeHtml(res.usulan.tanggal_diajukan)}</div>
        <div><strong>Kategori:</strong> ${escapeHtml(res.usulan.layanan)}</div>
        <div><strong>Jenis Kontrak:</strong> ${escapeHtml(res.usulan.sub_menu)}</div>
      </div>

      ${evaluasiInfoHtml}
      
      <h4 style="border-bottom:1px solid #eee; padding-bottom:6px; margin-bottom:10px;">Status Persetujuan Lampiran</h4>
      <div class="ce-dashboard-list" style="gap:8px;">
        ${(res.usulan.lampiran || []).map(l => {
          const label = String(l.key || '').replace(/_/g,' ').replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase());
          const icon = l.approved ? `<span style="color:#4caf50; font-weight:bold;">✅ Disetujui</span>` : `<span style="color:#ff9800; font-weight:bold;">⏳ Menunggu Review</span>`;
          return `
            <div class="ce-dashboard-list-item" style="cursor:default; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; padding:10px 14px;">
              <div>📄 ${label}</div>
              <div>${icon}</div>
            </div>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>

      ${readOnlyCardHtml}
    `;

    if (isApprovedOrCompleted) {
      renderTemplateSelectorForUsulan(res.usulan.layanan, res.usulan.sub_menu, 'usulan_tplSelectorWrap_' + res.usulan.id, null, null, res.usulan.id);
    }
  }
}

async function previewKontrakUserSelf(usulanId) {
  const selectEl = document.getElementById(`usulan_templateSelect_${usulanId}`);
  const templateRef = selectEl ? selectEl.value : '';
  if (!templateRef) { showToast('error', 'Pilih template terlebih dahulu.'); return; }

  const customFormData = {
    alamat: (document.getElementById(`usulan_alamat_${usulanId}`)?.value || '').trim(),
    nomor_telepon: (document.getElementById(`usulan_nomorTelepon_${usulanId}`)?.value || '').trim(),
    email: (document.getElementById(`usulan_email_${usulanId}`)?.value || '').trim(),
    nomor_surat_perjanjian: (document.getElementById(`usulan_nomorSurat_${usulanId}`)?.value || '').trim()
  };

  showToast('info', 'Membuat preview Dokumen Perjanjian Kerja...');
  
  try {
    const res = await rpc('generateKontrakFromUsulanVercel', [SESSION.token, templateRef, usulanId, customFormData]);
    if (res && res.success) {
      showToast('success', res.message || 'Preview dokumen berhasil dibuat.');
      const viewUrl = res.pdfUrl || res.pdfViewUrl || res.viewUrl || res.docViewUrl;
      if (res.base64) {
        const blobUrl = convertBase64ToBlobUrl_(res.base64, 'application/pdf');
        openPreviewModal(blobUrl);
      } else if (viewUrl) {
        openPreviewModal(viewUrl);
      } else {
        showToast('error', 'URL preview dokumen tidak ditemukan.');
      }
      return;
    } else if (res && res.success === false) {
      showToast('error', res.message || 'Gagal preview Kontrak.');
      return;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    showToast('error', 'Gagal membuat preview: ' + (err.message || err));
  }
}

/* ------------------------- ATASAN LANGSUNG REVIEW & EVALUASI TKK ------------------------- */

let activeEvaluasiUsulanId = null;
let activeEvaluasiDetail = null;
let evalTkkCanvas = null;
let evalTkkCtx = null;
let evalTkkIsDrawing = false;
let evalTkkHasDrawn = false;

async function loadKontrakAtasanReviewList() {
  const el = document.getElementById('kontrakAtasanReviewList');
  if (!el) return;
  el.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Memuat daftar usulan bawahan...</div>';

  try {
    const res = await rpc('getUsulanKontrakAtasanLangsung', [SESSION.token]);
    if (res && res.success) {
      renderKontrakAtasanReviewList(res.daftar || []);
      const badge = document.getElementById('kontrakAtasanBadge');
      if (badge) {
        const pendingCount = (res.daftar || []).filter(u => u.status === 'submitted_to_atasan' || u.status === 'under_atasan_review').length;
        badge.textContent = pendingCount;
        badge.style.display = pendingCount > 0 ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
      }
    } else {
      el.innerHTML = `<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">${escapeHtml(res?.message || 'Gagal memuat usulan.')}</div>`;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Terjadi kesalahan: ${escapeHtml(err.message || err)}</div>`;
  }
}

function renderKontrakAtasanReviewList(daftar) {
  const el = document.getElementById('kontrakAtasanReviewList');
  if (!el) return;
  if (!daftar.length) {
    el.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Belum ada usulan perpanjangan kontrak dari pegawai/bawahan yang memerlukan penilaian Anda.</div>';
    return;
  }

  el.innerHTML = daftar.map(u => {
    let badgeHtml = `<span class="badge" style="background:#f59e0b; color:#fff; padding:4px 8px; border-radius:4px; font-size:12px;">⏳ Belum Dinilai</span>`;
    let actionBtnText = '✍️ Lakukan Penilaian Kinerja';
    let actionBtnClass = 'btn btn-primary';

    if (u.status === 'under_atasan_review') {
      badgeHtml = `<span class="badge" style="background:#3b82f6; color:#fff; padding:4px 8px; border-radius:4px; font-size:12px;">🔍 Sedang Ditinjau</span>`;
    } else if (u.status === 'evaluated_extend') {
      badgeHtml = `<span class="badge" style="background:#8b5cf6; color:#fff; padding:4px 8px; border-radius:4px; font-size:12px;">✅ Selesai Dinilai (Diperpanjang)</span>`;
      actionBtnText = '🔄 Ubah / Buka Penilaian';
      actionBtnClass = 'btn btn-outline';
    } else if (u.status === 'evaluated_not_extend') {
      badgeHtml = `<span class="badge" style="background:#ef4444; color:#fff; padding:4px 8px; border-radius:4px; font-size:12px;">❌ Selesai Dinilai (Tidak Diperpanjang)</span>`;
      actionBtnText = '🔄 Ubah / Buka Penilaian';
      actionBtnClass = 'btn btn-outline';
    } else if (u.status === 'validated_by_admin' || u.status === 'contract_generated' || u.status === 'Disetujui' || u.status === 'Selesai') {
      badgeHtml = `<span class="badge" style="background:#10b981; color:#fff; padding:4px 8px; border-radius:4px; font-size:12px;">🎉 Divalidasi Admin</span>`;
      actionBtnText = '👁️ Lihat Formulir Evaluasi';
      actionBtnClass = 'btn btn-secondary';
    }

    return `
      <div class="ce-dashboard-list-item" style="cursor:default; flex-wrap:wrap; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; gap:12px;">
        <div style="flex:1; min-width:260px;">
          <div class="cdli-name" style="font-size:15px; font-weight:700;">${escapeHtml(u.nama || '-')}</div>
          <div class="cdli-meta" style="font-size:12.5px; color:#666; margin-top:3px;">
            NIP: <strong>${escapeHtml(u.nip || '-')}</strong> &bull; Unit: ${escapeHtml(u.unit || '-')} &bull; Kategori: ${escapeHtml(u.sub_menu || u.layanan || 'Tendik')}
          </div>
          <div style="font-size:12px; color:#888; margin-top:4px;">
            Diajukan: ${escapeHtml(u.tanggal_diajukan || '-')} &bull; Status: ${badgeHtml}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex; gap:8px; align-items:center;">
          <button type="button" class="${actionBtnClass} kp-small-btn" onclick="openModalEvaluasiTkk('${u.id}')" style="font-weight:600;">
            ${actionBtnText}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

async function openModalEvaluasiTkk(usulanId) {
  activeEvaluasiUsulanId = usulanId;
  const modal = document.getElementById('modalEvaluasiTkk');
  const infoEl = document.getElementById('evalTkkPegawaiInfo');
  const nomorSuratEl = document.getElementById('evalTkk_nomorSurat');
  const tahunEl = document.getElementById('evalTkk_tahunEvaluasi');
  
  if (!modal || !infoEl) return;
  modal.style.display = 'flex';
  infoEl.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center; padding:10px;">Memuat data pegawai...</div>';

  try {
    const res = await rpc('getDetailEvaluasiKontrak', [SESSION.token, usulanId]);
    if (!res || !res.success) {
      showToast('error', res?.message || 'Gagal memuat detail evaluasi.');
      tutupModalEvaluasiTkk();
      return;
    }

    activeEvaluasiDetail = res.data;
    const u = res.data;
    const at = res.atasan_info || {};

    infoEl.innerHTML = `
      <div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(220px, 1fr)); gap:10px; font-size:13px;">
        <div><strong>Nama Pegawai:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.nama || '-')}</div>
        <div><strong>NIP Pegawai:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.nip || '-')}</div>
        <div><strong>Unit Kerja:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.unit || '-')}</div>
        <div><strong>Jabatan:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.jabatan || u.form_data?.jabatan || '-')}</div>
        <div><strong>Pendidikan / Jurusan:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.pendidikan || u.form_data?.pendidikan || '-')}, ${escapeHtml(u.jurusan || u.form_data?.jurusan || '-')}</div>
        <div><strong>Atasan Langsung:</strong> ${escapeHtml(at.nama || SESSION.user?.nama || '-')} (NIP: ${escapeHtml(at.nip || SESSION.user?.nip || '-')})</div>
      </div>
    `;

    if (tahunEl) {
      tahunEl.value = u.tahun || new Date().getFullYear();
    }
    if (nomorSuratEl) {
      nomorSuratEl.value = u.evaluasi_nomor_surat || u.nomor_surat || '';
    }

    // Fill existing criteria scores if available
    const scores = u.evaluasi_kriteria || [2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 2];
    for (let i = 1; i <= 7; i++) {
      const val = scores[i - 1] || 2;
      const r = document.querySelector(`input[name="eval_p${i}"][value="${val}"]`);
      if (r) r.checked = true;
    }

    hitungTotalSkorEvaluasiTkk();
    setTimeout(() => initEvalTkkSignaturePad(), 100);
  } catch (err) {
    showToast('error', 'Terjadi kesalahan: ' + (err.message || err));
    tutupModalEvaluasiTkk();
  }
}

function tutupModalEvaluasiTkk() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('modalEvaluasiTkk');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
  activeEvaluasiUsulanId = null;
  activeEvaluasiDetail = null;
}

function hitungTotalSkorEvaluasiTkk() {
  let total = 0;
  for (let i = 1; i <= 7; i++) {
    const selected = document.querySelector(`input[name="eval_p${i}"]:checked`);
    total += selected ? parseInt(selected.value, 10) : 2;
  }

  const skorDisplay = document.getElementById('evalTkk_totalSkorDisplay');
  const rekDisplay = document.getElementById('evalTkk_rekomendasiDisplay');
  const tahun = parseInt(document.getElementById('evalTkk_tahunEvaluasi')?.value || new Date().getFullYear(), 10);
  const nextTahun = tahun + 1;

  if (skorDisplay) {
    skorDisplay.textContent = `${total} / 21`;
  }

  if (rekDisplay) {
    if (total > 10.5) {
      rekDisplay.textContent = `Diperpanjang Kontrak s.d. 31 Desember ${nextTahun}`;
      rekDisplay.style.color = '#16a34a';
    } else {
      rekDisplay.textContent = 'Tidak Diperpanjang';
      rekDisplay.style.color = '#dc2626';
    }
  }

  return total;
}

function initEvalTkkSignaturePad() {
  evalTkkCanvas = document.getElementById('evalTkkSignaturePad');
  if (!evalTkkCanvas) return;
  evalTkkCtx = evalTkkCanvas.getContext('2d');
  evalTkkCtx.strokeStyle = '#0f172a';
  evalTkkCtx.lineWidth = 2.5;
  evalTkkCtx.lineCap = 'round';
  evalTkkCtx.lineJoin = 'round';

  evalTkkHasDrawn = false;
  clearEvalTkkSignature();

  if (evalTkkCanvas._boundEvents) return;
  evalTkkCanvas._boundEvents = true;

  function getPos(e) {
    const rect = evalTkkCanvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const clientX = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX;
    const clientY = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;
    return {
      x: (clientX - rect.left) * (evalTkkCanvas.width / rect.width),
      y: (clientY - rect.top) * (evalTkkCanvas.height / rect.height)
    };
  }

  function startDraw(e) {
    evalTkkIsDrawing = true;
    evalTkkHasDrawn = true;
    const pos = getPos(e);
    evalTkkCtx.beginPath();
    evalTkkCtx.moveTo(pos.x, pos.y);
    if (e.cancelable && e.type.startsWith('touch')) e.preventDefault();
  }

  function draw(e) {
    if (!evalTkkIsDrawing) return;
    const pos = getPos(e);
    evalTkkCtx.lineTo(pos.x, pos.y);
    evalTkkCtx.stroke();
    if (e.cancelable && e.type.startsWith('touch')) e.preventDefault();
  }

  function stopDraw() {
    evalTkkIsDrawing = false;
  }

  evalTkkCanvas.addEventListener('mousedown', startDraw);
  evalTkkCanvas.addEventListener('mousemove', draw);
  window.addEventListener('mouseup', stopDraw);

  evalTkkCanvas.addEventListener('touchstart', startDraw, { passive: false });
  evalTkkCanvas.addEventListener('touchmove', draw, { passive: false });
  evalTkkCanvas.addEventListener('touchend', stopDraw);
}

function clearEvalTkkSignature() {
  if (!evalTkkCanvas || !evalTkkCtx) return;
  evalTkkCtx.clearRect(0, 0, evalTkkCanvas.width, evalTkkCanvas.height);
  evalTkkHasDrawn = false;
}

function collectEvaluasiTkkPayload() {
  const kriteria = [];
  for (let i = 1; i <= 7; i++) {
    const selected = document.querySelector(`input[name="eval_p${i}"]:checked`);
    kriteria.push(selected ? parseInt(selected.value, 10) : 2);
  }

  const totalSkor = hitungTotalSkorEvaluasiTkk();
  const nomorSurat = (document.getElementById('evalTkk_nomorSurat')?.value || '').trim();
  const tahun = (document.getElementById('evalTkk_tahunEvaluasi')?.value || '').trim();

  let ttdBase64 = '';
  if (evalTkkCanvas && evalTkkHasDrawn) {
    ttdBase64 = evalTkkCanvas.toDataURL('image/png');
  }

  return {
    kriteria,
    total_skor: totalSkor,
    nomor_surat: nomorSurat,
    tahun: tahun,
    ttd_base64: ttdBase64
  };
}

async function previewDocxEvaluasiTkk() {
  if (!activeEvaluasiUsulanId) return;
  const payload = collectEvaluasiTkkPayload();
  showToast('info', 'Membuat preview dokumen formulir evaluasi...');

  try {
    const res = await rpc('simpanDanGenerateEvaluasiTkk', [SESSION.token, activeEvaluasiUsulanId, payload, true]);
    if (res && res.success) {
      showToast('success', 'Preview berhasil dibuat.');
      if (res.base64) {
        downloadBase64File_(res.base64, res.fileName || 'Formulir_Evaluasi_Preview.docx', 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document');
      } else if (res.docUrl) {
        window.open(res.docUrl, '_blank');
      }
    } else {
      showToast('error', res?.message || 'Gagal membuat preview formulir evaluasi.');
    }
  } catch (err) {
    showToast('error', 'Terjadi kesalahan: ' + (err.message || err));
  }
}

async function simpanEvaluasiTkkFinal() {
  if (!activeEvaluasiUsulanId) return;
  const btn = document.getElementById('btnSimpanEvaluasiTkk');
  const payload = collectEvaluasiTkkPayload();

  if (!payload.ttd_base64) {
    showToast('error', 'Silakan bubuhkan tanda tangan Atasan Langsung pada kotak signature pad terlebih dahulu.');
    return;
  }

  setBtnLoading(btn, true, 'Menyimpan & Menggenerate...');

  try {
    const res = await rpc('simpanDanGenerateEvaluasiTkk', [SESSION.token, activeEvaluasiUsulanId, payload, false]);
    setBtnLoading(btn, false, '💾 Simpan & Generate Dokumen (.docx)');
    if (res && res.success) {
      showToast('success', res.message || 'Evaluasi kinerja berhasil disimpan & dokumen tergenerate.');
      tutupModalEvaluasiTkk();
      loadKontrakAtasanReviewList();
    } else {
      showToast('error', res?.message || 'Gagal menyimpan evaluasi.');
    }
  } catch (err) {
    setBtnLoading(btn, false, '💾 Simpan & Generate Dokumen (.docx)');
    showToast('error', 'Terjadi kesalahan: ' + (err.message || err));
  }
}

async function adminValidasiUsulanTendik(usulanId, isExtend) {
  const confirmMsg = isExtend 
    ? 'Apakah Anda yakin ingin memvalidasi usulan ini untuk DIPERPANJANG? Pegawai akan dapat membuat perjanjian kerja.' 
    : 'Apakah Anda yakin ingin menyelesaikan usulan ini sebagai TIDAK DIPERPANJANG?';
  
  if (!confirm(confirmMsg)) return;

  showToast('info', 'Memproses validasi usulan...');
  try {
    const res = await rpc('validasiUsulanKontrakTendik', [SESSION.token, usulanId, isExtend]);
    if (res && res.success) {
      showToast('success', res.message);
      viewKontrakReviewDetail(usulanId);
    } else {
      showToast('error', res?.message || 'Gagal memvalidasi usulan.');
    }
  } catch (err) {
    showToast('error', 'Gagal: ' + (err.message || err));
  }
}

async function generateKontrakUserSelf(usulanId) {
  const selectEl = document.getElementById(`usulan_templateSelect_${usulanId}`);
  const templateRef = selectEl ? selectEl.value : '';
  if (!templateRef) { showToast('error', 'Pilih template terlebih dahulu.'); return; }

  const customFormData = {
    alamat: (document.getElementById(`usulan_alamat_${usulanId}`)?.value || '').trim(),
    nomor_telepon: (document.getElementById(`usulan_nomorTelepon_${usulanId}`)?.value || '').trim(),
    email: (document.getElementById(`usulan_email_${usulanId}`)?.value || '').trim(),
    nomor_surat_perjanjian: (document.getElementById(`usulan_nomorSurat_${usulanId}`)?.value || '').trim()
  };

  const btn = document.getElementById(`usulan_genBtn_${usulanId}`) || document.getElementById('kontrakUser_genBtn');
  if (btn) setBtnLoading(btn, true, 'Memproses...');

  try {
    const res = await rpc('generateKontrakFromUsulanVercel', [SESSION.token, templateRef, usulanId, customFormData]);
    if (btn) setBtnLoading(btn, false, '🚀 Buat Dokumen Perjanjian Kerja Final');
    if (res && res.success) {
      showToast('success', res.message || 'Dokumen kontrak berhasil dibuat.');
      loadUsulanKontrakSaya();
      if (res.outputType === 'pdf') {
        openKontrakResultModal({ outputType: 'pdf', pdfUrl: res.pdfUrl, docViewUrl: res.pdfUrl, fileName: res.fileName });
      } else if (res.outputType === 'docx') {
        const dlUrl = 'data:' + res.mimeType + ';base64,' + res.base64;
        openKontrakResultModal({ isDocx: true, docViewUrl: dlUrl, fileName: res.fileName });
      } else {
        openKontrakResultModal({ docViewUrl: res.viewUrl, pdfDownloadUrl: `https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id=${res.fileId}`, fileName: res.fileName });
      }
      return;
    } else if (res && res.success === false) {
      showToast('error', res.message || 'Gagal membuat dokumen kontrak.');
      return;
    }
  } catch (err) {}

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(res => {
        if (btn) setBtnLoading(btn, false, '🚀 Buat Dokumen Perjanjian Kerja Final');
        if (res.success) {
          showToast('success', res.message || 'Dokumen kontrak berhasil dibuat.');
          loadUsulanKontrakSaya();
          if (res.outputType === 'pdf') {
            openKontrakResultModal({ outputType: 'pdf', pdfUrl: res.pdfUrl, docViewUrl: res.pdfUrl, fileName: res.fileName });
          } else if (res.outputType === 'docx') {
            const dlUrl = 'data:' + res.mimeType + ';base64,' + res.base64;
            openKontrakResultModal({ isDocx: true, docViewUrl: dlUrl, fileName: res.fileName });
          } else {
            openKontrakResultModal({ docViewUrl: res.viewUrl, pdfDownloadUrl: `https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id=${res.fileId}`, fileName: res.fileName });
          }
        } else {
          showToast('error', res.message);
        }
      })
      .withFailureHandler(err => {
        if (btn) setBtnLoading(btn, false, '🚀 Buat Dokumen Perjanjian Kerja Final');
        showToast('error', 'Gagal membuat kontrak: ' + (err.message || err));
      })
      .generateKontrakFromUsulanVercel(SESSION.token, templateRef, usulanId, customFormData);
  }
}

// --- ADMIN REVIEW FUNCTIONS ---

function loadKontrakReviewBadge() {
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      const badge = document.getElementById('kontrakReviewBadge');
      if (badge) {
        if (res.success && res.totalUsulanBaru > 0) {
          badge.textContent = res.totalUsulanBaru;
          badge.style.display = 'inline-block';
        } else {
          badge.style.display = 'none';
        }
      }
    })
    .getUsulanKontrakNotifikasiSummary(SESSION.token);
}

function loadKontrakReviewUnits() {
  const el = document.getElementById('kontrakReviewUnitList');
  el.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Memuat...</div>';
  
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (!res.success) { showToast('error', res.message); return; }
      if (!res.daftarUnit.length) {
        el.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Tidak ada usulan baru masuk.</div>';
        return;
      }
      el.innerHTML = res.daftarUnit.map(item => `
        <div class="ce-dashboard-list-item" onclick="viewKontrakReviewList('${escapeHtml(item.unit)}')">
          <div class="cdli-name">${escapeHtml(item.unit)}</div>
          <div class="cdli-meta">${item.jumlah} usulan baru</div>
        </div>`).join('');
    })
    .getUsulanKontrakNotifikasiSummary(SESSION.token);
}

let kontrakReviewSelectedUnit = '';
async function viewKontrakReviewList(unit) {
  kontrakReviewSelectedUnit = unit;
  document.getElementById('kontrakReviewUnitListState').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('kontrakReviewListState').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('kontrakReviewUnitTitle').textContent = `Daftar Usulan - ${unit}`;
  
  const el = document.getElementById('kontrakReviewDaftarPegawai');
  el.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Memuat...</div>';
  
  try {
    const res = await rpc('getUsulanKontrakListByUnit', [SESSION.token, unit]);
    if (res && res.success) {
      renderKontrakReviewListItems_(res.daftar || []);
      return;
    }
  } catch (err) {}

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(res => {
        if (!res.success) { showToast('error', res.message); return; }
        renderKontrakReviewListItems_(res.daftar || []);
      })
      .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', err.message || err))
      .getUsulanKontrakListByUnit(SESSION.token, unit);
  }
}

function renderKontrakReviewListItems_(daftar) {
  const el = document.getElementById('kontrakReviewDaftarPegawai');
  if (!el) return;
  if (!daftar || !daftar.length) {
    el.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Tidak ada usulan di unit ini.</div>';
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = daftar.map(u => `
    <div class="ce-dashboard-list-item" onclick="viewKontrakReviewDetail('${u.id}')">
      <div>
        <div class="cdli-name">${escapeHtml(u.nama)}</div>
        <div class="cdli-sub" style="font-size:12px; color:#666;">NIP: ${escapeHtml(u.nip)} | Kategori: ${escapeHtml(u.layanan)}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="cdli-meta" style="font-size:12px; text-align:right;">
        <div>${escapeHtml(u.jenis_usulan)} (${escapeHtml(u.tahun)})</div>
        <div style="color:${u.semua_lampiran_disetujui || u.status === 'Disetujui' ? '#4caf50' : '#ff9800'}; font-weight:bold;">
          ${u.status === 'Disetujui' ? '✅ Disetujui (Siap Generate)' : (u.semua_lampiran_disetujui ? 'Lampiran Lengkap' : 'Review Tertunda')}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`).join('');
}

function backToKontrakReviewUnits() {
  document.getElementById('kontrakReviewListState').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('kontrakReviewUnitListState').style.display = 'block';
  loadKontrakReviewUnits();
  loadKontrakReviewBadge();
}

let kontrakReviewActiveUsulanId = '';
async function viewKontrakReviewDetail(usulanId) {
  kontrakReviewActiveUsulanId = usulanId;
  document.getElementById('kontrakReviewListState').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('kontrakReviewDetailState').style.display = 'block';
  
  const el = document.getElementById('kontrakReviewDetailContent');
  el.innerHTML = '<div class="ce-dashboard-card cdc-empty">Memuat detail usulan...</div>';
  
  try {
    const res = await rpc('getUsulanKontrakById', [SESSION.token, usulanId]);
    if (res && res.success) {
      renderKontrakReviewDetailContent_(res.usulan);
      return;
    }
  } catch (err) {}

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(res => {
        if (!res.success) { showToast('error', res.message); return; }
        renderKontrakReviewDetailContent_(res.usulan);
      })
      .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memuat detail usulan: ' + (err.message || err)))
      .getUsulanKontrakById(SESSION.token, usulanId);
  }
}

function renderKontrakReviewDetailContent_(u) {
  const el = document.getElementById('kontrakReviewDetailContent');
  if (!el || !u) return;

  const LAMP_DEFINITIONS = [
    { key: 'ktp', label: 'KTP' },
    { key: 'kk', label: 'Kartu Keluarga' },
    { key: 'pas_foto', label: 'Pas Foto Terbaru' },
    { key: 'ijazah_transkrip', label: 'Ijazah & Transkrip Terakhir' },
    { key: 'surat_pengantar', label: 'Surat Pengantar Unit' },
    { key: 'surat_lamaran', label: 'Surat Lamaran' },
    { key: 'sim_ab', label: 'SIM A/B (Pengemudi)' },
    { key: 'str_aktif', label: 'STR Aktif (Medis)' },
    { key: 'keterangan_sehat', label: 'Surat Keterangan Sehat' }
  ];

  const lampiranList = LAMP_DEFINITIONS.map(item => {
    let url = u[item.key + '_url'] || u.form_data?.[item.key + '_url'] || u.form_data?.[item.key] || u[item.key] || '';
    if (!url && item.key === 'ijazah_transkrip') {
      url = u.ijazah_transkrip_url || u.ijazah_url || u.form_data?.ijazah_transkrip_url || u.form_data?.ijazah_url || u.form_data?.ijazah || u.ijazah || '';
    }
    if (!url && item.key === 'keterangan_sehat') {
      url = u.keterangan_sehat_url || u.ket_sehat_url || u.form_data?.keterangan_sehat_url || u.form_data?.ket_sehat || u.form_data?.keterangan_sehat || '';
    }
    if (!url && item.key === 'pas_foto') {
      url = u.pas_foto_url || u.pasfoto_url || u.form_data?.pas_foto_url || u.form_data?.pasfoto || '';
    }
    url = String(url || '').trim();
    const approved = !!u[item.key + '_approved'];
    return { key: item.key, label: item.label, url, approved };
  });

  const presentList = lampiranList.filter(l => l.url !== '');
  const allApproved = presentList.length > 0 ? presentList.every(l => l.approved) : u.status === 'Disetujui';

  const approveAllBtnHtml = u.status !== 'Disetujui' && !allApproved ? `
    <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" style="background:#4caf50; border-color:#4caf50; font-weight:700; font-size:14px; padding:10px 18px;" onclick="approveAllUsulanKontrakAttachments('${u.id}')">
      ✅ Setujui Semua Lampiran & Usulan
    </button>` : `<span class="badge" style="background:#4caf50; color:#fff; padding:6px 14px; font-size:13px; border-radius:6px; font-weight:bold;">✅ Usulan & Lampiran Disetujui</span>`;

  let savedTmtBulan = u.tmt_bulan || u.form_data?.tmt_bulan || '';
  let savedTmtTahun = u.tmt_tahun || u.form_data?.tmt_tahun || '';
  let savedTstBulan = u.tst_bulan || u.form_data?.tst_bulan || '';
  let savedTstTahun = u.tst_tahun || u.form_data?.tst_tahun || '';

  if (!savedTmtBulan || !savedTmtTahun || !savedTstBulan || !savedTstTahun) {
    const parsed = parsePeriodeKontrakString(u.periode_kontrak || u.form_data?.periode_kontrak || '');
    if (!savedTmtBulan) savedTmtBulan = parsed.tmtBulan;
    if (!savedTmtTahun) savedTmtTahun = parsed.tmtTahun;
    if (!savedTstBulan) savedTstBulan = parsed.tstBulan;
    if (!savedTstTahun) savedTstTahun = parsed.tstTahun;
  }

  const BULAN_NAMES = [
    'Januari', 'Februari', 'Maret', 'April', 'Mei', 'Juni',
    'Juli', 'Agustus', 'September', 'Oktober', 'November', 'Desember'
  ];
  const years = [];
  const currentYr = new Date().getFullYear();
  for (let y = currentYr - 2; y <= currentYr + 8; y++) {
    years.push(y);
  }

  const periodeInputHtml = `
    <div style="margin-top:20px; padding:16px; background:var(--surface-alt); border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:8px;">
      <label style="font-weight:700; font-size:14px; color:var(--text); display:block; margin-bottom:12px;">
        📅 Periode Kontrak (TMT Mulai & TST Selesai):
      </label>
      <div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(240px, 1fr)); gap:16px; margin-bottom:12px;">
        <div>
          <label style="font-size:12px; font-weight:600; color:var(--text-muted); display:block; margin-bottom:4px;">TMT KONTRAK (MULAI)</label>
          <div style="display:flex; gap:8px;">
            <select id="kontrakReview_tmtBulan_${u.id}" class="kontrak-select-dark" style="flex:1;" onchange="updatePeriodePreviewAdmin('${u.id}')">
              <option value="">Bulan...</option>
              ${BULAN_NAMES.map((b, i) => `<option value="${i+1}" ${String(savedTmtBulan) === String(i+1) ? 'selected' : ''}>${b}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
            <select id="kontrakReview_tmtTahun_${u.id}" class="kontrak-select-dark" style="flex:1;" onchange="updatePeriodePreviewAdmin('${u.id}')">
              <option value="">Tahun...</option>
              ${years.map(y => `<option value="${y}" ${String(savedTmtTahun) === String(y) ? 'selected' : ''}>${y}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label style="font-size:12px; font-weight:600; color:var(--text-muted); display:block; margin-bottom:4px;">TST KONTRAK (SELESAI)</label>
          <div style="display:flex; gap:8px;">
            <select id="kontrakReview_tstBulan_${u.id}" class="kontrak-select-dark" style="flex:1;" onchange="updatePeriodePreviewAdmin('${u.id}')">
              <option value="">Bulan...</option>
              ${BULAN_NAMES.map((b, i) => `<option value="${i+1}" ${String(savedTstBulan) === String(i+1) ? 'selected' : ''}>${b}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
            <select id="kontrakReview_tstTahun_${u.id}" class="kontrak-select-dark" style="flex:1;" onchange="updatePeriodePreviewAdmin('${u.id}')">
              <option value="">Tahun...</option>
              ${years.map(y => `<option value="${y}" ${String(savedTstTahun) === String(y) ? 'selected' : ''}>${y}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="kontrakReview_periodePreview_${u.id}" style="font-size:12px; font-weight:600; color:#0284c7; margin-bottom:12px;">
        ${(savedTmtBulan && savedTmtTahun && savedTstBulan && savedTstTahun) ? `Preview Terformat: 1 ${BULAN_NAMES[parseInt(savedTmtBulan, 10)-1]} ${savedTmtTahun} s.d 31 ${BULAN_NAMES[parseInt(savedTstBulan, 10)-1]} ${savedTstTahun}` : 'Pilih bulan & tahun TMT Mulai serta TST Selesai untuk melihat preview.'}
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex; justify-content:flex-end;">
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" style="font-weight:600;" onclick="saveKontrakPeriodeAdmin('${u.id}')">💾 Simpan Periode Kontrak</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

    const isTendik = (u.layanan === 'Kontrak Tendik');
    const tendikEvalCardHtml = isTendik ? `
      <div style="margin-top:20px; padding:18px; background:var(--surface-alt); border:2px solid var(--primary); border-radius:10px;">
        <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:10px; margin-bottom:12px;">
          <h4 style="margin:0; color:var(--primary);">👔 Penilaian Evaluasi Kinerja (Atasan Langsung)</h4>
          <div>
            ${u.status === 'evaluated_extend' ? '<span class="badge" style="background:#8b5cf6; color:#fff; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:6px; font-weight:700;">Sudah Dinilai (Rekomendasi Diperpanjang)</span>' : ''}
            ${u.status === 'evaluated_not_extend' ? '<span class="badge" style="background:#ef4444; color:#fff; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:6px; font-weight:700;">Sudah Dinilai (Tidak Diperpanjang)</span>' : ''}
            ${u.status === 'submitted_to_atasan' || u.status === 'under_atasan_review' ? '<span class="badge" style="background:#f59e0b; color:#fff; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:6px; font-weight:700;">Menunggu Penilaian Atasan</span>' : ''}
            ${u.status === 'validated_by_admin' ? '<span class="badge" style="background:#10b981; color:#fff; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:6px; font-weight:700;">Tervalidasi Admin</span>' : ''}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(220px, 1fr)); gap:12px; font-size:13.5px; margin-bottom:14px;">
          <div><strong>Atasan Langsung:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.atasan_nama || u.atasan_nip || '-')} (NIP: ${escapeHtml(u.atasan_nip || '-')})</div>
          <div><strong>Total Skor (7 Kriteria):</strong> <strong style="color:var(--primary); font-size:16px;">${escapeHtml(u.evaluasi_skor || '-')} / 21</strong></div>
          <div><strong>Rekomendasi:</strong> <strong style="color:${(u.evaluasi_skor || 0) > 10 ? '#16a34a' : '#dc2626'};">${escapeHtml(u.evaluasi_rekomendasi || '-')}</strong></div>
          <div><strong>Nomor Surat Evaluasi:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.evaluasi_nomor_surat || '-')}</div>
        </div>
        ${u.evaluasi_doc_url ? `
          <div style="margin-bottom:14px;">
            <a href="${u.evaluasi_doc_url}" target="_blank" class="btn btn-outline kp-small-btn" style="color:var(--primary); border-color:var(--primary); font-weight:700;">
              📥 Unduh Formulir Evaluasi Kinerja (.docx)
            </a>
          </div>` : ''}
        
        <div style="display:flex; gap:10px; flex-wrap:wrap; border-top:1px solid var(--border); padding-top:12px;">
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" style="background:#10b981; border-color:#10b981; font-weight:700;" onclick="adminValidasiUsulanTendik('${u.id}', true)">
            ✅ Validasi &amp; Setujui Usulan Tendik (Dapat Buat Perjanjian)
          </button>
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline" style="color:#ef4444; border-color:#ef4444; font-weight:600;" onclick="adminValidasiUsulanTendik('${u.id}', false)">
            ❌ Tutup Usulan (Tidak Diperpanjang)
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    ` : '';

  el.innerHTML = `
    <div class="grid-2" style="font-size:14px; gap:10px; margin-bottom:20px; border:1px solid #ddd; padding:16px; border-radius:8px; background:var(--surface);">
      <div><strong>NIP:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.nip || '-')}</div>
      <div><strong>Nama:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.nama || '-')}</div>
      <div><strong>Unit Kerja:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.unit || '-')}</div>
      <div><strong>Email:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.email || '-')}</div>
      <div><strong>Jenis Usulan:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.jenis_usulan || '-')}</div>
      <div><strong>Tahun Kontrak:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.tahun || '-')}</div>
      <div><strong>Evaluasi Kinerja:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.evaluasi_kinerja || '-')}</div>
      <div><strong>Kategori:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.layanan || '-')}</div>
      <div><strong>Jenis Kontrak:</strong> ${escapeHtml(u.sub_menu || '-')}</div>
      <div><strong>Status Usulan:</strong> <strong style="color:${u.status === 'Selesai' ? '#2196f3' : (u.status === 'Disetujui' || u.status === 'validated_by_admin' ? '#4caf50' : (u.status === 'Ditolak' || u.status === 'closed_not_extended' ? '#f44336' : '#ff9800'))}">${escapeHtml(u.status || 'Diajukan')}</strong></div>
    </div>

    ${tendikEvalCardHtml}
    
    <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-top:20px; margin-bottom:12px; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:10px;">
      <h4 style="margin:0;">Persetujuan File Lampiran Pegawai</h4>
      <div>${approveAllBtnHtml}</div>
    </div>

    <div class="ce-dashboard-list" style="margin-top:10px; gap:10px;">
      ${lampiranList.map(l => {
        const linkHtml = l.url 
          ? `<a href="${l.url}" target="_blank" style="text-decoration:none; color:#1957b3; font-weight:600; font-size:14px;">📄 ${l.label} (Buka File)</a>`
          : `<span style="color:#64748b; font-size:14px; font-weight:500;">📄 ${l.label}</span>`;
        
        const previewBtnHtml = l.url
          ? `<button type="button" class="btn btn-outline" style="padding:3px 10px; font-size:12px;" onclick="openPreviewModal('${escapeHtml(l.url)}')">👁️ Preview</button>`
          : `<span style="font-size:12px; color:#94a3b8; font-style:italic;">(Tidak diunggah)</span>`;

        return `
          <div class="ce-dashboard-list-item" style="cursor:default; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; padding:12px 16px; background:var(--surface); border:1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius:8px;">
            <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px; flex-wrap:wrap;">
              ${linkHtml}
              ${previewBtnHtml}
            </div>
            <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px;">
              <input type="checkbox" onchange="toggleAttachmentApproval('${u.id}', '${l.key}', this.checked)" ${l.approved ? 'checked' : ''} style="width:20px; height:20px; cursor:pointer;" />
              <span style="font-size:14px; font-weight:600; color:${l.approved ? '#4caf50' : '#64748b'};">${l.approved ? '✅ Disetujui' : 'Setujui'}</span>
            </div>
          </div>`;
      }).join('')}
    </div>

    ${periodeInputHtml}

    <div style="display:flex; gap:12px; justify-content:flex-end; margin-top:20px; border-top:1px solid #eee; padding-top:16px; flex-wrap:wrap;">
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline" style="color:var(--danger); border-color:var(--danger); font-weight:600;" onclick="adminRejectUsulanKontrak('${u.id}')">
        🚫 Tolak Usulan
      </button>
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline" style="color:#b91c1c; border-color:#b91c1c; background:#fee2e2; font-weight:700;" onclick="adminDeleteUsulanKontrak('${u.id}')">
        🗑️ Hapus Usulan
      </button>
    </div>
  `;
}

async function approveAllUsulanKontrakAttachments(usulanId) {
  showToast('info', 'Menyetujui semua lampiran...');
  try {
    const res = await rpc('approveAllUsulanKontrakAttachments', [SESSION.token, usulanId]);
    if (res && res.success) {
      showToast('success', res.message || 'Semua lampiran dan usulan disetujui.');
      viewKontrakReviewDetail(usulanId);
    } else {
      showToast('error', res?.message || 'Gagal menyetujui usulan.');
    }
  } catch (err) {
    if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(res => {
          if (res.success) {
            showToast('success', res.message);
            viewKontrakReviewDetail(usulanId);
          } else {
            showToast('error', res.message);
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => showToast('error', 'Gagal menyetujui: ' + (e.message || e)))
        .approveAllUsulanKontrakAttachments(SESSION.token, usulanId);
    } else {
      showToast('error', 'Gagal menyetujui usulan: ' + (err.message || err));
    }
  }
}

async function adminRejectUsulanKontrak(usulanId) {
  const alasan = prompt('Masukkan alasan penolakan usulan (opsional):');
  if (alasan === null) return;

  showToast('info', 'Memproses penolakan...');
  try {
    const res = await rpc('rejectUsulanKontrak', [SESSION.token, usulanId, alasan]);
    if (res && res.success) {
      showToast('success', res.message || 'Usulan berhasil ditolak.');
      viewKontrakReviewDetail(usulanId);
    } else {
      showToast('error', res?.message || 'Gagal menolak usulan.');
    }
  } catch (err) {
    if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(res => {
          if (res.success) {
            showToast('success', res.message);
            viewKontrakReviewDetail(usulanId);
          } else {
            showToast('error', res.message);
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => showToast('error', 'Gagal menolak usulan: ' + (e.message || e)))
        .updateStatusUsulanKontrak(SESSION.token, usulanId, 'Ditolak', alasan);
    } else {
      showToast('error', 'Gagal menolak usulan: ' + (err.message || err));
    }
  }
}

async function adminDeleteUsulanKontrak(usulanId) {
  if (!confirm('Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus usulan kontrak ini secara permanen?')) return;

  showToast('info', 'Menghapus usulan...');
  try {
    const res = await rpc('deleteUsulanKontrakSingle', [SESSION.token, usulanId]);
    if (res && res.success) {
      showToast('success', res.message || 'Usulan berhasil dihapus.');
      backToKontrakReviewList();
    } else {
      showToast('error', res?.message || 'Gagal menghapus usulan.');
    }
  } catch (err) {
    if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(res => {
          if (res.success) {
            showToast('success', res.message);
            backToKontrakReviewList();
          } else {
            showToast('error', res.message);
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(e => showToast('error', 'Gagal menghapus usulan: ' + (e.message || e)))
        .deleteUsulanKontrakSingle(SESSION.token, usulanId);
    } else {
      showToast('error', 'Gagal menghapus usulan: ' + (err.message || err));
    }
  }
}

function backToKontrakReviewList() {
  document.getElementById('kontrakReviewDetailState').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('kontrakReviewListState').style.display = 'block';
  viewKontrakReviewList(kontrakReviewSelectedUnit);
}

function toggleAttachmentApproval(usulanId, lampKey, disetujui) {
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (res.success) {
        showToast('success', res.message);
        viewKontrakReviewDetail(usulanId);
      } else {
        showToast('error', res.message);
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => showToast('error', 'Gagal memproses persetujuan: ' + (err.message || err)))
    .approveUsulanKontrakAttachment(SESSION.token, usulanId, lampKey, disetujui);
}

function generateKontrakFromUsulanAdmin(usulanId) {
  const selectEl = document.getElementById(`usulan_templateSelect_${usulanId}`);
  const templateRef = selectEl ? selectEl.value : '';
  if (!templateRef) { showToast('error', 'Pilih template terlebih dahulu.'); return; }

  const btn = document.getElementById('kontrakReview_genBtn');
  setBtnLoading(btn, true, 'Generating...');
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Generate Dokumen Kontrak Perjanjian Kerja');
      if (res.success) {
        showToast('success', res.message);
        viewKontrakReviewDetail(usulanId);
        if (res.outputType === 'pdf') {
          openKontrakResultModal({ outputType: 'pdf', pdfUrl: res.pdfUrl, docViewUrl: res.pdfUrl });
        } else if (res.outputType === 'docx') {
          const dlUrl = 'data:' + res.mimeType + ';base64,' + res.base64;
          openKontrakResultModal({ isDocx: true, docViewUrl: dlUrl, fileName: res.fileName });
        } else {
          openKontrakResultModal({ docViewUrl: res.viewUrl, pdfDownloadUrl: `https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id=${res.fileId}` });
        }
      } else {
        showToast('error', res.message);
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(err => {
      setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Generate Dokumen Kontrak Perjanjian Kerja');
      showToast('error', 'Gagal generate kontrak: ' + (err.message || err));
    })
    .generateKontrakFromUsulanVercel(SESSION.token, templateRef, usulanId);
}

/* =====================================================================
 * ALUR SK KENAIKAN PANGKAT (NON-ASN)
 * ===================================================================== */

// --- BADGE PERSURATAN / APPROVAL SK ---
function loadApprovalSkBadge() {
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(res => {
      if (!res.success) return;
      const badge = document.getElementById('approvalSkBadge');
      if (badge) {
        if (res.daftar && res.daftar.length > 0) {
          badge.textContent = res.daftar.length;
          badge.style.display = 'inline-block';
        } else {
          badge.style.display = 'none';
        }
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(() => {})
    .getSkApprovalQueue(SESSION.token);
}
